> \[Str1953] † \[יְהוֺשָׁמָע] only abbrev. (or txt. err.) הוֺשָׁמָע **n.pr.m.** (´י *hath heard,* cf. אֱלִישָׁמָע \& reff.)—a descendant of the royal house of Judah [1 Ch 3:18](https://ref.ly/1Chr3:18) (LXX Ὡσαμω(θ), LXXL Ωσαμ*ȧ*).

> \[Str3092,3146] יְהוֺשָׁפָט, יוֺשָׁפָט **n.pr.m.** LXX Ἰωσαφαθ, Ἰωσαφατ (´י *hath judged,* cf. אֱלִישָׁפָט, שְׁפַטְיָהוּ\& reff.)—**1\.** ´יְהוֺ (so all exc.**5\.** \& **6**) king of Judah, son of Asa [1 K 15:24](https://ref.ly/1Kgs15:24), [22:2](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:2) \+ 21 t. 1 K; [2 K 1:17](https://ref.ly/2Kgs1:17), [3:1](https://ref.ly/2Kgs3:1), [3:7](https://ref.ly/2Kgs3:7), [3:11](https://ref.ly/2Kgs3:11), [3:12](https://ref.ly/2Kgs3:12), [3:12](https://ref.ly/2Kgs3:12), [3:14](https://ref.ly/2Kgs3:14), [8:16](https://ref.ly/2Kgs8:16), [8:16](https://ref.ly/2Kgs8:16), [12:19](https://ref.ly/2Kgs12:19), [1 Ch 3:10](https://ref.ly/1Chr3:10), [2 Ch 17:1](https://ref.ly/2Chr17:1) \+ 40 t. 2 Ch . † **2\.** father of Jehu king of Israel [2 K 9:2](https://ref.ly/2Kgs9:2), [9:14](https://ref.ly/2Kgs9:14). † **3\.** chronicler under David \& Solomon, son of Ahilud [2 S 8:16](https://ref.ly/2Sam8:16), [1 K 4:3](https://ref.ly/1Kgs4:3), [1 Ch 18:15](https://ref.ly/1Chr18:15); also [2 S 20:24](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:24) (LXXL Σαφαν). † **4\.** one of Sol.'s 12 officers who provided victuals for the royal household [1 K 4:17](https://ref.ly/1Kgs4:17) (LXXL Ἰωασαφατ). † **5\.** ´יוֺ one of David's heroes [1 Ch 11:43](https://ref.ly/1Chr11:43). † **6\.** ´יוֺ a priest \& trumpeter in David's time [1 Ch 15:24](https://ref.ly/1Chr15:24). † **7\.** in **n.pr.loc.** עֵמֶק יְהוֺשָׁפָט [Jo 4:2](https://ref.ly/Joel3:2), [4:12](https://ref.ly/Joel3:12), symbolical name of a valley near Jerusalem, place of ultimate judgement.

## הוֺי (id: BDB02365)

\[Str1945] הוֺי:51 **interj.** (onomatop.; cf. ܗܳܘܳܝ \= הוֺ הוֺ [Am 5:16](https://ref.ly/Amos5:16) Syrver, \= הֵידָד [Je 51:14](https://ref.ly/Jer51:14) SyrVer ; also Mod. Aram *ú hú, ú hú* (in a lament), SoUrmia\-Dial. 102\. 4\)—expressing usually dissatisfaction and pain, Ah, Alas; Ha (not distinctly *Woe!* which is אוֺי): used in lamentations, [1 K 13:30](https://ref.ly/1Kgs13:30) and they mourned over him (saying) הוֺי אָחִי *Ah*, my brother! Je [22:18](https://ref.ly/Jer22:18) הוֺי אָדוֺן…הוֺי אָחִי וְהוֺי אָחוֺת וְהוֺי הֹדֹה, [34:5](https://ref.ly/Jer34:5) וְהוֺי אָדוֺן יִסְפְּדוּ לָח (cf. הוֺ־הוֺ [Am 5:16](https://ref.ly/Amos5:16)): hence [Is 1:4](https://ref.ly/Isa1:4) הוֺי גּוֺי חֹמֵא  *Ah!* sinful nation v:[24](https://ref.ly/Isa1:24) *Ah!* I will ease me of my adversaries; esp. preparatory to a declaration of judgment, [Is 10:5](https://ref.ly/Isa10:5) הוֺי אַשּׁוּר שֵׁבֶט אַפִּי, [17:12](https://ref.ly/Isa17:12), [28:1](https://ref.ly/Isa28:1), oft. sq. a ptcp. *Ah!* those that…, [5:8](https://ref.ly/Isa5:8), [5:11](https://ref.ly/Isa5:11), [5:18](https://ref.ly/Isa5:18), [29:15](https://ref.ly/Isa29:15), [31:1](https://ref.ly/Isa31:1), [45:9](https://ref.ly/Isa45:9), [45:10](https://ref.ly/Isa45:10), [Je 22:13](https://ref.ly/Jer22:13), [Am 5:18](https://ref.ly/Amos5:18), [6:1](https://ref.ly/Amos6:1), Hb [2:6](https://ref.ly/Hab2:6), [2:9](https://ref.ly/Hab2:9), [2:12](https://ref.ly/Hab2:12), [2:15](https://ref.ly/Hab2:15), [2:19](https://ref.ly/Hab2:19), [Zc 11:17](https://ref.ly/Zech11:17) \+ (more sympathetic than אוֺי לְ): sq. כִּי [Je 30:7](https://ref.ly/Jer30:7), אֶל [48:1](https://ref.ly/Jer48:1), עַל [50:27](https://ref.ly/Jer50:27), Ez [13:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:3), לְ [13:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:18). Sts. hardly more than an exclam. arousing attention *Ha!* (though perh. with a touch of sympathy or pity) [Is 18:1](https://ref.ly/Isa18:1), [55:1](https://ref.ly/Isa55:1), [Zc 2:10](https://ref.ly/Zech2:10), [2:10](https://ref.ly/Zech2:10), [2:11](https://ref.ly/Zech2:11), [Je 47:6](https://ref.ly/Jer47:6).

## הוּם (id: BDB02366)

\[Str1949] † \[הוּם, הִים] **vb.** murmur, roar, discomfit (Ar. هَامَ (med. ي) is *rush about madly* (lit., or in passion, love, \&c.), fig. *be distracted;* on √ הִים v. Hi:[Psalm 55:3](https://ref.ly/Ps55:3) NöZMG 1883, 540\)—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 ms. sf. וְהָמָם consec. [Dt 7:23](https://ref.ly/Deut7:23) מְהוּמָה גְדֹלָה ´וְה (´י subj.) *and he shall discomfit them (with) a great discomfiture* (acc. to Di ´וה is fr. הָמַם, here pointed as ו“ע because of מְהוּמָה; but v. Koi. 486 \& reff.); here, acc. to No1.c., belongs *Impf.*1 s. cohort. אָהִ֫ימָה (i. e. √ י“ע) [Psalm 55:3](https://ref.ly/Ps55:3) *am driven about* (Hi De), or *distracted.* **Niph.** *Impf.* 3 fs. וַתֵּהֹם [1 S 4:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam4:5) \+ 2 t. (on form cf. Ges§ 72 R 5\); *be in a stir,* of a city [Ru 1:19](https://ref.ly/Ruth1:19) (sq. על *over, on account of*, [1 K 1:45](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:45); of ground *re\-echoing* shout 1 S [4:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam4:5). **Hiph.** *Impf.* 1 s. אָהִָימָה [Psalm 55:3](https://ref.ly/Ps55:3) *shew disquietude* (so most, cf. supr.); but Ol Che וְאֶהֱמָוָה ( [77:4](https://ref.ly/Ps77:4)); Lag: Proph. Chald. xlviii. 2, BN 27 אֶהֱמֶה (v:[18](https://ref.ly/Ps55:18)); 3 fpl. תְּהִימֶנָה (?תֶּהֱמֶינָה) [Mi 2:12](https://ref.ly/Mic2:12) *they* (i.e. fold \& pasture) *shall murmur* with men.

> \[Str4103] † מְהוּמָה **n.f.** tumult, confusion, disquietde, discomfiture—abs. ´מ [Dt 7:23](https://ref.ly/Deut7:23) \+ 7 t. ; cstr. מְהוּמַת [1 S 5:11](https://ref.ly/1Sam5:11), Ez [22:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:5); pl. abs. מְהוּמוֺת [2 Ch 15:5](https://ref.ly/2Chr15:5), מְהוּמֹת [Am 3:9](https://ref.ly/Amos3:9); —**1\.** *tumult, confusion* [Am 3:9](https://ref.ly/Amos3:9) (‖עֲשׁוּקִים *oppression*), *disturbance, turmoil* (opp. peace) [2 Ch 15:5](https://ref.ly/2Chr15:5) (‖ vb. המם v:[6](https://ref.ly/2Chr15:6)), cf. Ez [22:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:5); *disquietude* (of mind) [Pr 15:16](https://ref.ly/Prov15:16); esp. *tumult \& confusion, panic,* as due to divine judgment ´מְהוּמַת־י [Zc 14:13](https://ref.ly/Zech14:13) cf. Ez [7:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek7:7); also [1 S 5:9](https://ref.ly/1Sam5:9) and מְהוּמַת מָוֶת v:[11](https://ref.ly/1Sam5:11). **2\.** *discomfiture,* (due to ´י) in war [1 S 14:20](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:20) (cf. v:[15](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:15), v:[22](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:22)); [Dt 7:23](https://ref.ly/Deut7:23) (acc. cogn. c. הום q.v.), [28:20](https://ref.ly/Deut28:20) (‖מְאֵרָה, מִגְעֶרֶת); cf. ´יוֺם מ [1 S 22:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam22:5) (‖מְבוּסָה, מְבוּכָה) *a day of discomfiture \& down\-treading \& confusion*.

> \[Str1950] הוֺמָם **n.pr.** v. sub המם. p. 243

## הוּן (id: BDB02369)

\[Str1951] † \[הוּן] **vb.** be easy (Ar. هَانَ *be light, easy,* cf. هَوْنٌ, هَيْنٌ (v. אוּן), Aram. NH הוֺן *gentleness,* ܗܽܘܢ, ܗܰܘܢܳܐ *is mind, sense;* vid. conject. on development of mngs. Fl Ch WB: i. 423\)—only **Hiph.** *Impf* וַתָּהִינוּ sq. Inf. [Dt 1:41](https://ref.ly/Deut1:41) *regard as easy* to go up, or *make light* of going up; so Thes Kn Ke Di RVm (construct. as [Nu 14:44](https://ref.ly/Num14:44) וַיַּעְפִּלוּ לַעֲלוֺת, though ‖ to this in Dt is v:[43](https://ref.ly/Deut1:43)).

[2 K 12:7](https://ref.ly/2Kgs12:7), [17:25](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:25) (both c. בְּ temp.); \& without neg., [Gn 8:13](https://ref.ly/Gen8:13) (P) [14:1](https://ref.ly/Gen14:1) \+, usu. c. בְּ temp. (38 t.), Hex chiefly P; rarely c. other prep. \& adv. as above; note esp. מִדֵּי sq. Inf. [1 S 18:30](https://ref.ly/1Sam18:30), [2 Ch 12:11](https://ref.ly/2Chr12:11); sts. the subj. of foll. Pf. precedes it c. וְ, as [Gn 22:1](https://ref.ly/Gen22:1), [41:1](https://ref.ly/Gen41:1), [1 S 18:19](https://ref.ly/1Sam18:19), [30:1](https://ref.ly/1Sam30:1), [2 K 2:9](https://ref.ly/2Kgs2:9), [4:40](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:40) \+; note esp. [2 S 17:27](https://ref.ly/2Sam17:27) where several subj. \& also several objects precede the Pf. (cf. Dr); in these cases temporal modifier is occasionally a circumst. cl. [2 S 13:30](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:30), [1 K 8:10](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:10), [2 K 19:37](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:37) \= [Is 37:38](https://ref.ly/Isa37:38); rarely subj. precedes Pf. without וְ [1 K 11:4](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:4), [21:1](https://ref.ly/1Kgs21:1), comp. subj. preceding Pt. without וְ [Ne 4:10](https://ref.ly/Neh4:10); in [2 Ch 8:1](https://ref.ly/2Chr8:1) the obj. (with וְ) precedes what is appar. the principal vb. [2 K 2:11](https://ref.ly/2Kgs2:11) (*id.*), [3:20](https://ref.ly/2Kgs3:20), [13:21](https://ref.ly/2Kgs13:21) (circumst. cl.). (*d*) rarely וַיְהִי (in this sense) sq. other constructions: אָז יִתֵּן [1 K 9:10](https://ref.ly/1Kgs9:10) (c. מִקְצֵה); Impf. frequentat. (*a*) γ, (*b*) δ, supr.). (*g*) וְהָיָה (ו conj.) sq. Impf. consec. v. **a.** (*e*) supr. **II.** *Come into being, become:*—**1\. a.** abs., in lively narrative, *arise, appear, come* וַתְּהִי צְעָקָה גְדֹלָה[Ex 12:80](https://ref.ly/Exod12:80) (J) *and there arose a great cry* in Egypt; וַיְהִי־קוֺל Ez [1:25](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:25) (del. Co cf. Da), [37:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek37:7) (del. קוֺל LXX Co); כִּי הָֽיְתָה הָֽרְוָחָה [Ex 8:11](https://ref.ly/Exod8:11) (J) Pharaoh saw *that respite had come;* [Mi 7:4](https://ref.ly/Mic7:4) *now shall come their confusion* (‖בוא); also of concrete objects וְהָֽיְתָה הַקֶּשֶׁת בֶּעָנָן [Gn 9:16](https://ref.ly/Gen9:16) (P) *and the bow shall appear in the clouds* (‖וְנִרְאֲתָה) וְרִמָּה לאֹ הָֽיְתָה בּוֺ [Ex 16:24](https://ref.ly/Exod16:24) (P) *and worms did not appear in it;* of condition or action begun \& continuing, as: famine [Gn 12:10](https://ref.ly/Gen12:10), [26:1](https://ref.ly/Gen26:1), [26:1](https://ref.ly/Gen26:1) (all J), [41:54](https://ref.ly/Gen41:54) (E), [Ru 1:1](https://ref.ly/Ruth1:1), [2 S 21:1](https://ref.ly/2Sam21:1), [2 K 6:25](https://ref.ly/2Kgs6:25); strife [Gn 13:7](https://ref.ly/Gen13:7), [13:8](https://ref.ly/Gen13:8) (J), Hb [1:3](https://ref.ly/Hab1:3) (‖מָדוֺן יִשָּא); oft. of action of the elements, as the coming of hail [Ex 9:18](https://ref.ly/Exod9:18), [9:24](https://ref.ly/Exod9:24), [9:26](https://ref.ly/Exod9:26) (all J), thunder \& lighting, etc., [Ex 19:16](https://ref.ly/Exod19:16) (E), rain [1 K 18:45](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:45), storm [Jon 1:4](https://ref.ly/Jonah1:4); esp. of creative fiats יְהִי אוֺר וַיְהִי אוֺר [Gn 1:3](https://ref.ly/Gen1:3) *let light appear, and light appeared,* cf. v:[5](https://ref.ly/Gen1:5), v:[8](https://ref.ly/Gen1:8), v:[13](https://ref.ly/Gen1:13), v:[19](https://ref.ly/Gen1:19), v:[23](https://ref.ly/Gen1:23), v:[31](https://ref.ly/Gen1:31) (all P); also *arise, come on the stage* or *scene,* וְאַחֲרָיו הָיָה שַׁמְגַּר Ju [3:31](https://ref.ly/Judg3:31) *and after him arose Shamgar* (\= וַיָּקָם [10:1](https://ref.ly/Judg10:1)); further וַעֲלָטָה הָיָה [Gn 15:17](https://ref.ly/Gen15:17) (JE) *and darkness came on;* הַבֹּקֶר הָיָה [Ex 10:13](https://ref.ly/Exod10:13) (J) *the morning came,* [19:16](https://ref.ly/Exod19:16) (E); וַיְהִי הַיּוֺם † [1 S 1:4](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:4) *and the day came,* when (v. Dr), [14:1](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:1), [2 K 4:8](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:8), [4:11](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:11), [4:18](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:18), [Jb 1:6](https://ref.ly/Job1:6), [1:18](https://ref.ly/Job1:18), [2:1](https://ref.ly/Job2:1). **b.** sq. prep.: בְּ, וְיָדֵנוּ אַל־תְּהִי־בוֺ [Gn 37:27](https://ref.ly/Gen37:27) (J) *but our hand, let it not come upon him,* of hand of ´יּ [1 S 5:9](https://ref.ly/1Sam5:9), [7:13](https://ref.ly/1Sam7:13), [12:15](https://ref.ly/1Sam12:15) \+ (all in hostile sense); בְּכָל־שֶׁ־ֲר יֶשׁ־לוֺ ´וַיְהִי בִּרְכַּת י [Gn 39:5](https://ref.ly/Gen39:5) (J) *and the blessing of* ´י *came into* (on) *all that he had;* but also of plague [Ex 12:13](https://ref.ly/Exod12:13) (P); sq. עַל, וּמֵי הַמַּבּוּל הָיוּ עַל־הָאָרֶץ [Gn 7:10](https://ref.ly/Gen7:10) (J) *that the waters of the flood came upon the earth;* עָלַי הָיוּ כֻלָּנָה [Gn 42:36](https://ref.ly/Gen42:36) (E) [Gn 9:2](https://ref.ly/Gen9:2) (P) *fear shall come upon,* cf. [35:25](https://ref.ly/Gen35:25) (P) [2 Ch 14:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr14:13), [17:19](https://ref.ly/2Chr17:19), [20:29](https://ref.ly/2Chr20:29) \+; of wrath [Jos 22:20](https://ref.ly/Josh22:20), [1 K 3:27](https://ref.ly/1Kgs3:27); so of Saul's evil spirit fr. God [1 S 16:16](https://ref.ly/1Sam16:16), [16:23](https://ref.ly/1Sam16:23), [19:9](https://ref.ly/1Sam19:9) (אֶל); but sq. עַל also in good sense ´וַתְּהִי עָלָיו רוּחַ י conferring strength \& efficiency Ju [3:10](https://ref.ly/Judg3:10), [11:29](https://ref.ly/Judg11:29) (היה \= צלח Ju [14:6](https://ref.ly/Judg14:6) \+ oft.), cf. [Nu 24:2](https://ref.ly/Num24:2) (JE) of prophetic power; (on ´וַתְּהִי לָיו יַד י [2 K 3:15](https://ref.ly/2Kgs3:15), Ez [3:22](https://ref.ly/Ezek3:22) and the like, vid. יָד); יַד אֱלֽהֵינוּ הָֽיְתָה עָלֵינוּ [Ezr 8:31](https://ref.ly/Ezra8:31), so [Psalm 80:18](https://ref.ly/Ps80:18); even וַתְּהִי עַל־ראֹשׁ דָּוִד [2 S 12:30](https://ref.ly/2Sam12:30) *and it* (the crown, עֲטֶרֶת) *came* (i.e. was put, set) *on David's head;* with a diff. mng. וַנִּהְיֶה עֲלֵיהֶם עַד־פֶּתַח הַשָּׁעַר [2 S 11:23](https://ref.ly/2Sam11:23) *and we were* close *upon them even to the gateway* (cf. We Dr); *come, go, follow* (sq. אַחֲרַי) i.e. take the side of, adhere to, [Ex 23:2](https://ref.ly/Exod23:2) (JE) [1 S 12:14](https://ref.ly/1Sam12:14), [2 S 2:10](https://ref.ly/2Sam2:10), [1 K 12:20](https://ref.ly/1Kgs12:20), [16:21](https://ref.ly/1Kgs16:21); sq. לְ of blessings [Gn 49:26](https://ref.ly/Gen49:26) (poem); of judgement, punishment [Je 40:3](https://ref.ly/Jer40:3) esp. אֶל…ויהי דבר *and the word of … came unto:* word of Samuel [1 S 4:1](https://ref.ly/1Sam4:1); usually word of God (or ´י) [Gn 15:1](https://ref.ly/Gen15:1), [1 S 15:10](https://ref.ly/1Sam15:10) \+ ; constantly in prophets: [Ho 1:1](https://ref.ly/Hos1:1), [Mi 1:1](https://ref.ly/Mic1:1) etc.; esp. Je \& Ez: [Je 1:4](https://ref.ly/Jer1:4), [1:11](https://ref.ly/Jer1:11), [1:13](https://ref.ly/Jer1:13), [2:1](https://ref.ly/Jer2:1), [13:8](https://ref.ly/Jer13:8) \+; Ez [3:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek3:16), [6:1](https://ref.ly/Ezek6:1), [7:1](https://ref.ly/Ezek7:1), [11:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek11:14), [11:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek11:17), [11:21](https://ref.ly/Ezek11:21), [11:26](https://ref.ly/Ezek11:26), [12:1](https://ref.ly/Ezek12:1), [12:8](https://ref.ly/Ezek12:8) \+; so also abs. הָיָה הַמַּשָּא הַזֶּה [Is 14:28](https://ref.ly/Isa14:28) in the death\-year of king Ahaz *came this utterance;* also sq. מִן; מַלְכֵי עַמִּין מִמֶּנָּה יִהְיוּ [Gn 17:16](https://ref.ly/Gen17:16) (P), וּקְהַל גּוֺיִם יִהְיֶה מִמֶָּךָּ [35:11](https://ref.ly/Gen35:11) (P), וְהָיָה אַדִּירוֺ מִמֶּנּוּ [Je 30:21](https://ref.ly/Jer30:21) (‖מִקִּרְבּוֺ יֵצֵא); הַכֹּל הָיָה מִן־הֶעָפָר וְהַכֹּל שָׁב אֶל הֶעָפָר [Ec 3:20](https://ref.ly/Eccl3:20) *all came from the dust, \& all return unto the dust;* וַיְהִי קֶצֶף גָּדוֺל מֵאֵת צְבָאוֺת ´י [Zc 7:12](https://ref.ly/Zech7:12). **2\.** *become :* **a.** sq. pred. noun (to be viewed as implicit accus. Dr§ 161, 3 n., according to the Arab. usage WAG ii, § 41\. 44\. 74\): הִוא הָֽיְתָה אֵם כָּל־חָי [Gn 3:20](https://ref.ly/Gen3:20) (J) *it was she that became the mother of all living;* וַיְהִי הֶבֶל רֹעֵה צאֹן וְקַיִן הָיָה עֹבֵד אֲדָמָה[4:2](https://ref.ly/Gen4:2) (J) *and Abel became a shepherd of flocks, while Cain became a tiller of ground;* [2 S 8:14](https://ref.ly/2Sam8:14), [Mi 2:11](https://ref.ly/Mic2:11) \+ oft. **b.** sq. pred. adj. [Gn 38:7](https://ref.ly/Gen38:7), [Ex 36:13](https://ref.ly/Exod36:13) \+; cf. [Gn 37:20](https://ref.ly/Gen37:20) *let us see what his dream will become* (turn out to be, signify); **c.** *become like* (כְּ), וִהְיִיתֶם כֵּאלֽהִים [Gn 3:5](https://ref.ly/Gen3:5) *and ye shall become like gods* cf. v:[22](https://ref.ly/Gen3:22), [Nu 11:1](https://ref.ly/Num11:1) (JE), [Ho 2:1](https://ref.ly/Hos2:1), [7:11](https://ref.ly/Hos7:11), [Is 9:18](https://ref.ly/Isa9:18), [1 K 7:8](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:8), [Zc 9:7](https://ref.ly/Zech9:7) \+; cf. [Gn 15:5](https://ref.ly/Gen15:5) *so (כֹּה) shall thy seed become;* specif. of likeness in punishment [Is 1:9](https://ref.ly/Isa1:9), [1 S 17:36](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:36) \+; also with כְּ repeated וְהָיָה כָעָם כַּכֹּהֵן [Ho 4:9](https://ref.ly/Hos4:9) *and the people shall become like the priest* (lit., and the like of the people shall become the like of the priest), so esp. [Is 24:2](https://ref.ly/Isa24:2) (6 pairs of words as above); וְהָיָה כַצַּדִּיק כָּרָשָׁע [Gn 18:25](https://ref.ly/Gen18:25) (J), v. כְּ. **b** \+ **c** [Gn 27:32](https://ref.ly/Gen27:32) *his hands had become like the hands of Esau his brother, hairy.* **d.** sq. pred. \+ לְ Pers. וּתְהִי אִשָּׁה לְבֶן־אֲדֹנֶיךָ [Gn 24:51](https://ref.ly/Gen24:51) (J) *and let her become wife to the son of thy lord,* [1 S 16:21](https://ref.ly/1Sam16:21), [1 K 11:25](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:25), [Psalm 89:42](https://ref.ly/Ps89:42), [1 Ch 18:6](https://ref.ly/1Chr18:6) \+; \= *be instituted, estanblished,* וְהָיָה לָהֶסחָק־עוֺלָם [Ex 30:21](https://ref.ly/Exod30:21) (P) *and it shall be established for them as a perpetual ordinance* (cf. **f** infr.). **e.** sq. לְ pred.: וַיְהִי הָאָדָם לְנֶפֶשׁ חַיָּה [Gn 2:7](https://ref.ly/Gen2:7) (J) *and the man became a living soul;* וְהָיָה לְאַרְבָּעָה רָאשִׁים[Gn 2:10](https://ref.ly/Gen2:10) (J) *and it became four heads;* יִהְיֶה־לְעָם [Gn 48:19](https://ref.ly/Gen48:19) (J) *he too shall become a people;* וִהְיוּ לַאֲנָשִׁים [1 S 4:9](https://ref.ly/1Sam4:9) *and become* (shew yourselves to be) *men* (‖הִתְחַזְּקוּ; cf. with double לְ, **f** infr.); [Zp 1:13](https://ref.ly/Zeph1:13), Ez [17:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek17:6), [47:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:12), [Jb 16:8](https://ref.ly/Job16:8); עלמֶלֶךְ עַל־כָּל־הָאָרֶץ ´וְהָיָה י [Zc 14:9](https://ref.ly/Zech14:9); וַיְהִי לְמַס עֹבֵד † [Gn 49:15](https://ref.ly/Gen49:15), [Jos 16:10](https://ref.ly/Josh16:10); so oft.; also in sense of *turn into,* be changed so as to become, וַיְהִי לְנָחָשׁ[Ex 4:3](https://ref.ly/Exod4:3) *and it became a serpent,* v:[4](https://ref.ly/Exod4:4), [7:10](https://ref.ly/Exod7:10), [Is 1:22](https://ref.ly/Isa1:22), [Dt 26:5](https://ref.ly/Deut26:5) \+; fig. וַיְהִי לְמָיִם [Jos 7:5](https://ref.ly/Josh7:5) *and it* (their heart) *turned to water,* so וְהוּא הָיָה לְאֶבֶן [1 S 25:37](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:37); or, *serve as* וְהָיוּ לִמְאוֺרֹת [Gn 1:15](https://ref.ly/Gen1:15) (P) *and they shall become* (fulfil the function of, serve as) *luminaries,* cf. v:[14](https://ref.ly/Gen1:14); וְהָיָה לְאוֺת בְּרִית [Gn 9:13](https://ref.ly/Gen9:13), [17:11](https://ref.ly/Gen17:11) (both P). **f.** oft. c. לְ pred. \+ לְ Pers.: וַתְּהִי לָהֶם הַלְּבֵנָה לְאָבֶן[Gn 11:3](https://ref.ly/Gen11:3) (J) *and the bricks became* (served as) *stone for them;* וַתְּהִי לִי לְאִשָּׁה [Gn 20:12](https://ref.ly/Gen20:12) (E) cf. [24:67](https://ref.ly/Gen24:67) (J) \+, …וַתִּהְיֶינָה מַחְלָה וְחָגְלָה לַבְנֵי דֹדֵיהֶן לְנָשִׁים [Nu 36:11](https://ref.ly/Num36:11); וְהָיָה לְךָ וְלָהֶם לְאָכְלָה [6:21](https://ref.ly/Num6:21) (P), [Ex 2:10](https://ref.ly/Exod2:10), [15:2](https://ref.ly/Exod15:2), Ju [8:27](https://ref.ly/Judg8:27); לִי לֵאלֽהִים ´וְהָיָה י [Gn 28:21](https://ref.ly/Gen28:21) (E); אֲנִי אֶהְיֶה לוֺ לְאָב וְהוּא יִהוּא יִהְיֶה־לִי לְבֵן [1 Ch 17:3](https://ref.ly/1Chr17:3) \+; \= *shew or prove oneself as* הֱיֵה־לִי לְבֶן־חַיִל [1 S 18:17](https://ref.ly/1Sam18:17) *shew thyself for me a valiant man* (cf. [1 S 4:9](https://ref.ly/1Sam4:9) **e** supr.); \= *be instituted, established* (cf. **e** supr.) וְהָיָה לְאַהֲרֹן וּלְבָנָיו לְחָק־עוֺלָם [Ex 29:28](https://ref.ly/Exod29:28) *and it shall be instituted for Aaron and his sons as a perpetual ordinance;* cf. v:[9](https://ref.ly/Exod29:9), [Lv 10:15](https://ref.ly/Lev10:15); וְהָֽיְתָה לִהְיֹת לָהֶם מָשְׁחָתָם לִכְהֻנַּת עוֺלָם לְדֹרֹתָם [Ex 40:15](https://ref.ly/Exod40:15) (all P). **g.** with עַל and לְ [1 S 22:2](https://ref.ly/1Sam22:2) וַיְהִי עֲלֵיהֶם לְשָר *and he became over them prince.* **h.** sts. c. לְ Pers. only \= *become the property of, come into the possession of* ´וַיְהִי־לוֺ צאֹן־וּבָקָר וְגו [Gn 12:15](https://ref.ly/Gen12:15) (J), *and he came to have flocks and herds* etc., [26:14](https://ref.ly/Gen26:14), [30:43](https://ref.ly/Gen30:43); לאֹ לוֺ יִהְיֶה הַָ\+זּרַע [Gn 38:9](https://ref.ly/Gen38:9) *not his should the seed become;* ´וְהָיָה לי Ju [11:31](https://ref.ly/Judg11:31) *it shall become Yah\-weh's;* [Dt 10:9](https://ref.ly/Deut10:9), [Jos 13:29](https://ref.ly/Josh13:29), [1 K 10:26](https://ref.ly/1Kgs10:26), [11:3](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:3) \+ oft.; so of a woman, as wife תְּהִי־נָא לְךָ Ju [15:2](https://ref.ly/Judg15:2) *pray let her become thine,* cf. also וַתְּהי אֵשֶׁת שִׁמְשׁוֺן לְמֵרֵעֵהוּ [14:20](https://ref.ly/Judg14:20), [Ho 3:3](https://ref.ly/Hos3:3), [Ru 1:13](https://ref.ly/Ruth1:13), [Lv 21:3](https://ref.ly/Lev21:3), [Dt 24:2](https://ref.ly/Deut24:2), [Je 3:1](https://ref.ly/Jer3:1), Ez [16:8](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:8) (and also \+ לְאִשָּׁה, לְנָשִׁים v. **f** supr.) **III.** Be (often with subordinate idea of becoming) ;—**1\.** *exist, be in existence* (i.e. orig. *have come into existence*), כָּל־הַיּמִים אֲשֶׁר הָיָה [1 S 1:28](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:28) *all the days which he shall have been* (lived, \= חַי [Gn 5:5](https://ref.ly/Gen5:5) etc., DrSm), [Jb 3:16](https://ref.ly/Job3:16), [Is 23:13](https://ref.ly/Isa23:13); oft. c. בְּ loc. וְכֹל שִיחַ הַשָּדֶה טֶרֶם יִהְיֶה בָאָרץ [Gn 2:5](https://ref.ly/Gen2:5) (J) *and there was not yet in the earth any shrub of the field;* ´וּבְאֵלֶּה לאֹ הָיָה אִישׁ וגו [Nu 26:64](https://ref.ly/Num26:64) (P) *and among them there was not a man to be found,* etc.; also וְלאֹ הָיָה כַּיּוֺם הַהוּא לְפָנָיו וְאַחֲרָיו [Jos 10:14](https://ref.ly/Josh10:14) (JE) *\& there hath not been a day like that before it nor since.* cf. [2 S 14:25](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:25), [1 K 18:5](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:5), [21:25](https://ref.ly/1Kgs21:25), [23:25](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:25), [Ne 13:26](https://ref.ly/Neh13:26) \+; somewhat weaker, in the freq. introductory clause וַיְהִי אִישׁ *now there was a man* Ju [13:2](https://ref.ly/Judg13:2), [1 S 1:1](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:1), [9:1](https://ref.ly/1Sam9:1), [Nu 9:6](https://ref.ly/Num9:6) (P), [Jb 1:1](https://ref.ly/Job1:1) \+; on אֶהְיֶה אֲשֶׁר אֶהְיֶה [Ex 3:14](https://ref.ly/Exod3:14) v. יהוֺה. **2\.** *abide, remain, continue* (with word of place or time) וַיְהִי משֶׁה בָּהָר [Ex 24:18](https://ref.ly/Exod24:18) *and Moses remainded in the mount* forty days, etc., so [34:28](https://ref.ly/Exod34:28) (both JE), Ju [17:4](https://ref.ly/Judg17:4), [17:12](https://ref.ly/Judg17:12), [1 S 6:1](https://ref.ly/1Sam6:1), [1 K 11:20](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:20), [2 K 11:3](https://ref.ly/2Kgs11:3) \= [2 Ch 22:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr22:12) \+; also [Lv 22:27](https://ref.ly/Lev22:27), [25:28](https://ref.ly/Lev25:28) (both H), etc.; sq. עַד temp. *remain until* [Dt 22:2](https://ref.ly/Deut22:2), [1 K 11:40](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:40), [2 K 15:5](https://ref.ly/2Kgs15:5), [2 Ch 5:9](https://ref.ly/2Chr5:9), [26:21](https://ref.ly/2Chr26:21), [Dn 1:21](https://ref.ly/Dan1:21) etc.; cf. הוּא יִהְיֶה לְעוֺלָם [Ec 3:14](https://ref.ly/Eccl3:14); so also of space sq. עַד *extend unto, as far as,* [Jos 19:10](https://ref.ly/Josh19:10): of net beneath altar, [Ex 27:5](https://ref.ly/Exod27:5) *shall reach as far as* (עַד) *the middle of the altar*. **3\.** with word of locality, *be in* or *at a place, be situated stand, lie;* בִּהְיוֺתָם בַּשָּדֶה [Gn 4:8](https://ref.ly/Gen4:8) (J) *when they were in the field,* הַמָּקוֺם אֲשֶׁר־הָיָה שָׁם אָהֳלֽה בַּתְּחִלָּה [13:3](https://ref.ly/Gen13:3) (J) *the place where his tent had stood at first;* וְהָאָרֶץ תִּהְיֶה לִפְנֵיכֶם [34:10](https://ref.ly/Gen34:10) *and the land, it lieth before you;* cf. [Jos 19:1](https://ref.ly/Josh19:1), Ju [7:1](https://ref.ly/Judg7:1), [7:8](https://ref.ly/Judg7:8), [Mi 5:6](https://ref.ly/Mic5:6), [5:7](https://ref.ly/Mic5:7); sq. עַל *be, be found or situated upon* [Is 30:25](https://ref.ly/Isa30:25) *and there shall be upon every high mountain* etc., *streams, water\-courses;* Ez [37:27](https://ref.ly/Ezek37:27) *and my dwelling\-place shall be over them;* fig. of guilt דָּמִים וְהָיָה עָלָיךָ [Dt 19:10](https://ref.ly/Deut19:10) *and blood* (\-guilt) *be upon thee.* **4\.** as copula, joining subj. \& pred.:—**a.** sq. pred. adj. וַיִּהְיוּ שְׁנֵיהֶם עֲרוּמִּים [Gn 2:25](https://ref.ly/Gen2:25), וְהַנָּחָשׁ הָיָה עָרוּם [3:1](https://ref.ly/Gen3:1), בִּהְיוֺתָם כֹּאֲבִים [34:25](https://ref.ly/Gen34:25), וַיְהִי יוֺסֵף יְפֵה־תֹאַר [39:6](https://ref.ly/Gen39:6); [Dt 23:15](https://ref.ly/Deut23:15), [Jos 19:9](https://ref.ly/Josh19:9), Ju [11:1](https://ref.ly/Judg11:1), [Jb 1:1](https://ref.ly/Job1:1), [1:3b](https://ref.ly/Job1:3), [2 Ch 7:21](https://ref.ly/2Chr7:21). **b.** sq. pred. noun [Gn 1:2](https://ref.ly/Gen1:2), [9:18](https://ref.ly/Gen9:18), [25:3](https://ref.ly/Gen25:3), [36:14](https://ref.ly/Gen36:14), [40:13](https://ref.ly/Gen40:13), [1 S 17:34](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:34), [17:42](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:42), [2 S 8:10](https://ref.ly/2Sam8:10), [20:25](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:25), [1 K 10:6](https://ref.ly/1Kgs10:6), [2 K 3:4](https://ref.ly/2Kgs3:4), [5:1](https://ref.ly/2Kgs5:1), [5:1](https://ref.ly/2Kgs5:1), [1 Ch 9:20](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:20), [11:20](https://ref.ly/1Chr11:20), [18:10](https://ref.ly/1Chr18:10), [26:10](https://ref.ly/1Chr26:10), [2 Ch 13:7](https://ref.ly/2Chr13:7); כִּי לאֹ־הָיָה מֶלֶח יִשְרָאֵל [2 Ch 18:32](https://ref.ly/2Chr18:32) (‖ [1 K 22:33](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:33) כִּי־לאֹ־מֶלֶח ישראל הוּא) \+ ; oft. of age: וַיְהִי אַבְרָם בֶּן־תִּשְׁעִים שָׁנָה וְתֵשַׁע שָׁנִים [Gn 17:1](https://ref.ly/Gen17:1) (P) [2 K 8:17](https://ref.ly/2Kgs8:17), [14:2](https://ref.ly/2Kgs14:2), [15:2](https://ref.ly/2Kgs15:2), [15:33](https://ref.ly/2Kgs15:33), [18:2](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:2), [2 Ch 21:20](https://ref.ly/2Chr21:20), [27:8](https://ref.ly/2Chr27:8) \+; sq. pred. Inf. abs. (very anomal.) וְהַמַּיִם הָיוּ הָיוּ הָלוֺח וְחָסוֺר [Gn 8:5](https://ref.ly/Gen8:5); \= *amount to, come to,* in enum. of days of life number of people, amounts of money, etc.: [Gn 5:4](https://ref.ly/Gen5:4), [5:5](https://ref.ly/Gen5:5), [5:8](https://ref.ly/Gen5:8), [5:11](https://ref.ly/Gen5:11), [5:14](https://ref.ly/Gen5:14), [9:29](https://ref.ly/Gen9:29), [11:32](https://ref.ly/Gen11:32), [23:1](https://ref.ly/Gen23:1) \+; [Ex 38:24](https://ref.ly/Exod38:24), [Nu 1:46](https://ref.ly/Num1:46), etc. **c.** sq. adv. \& adverbial phr.: לאֹ־טוֺב הֱיוֺת הָאָדָם לְבַדּוֺ[Gn 2:18](https://ref.ly/Gen2:18) (J) *it is not good that the man be alone;* חָדַל לִהְיוֺת לְשָרָה אֹרַה כַּנָּשִׁים [18:11](https://ref.ly/Gen18:11) (J). **d.** sq. prep.: *(a) be with, accompany* c. עִם, subj. most oft. ´י or אלהים with added idea of protection, aid [Gn 26:3](https://ref.ly/Gen26:3), [31:3](https://ref.ly/Gen31:3), [39:10](https://ref.ly/Gen39:10), [48:21](https://ref.ly/Gen48:21), [Jos 1:3](https://ref.ly/Josh1:3), [6:27](https://ref.ly/Josh6:27), Ju [1:19](https://ref.ly/Judg1:19), [2:18](https://ref.ly/Judg2:18), [1 S 3:19](https://ref.ly/1Sam3:19), [18:12](https://ref.ly/1Sam18:12), [20:13](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:13), [1 K 1:37](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:37), [1 Ch 11:13](https://ref.ly/1Chr11:13), [2 Ch 17:3](https://ref.ly/2Chr17:3); sq. עִמָּדִי [Gn 28:20](https://ref.ly/Gen28:20), [31:5](https://ref.ly/Gen31:5), [35:3](https://ref.ly/Gen35:3); sq. עִם, human subj., of accompaniment וְהָיָההַכֹּהֵן בֶּן־אַהֲרֹן עִם־הַלְוִיִּם [Ne 10:39](https://ref.ly/Neh10:39); [1 Ch 11:12](https://ref.ly/1Chr11:12), rd. also in ‖ [2 S 23:9](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:9) (Th We Dr); \= *be on the side of* [1 K 1:8](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:8); c. עִם also \= *be near* הָיָה עִם־גֹּרֶן האורנה ´וּמַלְאַח י [2 S 24:16](https://ref.ly/2Sam24:16) (cf. Dr); also [2 S 13:20](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:20) implying sexual intercourse, so [Gn 39:10](https://ref.ly/Gen39:10) (perh. gloss, cf. Di); peculiar uses with עִם are: וּדְבַר־אַבְנֵר הָיָה עִם־זִקְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל [2 S 3:17](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:17) *and the speech of A. had been with the elders of Isr.,* cf. [1 K 1:7](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:7); וַיְהִי עִם־לְבַב דָּוִד אָבִּי לִבְנוֺת [1 K 8:17](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:17) *and it was with* (i.e. in) *the heart of David my father to build,* \= [2 Ch 6:7](https://ref.ly/2Chr6:7) cf. [1 K 8:18](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:18), [8:18](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:18), [1 Ch 22:7](https://ref.ly/1Chr22:7), [2 Ch 6:8](https://ref.ly/2Chr6:8), [6:8](https://ref.ly/2Chr6:8), [24:4](https://ref.ly/2Chr24:4); also [1 K 10:2](https://ref.ly/1Kgs10:2) \= [2 Ch 9:1](https://ref.ly/2Chr9:1); (*b*) be with, sq. אֶת, subj. ´י, אלהים, [Gn 21:20](https://ref.ly/Gen21:20), [39:2](https://ref.ly/Gen39:2), [39:21](https://ref.ly/Gen39:21) but also c. human subj. וּבָנָיוּ הָיוּ אֶת־מִקְנֵהוּ [Gn 34:5](https://ref.ly/Gen34:5), cf. [1 S 29:8](https://ref.ly/1Sam29:8) \+; (*c*) sq. לְ of possessor כֶּרֶם הָיָה לִידִדִי [Is 5:1](https://ref.ly/Isa5:1) *all vineyard had my beloved,* [Gn 30:30](https://ref.ly/Gen30:30) cf. [Ex 20:3](https://ref.ly/Exod20:3) \= [Dt 5:7](https://ref.ly/Deut5:7), Ju [18:27](https://ref.ly/Judg18:27), [1 S 9:2](https://ref.ly/1Sam9:2), [1 K 5:6](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:6) \+ oft. (cf. I. **2 d, f, h**); (*d*) sq. לְ \+ other prep.: of boundary וַיְהִי לָהֶם הַגְּבוּל מֵעֲרוֺעֵר[Jos 13:16](https://ref.ly/Josh13:16), cf. וַיְהִי גְבוּלָם מִמַּחֲנַיִם v:[30](https://ref.ly/Josh13:30); וַוְהִי לָהֶם הַגְּבוּל לִפְאַת צָפנַָה מִיַּרְדֵּן [18:12](https://ref.ly/Josh18:12), etc.; (*e*) sq. בְּ *be among,* or *of,* [Am 1:1](https://ref.ly/Amos1:1) *who was* one *of the herdsmen,* [1 K 2:7](https://ref.ly/1Kgs2:7) *let them be among those eating,* [Pr 22:26](https://ref.ly/Prov22:26), [23:20](https://ref.ly/Prov23:20). **5\.** periphrastic conjug.: **a.** היה \+ pt., of continuous state, or condition, sts., esp. late (cf. NH), of habit, c. וַיְהִי also of beginning and continuance (cf. Dr§ 135, (5\)): pt. act. [39:8](https://ref.ly/Ezek39:8) (‖ *id.*);—also אֵיכָה נִהְיְתָה הָרָעָה הַזּאֹת Ju [20:3](https://ref.ly/Judg20:3) *how hath this wickedness been done ?* cf. v:[12](https://ref.ly/Judg20:12), [Je 5:30](https://ref.ly/Jer5:30), [48:19](https://ref.ly/Jer48:19) also [Ne 6:8](https://ref.ly/Neh6:8); c. לאֹ, existence denied ´לאֹ נ [Zc 8:10](https://ref.ly/Zech8:10) (‖אֵיֶ\+ננָּה); in all the above (exc. [Jo 2:2](https://ref.ly/Joel2:2), [Dn 12:1](https://ref.ly/Dan12:1) \& Ez [21:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:12), [39:8](https://ref.ly/Ezek39:8)) personal agency is clearly implied; it is expressed ´מֵאֵת אֲדֹנִי הַמֶּלֶצ נ הַדָּבָר הַזֶּה [1 K 1:27](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:27), so of divine agency (מֵאִתִּי) [1 K 12:24](https://ref.ly/1Kgs12:24) \= [2 Ch 11:4](https://ref.ly/2Chr11:4); further *be attained, secured,* of wages [Zc 8:10](https://ref.ly/Zech8:10) (c. לאֹ, ‖אֵינֶ\+נָּה); תַּאֲוָּה נִהְיָה [Pr 13:19](https://ref.ly/Prov13:19) *a desire come to pass, realized.* **2\.** *be done, finished, gone,* only Dn וּשְׁנָתוֺ נִהְיְתָה עָלָיו [Dn 2:1](https://ref.ly/Dan2:1) *and his sleep was finished upon him,* i.e. left him (cf. Aram. [Dn 6:19](https://ref.ly/Dan6:19)); נִהְיֵיתִי [8:27](https://ref.ly/Dan8:27) *I came to an end, was exhausted* (si vera l., del. ## הֵיךְ (id: BDB02384)

\[Str1963] † הֵיךְ **adv.** How? (היך, ܗܝܟ, *id.,* in Palest. Aram., as [Jb 21:34](https://ref.ly/Job21:34) Targum, [Gn 3:9](https://ref.ly/Gen3:9), [Dt 1:12](https://ref.ly/Deut1:12) TargumPa. J; [Mat 22:12](https://ref.ly/Matt22:12) SyrVerJerus. (though usu. these words have the force of *as*), and in הֵיכְדֵין *how, then?* [Psalm 73:11](https://ref.ly/Ps73:11) \+ Targum; cf. BevanDan.P. 29\)—only twice, in late Heb., [1 Ch 13:12](https://ref.ly/1Chr13:12) (‖ [2 S 6:9](https://ref.ly/2Sam6:9) אֵיח), [Dan 10:17](https://ref.ly/Dan10:17).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 73:11; Matthew 22:12; Daniel 10:17; Deuteronomy 1:12; Job 21:34; 2 Samuel 6:9; 1 Chronicles 13:12; Genesis 3:9


## הֵיכָל (id: BDB02385)

\[Str1964] † הֵיכָל **n.m.**:[Na 2:7](https://ref.ly/Nah2:7) (on [Is 44:28](https://ref.ly/Isa44:28) cf; Che Di) palace, temple (v. also NH *id. temple,* Aram. הֵיכָלָא, הֵיכְלָא, ܗܰܝܟܰܠ, *palace, temple,* Palm. הכלא *temple* Vog16; cf. Ar. هَيْكَلٌ (prob. Aram. loan\-wd. acc. to Frä274), Eth. ሀይከል፡ *temple;* As. *ekallu, palace, temple;*—prob. loan\-wd. fr. Akk. *e\-gal, great house,* cf. O1:§ 191 b HoffmAGG xxxvi. Mal 1889, 25 Dlw; fr. an orig. יכל LagBN 121, but cf. HomZMG 1890\.547\) abs. ´ה [Am 8:3](https://ref.ly/Amos8:3) \+ 27 t. his abode in the heavens [2 S 22:7](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:7) \= [Psalm 18:7](https://ref.ly/Ps18:7), [Psalm 11:4](https://ref.ly/Ps11:4) (‖שָׁמַיִם; cf. זְבֻל קָדְשׁוֺ [Is 63:15](https://ref.ly/Isa63:15), ‖ *id.*), [29:9](https://ref.ly/Isa29:9); > \[Str1969] † הִין **n.m.**:[Lv 19:36](https://ref.ly/Lev19:36) hin, a liquid measure (LXX εἴν (Ἴν; LXXL Ἴν); but χοῦς [Lv 19:36](https://ref.ly/Lev19:36); Gk. ἴυιον, Egypt. measure, \= *sextarius,* acc. t o BöckhMetrol. Unters. 244 cf. BrandisMünz\- Mass\- \& Gewichtswesen 29, 30; the Heb. hin (6\.06 litres) was, however, much larger than the Egypt. hin (0\.456 litre), v. HultschMetrol., Tab. xxi. p. 714, \& pp. 368, 450 ff.; v. further RiHWB SmithDict.Bib. E. MeyerGesch. i. 228\)—abs. הִין [Ex 30:24](https://ref.ly/Exod30:24) \+ 4 t. Ez 45, 46; הַהִין [Ex 29:40](https://ref.ly/Exod29:40), [29:40](https://ref.ly/Exod29:40) \+ 14 t.; cstr. הִין [Lv 19:36](https://ref.ly/Lev19:36); —**1\.** a measure, used of quantity of water for drinking Ez [4:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek4:11); usually in connexion with offerings: **a.** of oil used with meal\-offering [Ex 29:40](https://ref.ly/Exod29:40), [Nu 15:4](https://ref.ly/Num15:4), [15:6](https://ref.ly/Num15:6), [15:9](https://ref.ly/Num15:9), [28:5](https://ref.ly/Num28:5) (all P), Ez [45:24](https://ref.ly/Ezek45:24), [46:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek46:5), [46:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek46:6), [46:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek46:11), [46:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek46:14); —usually 1/4 hin to 1/10 ephah (of meal\-offering); [Nu 15:6](https://ref.ly/Num15:6) 1/3; v:[9](https://ref.ly/Num15:9) 1/2; in Ez a (whole) hin, exc. [46:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek46:14) 1/3 hin to 1/6\. ephah. **b.** used in mixing the anointing oil [Ex 30:24](https://ref.ly/Exod30:24). **c.** of wine as drink\-offering [Lv 23:13](https://ref.ly/Lev23:13) (H); elsewh. P, [Ex 29:40](https://ref.ly/Exod29:40), [Nu 15:5](https://ref.ly/Num15:5), [28:14](https://ref.ly/Num28:14) (all 1/4 hin) [15:7](https://ref.ly/Num15:7), [28:14](https://ref.ly/Num28:14) (both 1/3 hin), [15:10](https://ref.ly/Num15:10), [28:14](https://ref.ly/Num28:14) (both 1/2 hin; all with 1/10 to 3/10 ephah in case of meal\-offering). **d.** once of שֵׁכָר as drink\-offering [Nu 28:7](https://ref.ly/Num28:7) (P; 1/4 hin; cf. Di). **2\.** vessel with capacity of hin [Lv 19:36](https://ref.ly/Lev19:36) (H) הִין־צֶדֶק *a just hin* (‖´מאֹזְנֵי צ, ´אַבְנֵי־צ, ´אֵיפַת צ).—The hin \= 1/6 bath acc. to Joseph.:Ant. iii. 8, 3; 9, 4, \= 6\.06 litres (Hultschl.c.), v. II. בַּת. On order of words in above passages, note that הִין (´רְבִיעִת ה etc.) is usually appos. of liquid measured, \& either precedes it [Ex 29:40](https://ref.ly/Exod29:40), [Nu 15:4](https://ref.ly/Num15:4), [28:14](https://ref.ly/Num28:14), or, more oft., follows it [Ex 30:24](https://ref.ly/Exod30:24) \+ 10 t.; [Nu 28:7](https://ref.ly/Num28:7) they are in diff. clauses; in [Ex 29:40](https://ref.ly/Exod29:40) we find רֶבַע הַהִין…בְּשֶׁמֶן, cf. [Nu 15:6](https://ref.ly/Num15:6), [15:9](https://ref.ly/Num15:9), [28:5](https://ref.ly/Num28:5); —[Nu 28:14](https://ref.ly/Num28:14) is peculiar: וְנִסְכֵּיהֶם חֲצִי הַהִין יִהְיֶה לַפָּר וּשְׁלִישִׁת הַהִין לָאַיִל וּרְבִיעִת הַהִין לַכֶּבֶש ָ\+ייִן (יין only at end).

## הָכַר (id: BDB02391)

\[Str1970] † \[הָכַר] **vb.** only **Qal** or **Hiph.** (Ges§ 53 R. 4 Köi. 251\) *Impf.* 2 mpl. תַּהְכְּרוּ in phrase לאֹ־תֵבשׁוּ תַהְכְּרוּ־לִי [Jb 19:3](https://ref.ly/Job19:3), sense very dubious ; Schult Thes De al. (as Hiph.) *ye came me* (לְ \= acc.) *to wonder* (cf. Ar. هَكَرَ); others (as Qal) *ye deal hardy with, wrong me* (so context seems rather to require, v. Ges in Add84 RobGes Ew Di Da RV; cf. Ar. حَكَرَ *wrong, detract from;* Codd. rd. תחכרו, which is preferable, if this mng. be adopted; LXX ἑπίκεισθέ μοι, Vulgate *opprimentes*); conjectures are תְּחָֽרְפוּ O1Hiob; תַּחַבְרוּ אֵלָי Me; Siegf תִּתְחַבְּרוּ.

> 229:b. \[הָלָא]: [Mi 4:7](https://ref.ly/Mic4:7) We prop. הַנַּחֲלָה (√ חלה) *her that is diseased* (note ‖הַצֹּלֵעָה, and cf. Ez [34:21](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:21)), so Now; Gr הַנִּלְּאָה *her that is exhausted;* Marti allows either of these.

## הָֽ֫לְאָה (id: BDB02395)

\[Str1973] † הָֽ֫לְאָה (i.e. *hā\-le’ āh,* the final *\-āh,* the ה *locale,* being toneless), **adv.** out there, onwards, further (Targum הָלָא, always with לְ or מֵ prefixed; Syr. ܠܗܰܠ, ܘܰܠܗܰܠ PS1009 *id.;* Ar.هَلَا *forward! on!* F1Kl. Schr. i. 355\)—**a.** of *place* [Gn 19:9](https://ref.ly/Gen19:9) גֶּשׁ־הָֽלְאָה *approach out there!* i.e. *make way, get back* (LXX ἁπόστα ἑκεἰ: Vulgate *recede illuc*), [Nu 17:2](https://ref.ly/Num17:2) and the fire זְרֵה־הָֽלְאָה scatter *yonder;* מֵהָֽלְאָה לְ *beyond* (lit. *off the yonder side of;* v. מִן **4 c**) [Gn 35:21](https://ref.ly/Gen35:21), [Je 22:19](https://ref.ly/Jer22:19), [Am 5:27](https://ref.ly/Amos5:27) *beyond* Damascus: וָהָֽלְאָה attached to an indication of place, to mark direction, [1 S 10:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam10:3) and thou shalt pass on מִשָּׁם וָהָֽלְאָה thence *and onwards;* [20:22](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:22) מִמְּךָ וָהָֽלְאָה from thee *and onwards* i.e. beyond thee (opp. to מִמְּךָ וָהֵ֫נָּה v:[21](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:21)), v:[37](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:37), [Nu 32:19](https://ref.ly/Num32:19) on the side of Jordan *forwards* (opp. to ‘on the side of Jordan eastwards’). **b.** of *time,* [Lv 22:27](https://ref.ly/Lev22:27) from the 8th day וָהָֽלְאָה *and onwards,* [Nu 15:23](https://ref.ly/Num15:23), [1 S 18:9](https://ref.ly/1Sam18:9) מהיום ההוא והלאה from that day *and onwards,* Ez [39:22](https://ref.ly/Ezek39:22), [43:27](https://ref.ly/Ezek43:27), [Is 18:2](https://ref.ly/Isa18:2), [18:7](https://ref.ly/Isa18:7). Hence

## הַלָּז (id: BDB02397)

\[Str1975] † הַלָּז **pron.comm.** this, a rare synonym of זֶה or הַזֶּה, *this* (Ar. أَلَّدِي (also اَلَّذْ), pl. اَلَّذِينَ, *who, which* (cf. זֶה, which is used also sts. as rel.), formed from اَلْ, the def. art., and the pron ܗܠܟ זֶה, זוּ, with the insertion of the demonstr. element *la,* and thus in fact \= הַזֶּה: WAGi. § 347, CG p. 117\)—usually with a subst. defined by the art., Ju [6:20](https://ref.ly/Judg6:20) הַסֶּלַע הַלָּז *this* rock, [1 S 14:1](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:1) מֵעֵבֶר הַלָּז *off this* side\-across, [17:26](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:26) אֶת־הַפְּלִשְׁתִּי הַלָּז, (\+ [20:19](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:19) LXX Th We Dr τὸ ἐργαβ ἐκεῖνο, i.e. (or הַלָּאז) הָאַרְגָּב הַלָּז *this* mound (*or* cairn), for האבן האזל) [2 K 23:17](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:17), [Zc 2:8](https://ref.ly/Zech2:8); with a *fem.,* [2 K 4:25](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:25) הַשּׁוּנַמִּית הַלּז: without a subst. [Dn 8:16](https://ref.ly/Dan8:16) לְהַלָּז to *this one* (\= normal לָזֶה). The fuller form is

* **Associated Passages:** 2 Kings 23:17; Zechariah 2:8; 1 Samuel 14:1; 2 Kings 4:25; 1 Samuel 20:19; Judges 6:20; 1 Samuel 17:26; Daniel 8:16

Imv. in same construction: לֶח־לְךָ אֶל [Gn 22:2](https://ref.ly/Gen22:2) (E) *get thee unto*, לְכוּ לָכֶם לְאָהֳלֵיכֶם [Jos 22:4](https://ref.ly/Josh22:4) (D) also לֶח־לְךָ מִן [Gn 12:1](https://ref.ly/Gen12:1) (J), \& לְכִי־לָךְ Ct [2:10](https://ref.ly/Song2:10), [2:13](https://ref.ly/Song2:13) *get thee away, come away.* θ adverbial phrases of manner c. prep.: (*a*) בְּ, בְּשָׁלוֺם ´ה *go in peace* [Gn 26:31](https://ref.ly/Gen26:31)(J) [1 S 29:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam29:7), [2 S 3:21](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:21), [3:22](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:22), [3:23](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:23), [15:9](https://ref.ly/2Sam15:9); בְּכֹהַ הָאֲכִילָה הַהִיא ´ה [1 K 19:8](https://ref.ly/1Kgs19:8), בְּחֵמָה ´ה [2 K 5:12](https://ref.ly/2Kgs5:12) cf. also [1 S 17:39](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:39), [Is 52:12](https://ref.ly/Isa52:12); בְּסַעֲרוֺת תֵּימָן ´וְה [Zc 9:14](https://ref.ly/Zech9:14) of ´י, *and he shall go in* (or with) *storms of the south;* בְּ pretii, הַהֹלְכִים בְּנַפְשׁוֺתָם [2 S 23:17](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:17) *who went at risk of their life* (otherwise in ‖ [1 Ch 11:19](https://ref.ly/1Chr11:19)); cf. (*b*) אֶל, אֶל־נַפְשׁוֺ ´ה [1 K 19:3](https://ref.ly/1Kgs19:3) *he went for his life. (c)* לְ, לְרַגְלָהּ ´ה [1 S 25:42](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:42) *go according to her foot*, i.e. follow, attend her; לֵךְ לְשָׁלוֺם *go to, for* (in) *peace* [Ex 4:18](https://ref.ly/Exod4:18) (E) Ju [18:6](https://ref.ly/Judg18:6), [1 S 1:17](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:17), [20:13](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:13), [20:42](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:42), [2 K 5:19](https://ref.ly/2Kgs5:19); וְהֹלְכִים לְתֻמָּם [2 S 15:11](https://ref.ly/2Sam15:11). κ. with adj. or noun, agreeing with subj. of vb., in acc. (Ges§ 118\.5\), sometimes where Lat. \& Eng. use an attributive or appositive: *walk barefoot* [2 S 15:30](https://ref.ly/2Sam15:30); *walk naked and barefoot* [Is 20:3](https://ref.ly/Isa20:3) (c. acc. of duration); ´וַתֵּלַכְנָה נְטֻו֯וֺת גָּרוֺן רגו [Is 3:16](https://ref.ly/Isa3:16); cf. [1 K 8:66](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:66); also לאֹ תֵלֵךְ רָכִיל בְּעַמֶָּךָ [Lv 19:16](https://ref.ly/Lev19:16) (H) *thou* *shalt not walk as a slanderer among thy people,* [Je 9:3](https://ref.ly/Jer9:3), הוֺלֵךְ רָכִיל [Pr 11:13](https://ref.ly/Prov11:13), [20:19](https://ref.ly/Prov20:19); and even הֹלְכֵי רָכִיל [Je 6:28](https://ref.ly/Jer6:28) (yet רכיל poss. abstr., ´ר ´ה *walk in slander*); with adv. accus., construed more generally (Ges§ 118, 5\. c., Ew§ 279 c); *ye shall not walk loftily* (רוֺמָה) [Mi 2:3](https://ref.ly/Mic2:3); *they shall come bending* (שְׁחוֺחַ) [Is 60:14](https://ref.ly/Isa60:14); *and Agag came delicately* (מַעֲרַנֹּת) [1 S 15:82](https://ref.ly/1Sam15:82); cf. also [Lv 26:23](https://ref.ly/Lev26:23) (II. **3b**) [Ex 3:21](https://ref.ly/Exod3:21), [2 S 17:18](https://ref.ly/2Sam17:18).

 **2\.** Also of animals, in similar meanings and combinations: הָלַךְ [Na 2:12](https://ref.ly/Nah2:12) *walk about, prowl* (lions, fig. of rulers of Assyria); [Pr 30:29](https://ref.ly/Prov30:29) (vid. v:[30](https://ref.ly/Prov30:30), v:[31](https://ref.ly/Prov30:31), of three beasts \& a king); *walk, go, move* ´בִּמְסִלָּה אַחַת ה [1 S 6:12](https://ref.ly/1Sam6:12) (of cows bringing ark), horses in Zec.'s vision [Zc 6:7](https://ref.ly/Zech6:7) וַיְבַקְשׁוּ לָלֶכֶת לְהִתְהַלֵךְ בָּאָרֶץ; of beasts \& birds [Je 9:9](https://ref.ly/Jer9:9), cf. [50:3](https://ref.ly/Jer50:3) *both man \& beast, they are fled, they are gone;* of flocks and herbs (מִקְנֶה) יֵלֵח עִמָּנוּ [Ex 10:26](https://ref.ly/Exod10:26) *they shall go with us;* מֵהר אֶל־גִּבְעָה ´ה [Je 50:6](https://ref.ly/Jer50:6) *they have gone fr. mt. to hill* (Isr. under fig. of wandering sheep); in [1 S 14:26](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:26) rd. prob. הָלַךְ for הֵלֶךְ q.v.; esp. in laws of clean \& unclean [Lv 11:20](https://ref.ly/Lev11:20), [11:21](https://ref.ly/Lev11:21) *every swarming winged thing that goeth on* (הַהֹלֵחעַל) *all fours* cf. v:[27](https://ref.ly/Lev11:27), v:[42](https://ref.ly/Lev11:42); also v:[27](https://ref.ly/Lev11:27) *everything that goeth on its paws,* \& v:[42](https://ref.ly/Lev11:42) *everything that goeth on (its) belly,* (all P), so of serpent [Gn 3:14](https://ref.ly/Gen3:14) *on thy belly* (עַל־גְּחֹנְךָ) *shalt thou go* (J), cf. also [Je 46:22](https://ref.ly/Jer46:22) קוֺלָהּ כַּנָּחָשׁ יֵלֵח; (where appar. rel. cl., *her voice like the serpent that moves,* i.e. like the rustling sound of a gliding serpent, cf. VB); locusts [Jos 2:7](https://ref.ly/Josh2:7) (אִישׁ בִּדְרָכָיו), v:[8](https://ref.ly/Josh2:8) (גֶּבֶר בִּמְסִלָּתוֺ). **3\.** in like manner of inanimate things, as: the ark (הֵַתּבָה) [Gn 7:18](https://ref.ly/Gen7:18) (P; sq. עַל־פְּנֵי הַמָּיִם); ships [1 K 22:49](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:49) (sq. ה—ָ loc.) \= [2 Ch 20:36](https://ref.ly/2Chr20:36) (sq. acc. of limit), cf. [2 Ch 9:21](https://ref.ly/2Chr9:21) (sq. acc.) [20:37](https://ref.ly/2Chr20:37) (אֶל); of אֳנִי־שַׁיִט [Is 33:21](https://ref.ly/Isa33:21), ls*galley with oars*rs (sq. בְּ of place in which); wheels (in Ezekiel's vision) Ez [1:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:17), [1:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:17), [1:19](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:19), [1:21](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:21), [10:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek10:11), [10:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek10:11), [10:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek10:11), [10:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek10:11); moon [Jb 31:26](https://ref.ly/Job31:26); wind [Psalm 78:39](https://ref.ly/Ps78:39), [Ec 1:6](https://ref.ly/Eccl1:6), [1:6](https://ref.ly/Eccl1:6); of streams (הַנְחָלִים) \= *flow* אֶל־הַיָּם [Ec 1:7](https://ref.ly/Eccl1:7); cf. אֶל־מְקוֺם שֶׁהַנְחָלִים הֹלְכִים שָׁם הֵם שָׁבִים לָלֶכֶת v:[7](https://ref.ly/Eccl1:7) *unto the place whither the streams flow, thither they flow again;* also אֲפִיקִים, sq. מַיִם as accus. of that with wh., [Jo 4:18](https://ref.ly/Joel4:18) ‖ of hills *flowing* חָלָב, *with milk;* cf. further fig. כָּל־בִּרְכַּ יִם תֵּלַכְנָה מַיִם Ez [7:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek7:17), [21:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:12) *all knees shall flow down in water,* i.e. be as weak as water; נְהָרוֺת Ez [31:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek31:4) (sq. סָבִיב); וְהָלַךְ עַל־כָּל־גְּדוֺתָיו [Is 8:7](https://ref.ly/Isa8:7) i.e. *overflow* (‖עָלָה עַל־אֲפִיקָיו) cf. [Jos 4:18](https://ref.ly/Josh4:18) (JE); so of water (as SI4) [Is 8:6](https://ref.ly/Isa8:6), [1 K 18:35](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:35) (הַמַּיִם סָבִיב לַמִּזְבֵָּחַוַיֵּלְכוּ), [Psalm 105:41](https://ref.ly/Ps105:41) (הָֽלְכוּ בַּצִיּוֺת נָהָר, *they flowed through dry regions as a river*); (on Inf. abs. used of waters of flood, [Gn 8:3](https://ref.ly/Gen8:3), [8:5](https://ref.ly/Gen8:5), cf. infr. **4 c** (4\), (5\)); of the sea הַיָּם הוֺלֵח וְסֹעֵר [Jon 1:11](https://ref.ly/Jonah1:11), [1:13](https://ref.ly/Jonah1:13) *was going on and being stormy \= was growing more and more stormy* (cf. infr. **4 d**); of wine (יַיִו) Ct [7:10](https://ref.ly/Song7:10) *that goeth down, for my beloved, smoothly* (smile); also of lighting *go, dart* (בָּרָק) [Jb 38:35](https://ref.ly/Job38:35), so אֵשׁ [Ex 9:23](https://ref.ly/Exod9:23) (E) וַתִּהֲלַח אֵשׁ אָרְצָה *and fire darted to earth* (on form, here \& [Psalm 73:9](https://ref.ly/Ps73:9) infr., as intens., of rapid motion, cf. Thes Ew§ 138 b Köi. 415\); אֵשׁ לְפָנָיו תֵּלֵח [Psalm 97:3](https://ref.ly/Ps97:3); of torches, in fig. of violent breathing of crocodile יַהֲלָֽכוּ מִפִּיו לַפִּידִים [Jb 41:11](https://ref.ly/Job41:11); of sword אַחֲרַיִךְ תֵּ֫לֶךְ חָרֶב % [Je 48:2](https://ref.ly/Jer48:2), of arrow בָּרָק [Jb 20:25](https://ref.ly/Job20:25) drawn from a wound; of pestilence דֶּבֶר (personified) [Psalm 91:6](https://ref.ly/Ps91:6) (בָּאֹפֶל), Hb [3:5](https://ref.ly/Hab3:5) (לְפָנָיו); of fame שָׁעְֶוֺ הוֺלֵח בְּכָל־הַמְּדִינוֺת [Est 9:4](https://ref.ly/Esth9:4); of the tongue (bad sense) וּלְשׁוֺנָם תּהֲלַח בָּאָרֶץ [Psalm 73:9](https://ref.ly/Ps73:9) (cf. [Ex 9:23](https://ref.ly/Exod9:23) supr.; ‖שַׁתּוּ בַשָׁמַיִם פִּיהֶם); of sound of trumpet [Ex 19:19](https://ref.ly/Exod19:19) (Pt. cf. infra **4 d**); of letters אִגְּרוֺת [Ne 6:17](https://ref.ly/Neh6:17) (sq. עַל, unto); of dew (טַל, ‖עֲנַן־בֹּקֶר) sim. of Isr.'s חסֶד [Ho 6:4](https://ref.ly/Hos6:4) cf. [13:3](https://ref.ly/Hos13:3) *like dew that passeth away;* so of עָנָן [Jb 7:9](https://ref.ly/Job7:9) עָנָן וַיֵּלַָחכָּלָה; of rain הַגֶּשֶׁם חָלַף הָלַךְ לוֺ Ct [2:11](https://ref.ly/Song2:11) *the rain is over, it hath taken itself away;* of ark of ´י (´אֲרוֺן י [1 S 6:8](https://ref.ly/1Sam6:8) *depart* (cf. also **5 d**); of shadow on dial *go forward* [2 K 20:9](https://ref.ly/2Kgs20:9) (opp. שׁוּב; ‖נָטָה v:[20](https://ref.ly/2Kgs20:20) \& יַרַד [Is 38:8](https://ref.ly/Isa38:8)); *grow,* of wicked under fig. of tress, יֵלְכוּ גַּם־עָשוּ פֶָּרִי [Je 12:2](https://ref.ly/Jer12:2); cf. יֵלְכוּ יוֺנְקוֺתָיו [Ho 14:3](https://ref.ly/Hos14:3) *his branches shall spread,* said of Isr. under fig. of tree; of name (\= fame) [2 Ch 26:8](https://ref.ly/2Chr26:8) (sq. עַד־לְבֹא מִעצרַיִם); also of a thing that does not actually move; וְהָלַךְ הַגְּבוּל אֶל [Jos 17:7](https://ref.ly/Josh17:7) *and the boundary went unto* etc., cf. [16:8](https://ref.ly/Josh16:8) (sq. מִן \& ה—ָ loc.);—in [2 K 13:21](https://ref.ly/2Kgs13:21) *and it* (the dead body) *went* i.e. was let down into the grave, rd. וַיֵּלְכוּ for וַיֵּלֶח LXXL Th Klo, *and they went away*. † **4\.** The Inf. abs. is often used **a.** as in other vbs., quite independently (Ges§ 113, 1\): וְהָלֽךְ אַחֲרִי אֱלֽהִים אֲחֵרִים… … [Je 7:9](https://ref.ly/Jer7:9) (series of 6 Inf. abs.) (are there) stealing, murdering, and adultery and false swearing and incense\-burning to the Baal, *and going after other gods…?* as obj., וְלאֹ־אָבוּ בִרְרָכָיו הָלוֺךְ [Is 42:24](https://ref.ly/Isa42:24) *and they desired not walking in his ways;* as appos. of obj., [Je 23:14](https://ref.ly/Jer23:14) *I have seen … an abominable thing, committing adultery and walking in lies* (נָאוֺף וְהָלוֺךְ בַּשֶּׁקֶר); cf. וַיַּעַש כֵּן הָלוֺךְ עָרוֺם וְיָחֵף [Is 20:2](https://ref.ly/Isa20:2) *and he did so, a walking naked and barefoot,* where also two adverb. accus. modifying the Inf. abs. (Ges§ 113, 2\). **b.** to intensify meaning of finite form of הָלַךְ (Ges§ 118, 3\): (1\) before finite vb. הָלוֺךְ אֵלֵךְ עִמָּךְ Ju [4:9](https://ref.ly/Judg4:9) *I will surely go with thee,* cf. [Je 37:9](https://ref.ly/Jer37:9); הָלֽךְ הָלַכְתָּ [Gn 31:30](https://ref.ly/Gen31:30) concessive, *thou art indeed gone,* הָעֵצִים הָּלוֺךְ חָֽלְכוּ Ju [9:8](https://ref.ly/Judg9:8) introductory, *the trees went forth on a time* to anoint them a king. (2\) after finite vb. וַיֵּלֶךְ הָלוֺךְ [2 S 3:24](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:24) *and he is actually gone!* (a grave impurdence to permit it!), ´נֵלְכָה הָלוֺךְ לְחַלּוֺת אֶת־פְּנֵי י [Zc 8:21](https://ref.ly/Zech8:21) *let us by all means go to seek the favour of* ´י **c.** most noteworthy is the joining of the Inf. abs. *(I)* with a following Inf. abs. denoting a simulaneous action or process, and so emphasizing duration or continuance: *a.* both Infs. preceding the finite vb., הָלוֺךְ וְטָפֹף תֵּלַכְנָה[Is 3:16](https://ref.ly/Isa3:16) *a going and atripping they go,* i.e. they keep tripping as they go; so הָלוֺךְ וּבָכוֺ יֵלֵָכוּ [Je 50:4](https://ref.ly/Jer50:4) *continually weeping they shall go;* β. one Inf. before, \& one after the fin.vb. הָלוֺךְ יֵלֵךְ וּבָבֹה [Psalm 126:6](https://ref.ly/Ps126:6) *continually may* one *go weeping* γ. elsewhere both after fin. vb. [Jos 6:9](https://ref.ly/Josh6:9) *the rearguard was going* (הֹלֵךְ) *after the ark, continually blowing on the trumpets* (הָלוֺךְ וְתָקוֺעַ בַּשּׁוֺפָרוֺת), cf. v:[13](https://ref.ly/Josh6:13) (where הָו֯לֽךְ cf, infr.; both JE), …וַיֵּלֶח הָלוֺךְ וּבָכוֺ [2 S 3:16](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:16), of cows הָֽלְכוּ הָלוֺךְ וְגָעוֺ [1 S 6:12](https://ref.ly/1Sam6:12) *they went continually lowing,* וַיֵּלֶךְ הָלוֺךְ וְאָבֹל Ju [14:9](https://ref.ly/Judg14:9) *and he went on, eating as he went;* הֹלְכִים הָלוֺךְ וְרַבֵּר [2 K 2:11](https://ref.ly/2Kgs2:11) *they were going on, talking as they went;* in הֹלֵךְ הָלךְ וּבֹכֶה [Je 41:6](https://ref.ly/Jer41:6), the pt. בֹּכֶה is peculiar, rd. perh. בָּכֹה, and for הֹלֵךְ rd. הֹלְכִים LXX Hi cf. Gf. (2\) with a foll. vb. fin. c. ו consec. (rare): הֹלְכִים הָלוֺךְ וְתֽקְעוּ [Jos 6:13](https://ref.ly/Josh6:13) (JE) they were *going along continually blowing* on the trumpets; וַתַּלֶךְ הָלוֺךְ וְזָעָקָה [2 S 13:19](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:19) *and she went, crying out as she went;* הָלוֺךְ…הֹלֵךְ וִיְקַלֵּל [2 S 16:13](https://ref.ly/2Sam16:13) he *went along…cursing as he went;* so [1 S 19:23](https://ref.ly/1Sam19:23) *and he went on, prophesying as he went,* until he came, etc. (3\) in cases where vb. fin. is foll. by Inf. abs. \+ adj. denoting progress, advance: וַיּלֶךְ חָלוֺךְ וְקָרֵב % [2 S 18:25](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:25) *and he came continually nearer* (nearer \& nearer); elsewh. with idea of actual motion lost fr.vb. fin.: וַיֵּלֶךְ הָלוֺךְ וָרָב [1 S 14:19](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:19) *and he tumult kept growing greater and greater;* so וְגָדוֺל וַיֵּלֶךְ דָּוִד הָלוֺךְ [2 S 5:10](https://ref.ly/2Sam5:10) \= [1 Ch 11:9](https://ref.ly/1Chr11:9); cf. Ju [4:24](https://ref.ly/Judg4:24) *and the hand of the sons of Israel kept growing more and more severe* (הָלוֺךְ וְקָשָׁה…וַתֵּלָךְ) *upon Jabin;* note esp. וַיִּגֲּדּל הָאִישׁ וַיֵּלֶךְ הָלוֺךְ וְגָדֵל עַד־בִּ־י גֶרַל מְאֹד [Gn 26:13](https://ref.ly/Gen26:13) (J) *and the man grew great and kept on growing greater and greater until* etc. (נָּדֵל partic. adj.). (4\) twice, where vb. fin. is not הלך, but another vb. denoting motion: הָלוֺךְ וָשׁוֺב…וַיָּשֻׁבוּ הַמַּיִם [Gn 8:3](https://ref.ly/Gen8:3) (J) *and he waters retired continually more and more;* וַיִסַּע אַבְּרָם הָלוֺךְ וְנָסוֺעַ הַנֶּ֫גְבָּה [Gn 12:9](https://ref.ly/Gen12:9) (J) *and Abram journeyed on continually further toward the south country.* (5\) quite by itself is [Gn 8:5](https://ref.ly/Gen8:5) (P) וְהַמַּיִם הָיוּ הָלוֺךְ וְחָסוֺר *and he waters, they continually diminished* (were a going and a diminishin; the Infs. abs. being predicate). (6\) 13 t. the Inf. abs. \= Imv. \& is followed by Pf. consec. (chiefly in Je): הָלוֺךְ וְדִבַּרְתָּ֫ [2 S 24:12](https://ref.ly/2Sam24:12) *go and speak,* so [Je 35:2](https://ref.ly/Jer35:2); וְאָמַרְתָּ֫ ´ה *go and say,* [Is 38:5](https://ref.ly/Isa38:5), [Je 28:13](https://ref.ly/Jer28:13), [34:2](https://ref.ly/Jer34:2), [35:13](https://ref.ly/Jer35:13), [39:16](https://ref.ly/Jer39:16); וְקָרָאתָ ´ה [Je 2:2](https://ref.ly/Jer2:2), [3:12](https://ref.ly/Jer3:12); also וְרָחַחְםתָּ֫ ´ה [2 K 5:10](https://ref.ly/2Kgs5:10) *go and wash,* וְקָנִיתָ´ה [Je 13:1](https://ref.ly/Jer13:1), [19:1](https://ref.ly/Jer19:1) *go and buy,* וְעָמַדְתָּ֫ ´ה [17:19](https://ref.ly/Jer17:19) *go and stand.* **d.** akin to the use of Inf. abs. are some instances of Pt. הֹלֵךְ \+ adj. (or pt.): וַיֵּלֶךְ הַפְּלִשְׁתִּי הֹלֵךְ וְקָרֵב [1 S 17:41](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:41) *and the Philistine came continually nearer* (cf. [2 S 18:25](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:25) supr.); in other cases as predicate, with same idea of *growing, increasing:* וְדָוִד הֹלֵךְ וְחָזֵק וּבֵית שָׁאוּל הֹלְכִים וְדַלִּים [2 S 3:1](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:1) *and David was growing stronger and stronger, while the house of Saul were growing weaker and weaker,* cf. [2 Ch 17:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr17:12), [Est 9:4](https://ref.ly/Esth9:4) (of individuals), [2 S 15:12](https://ref.ly/2Sam15:12) (of people); \+ 2 adj. וְהַנַּעַד שְׁמוּאֵל הֹלֵךְ וְגָדֵל וָטוֺב [1 S 2:26](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:26); cf. (with different order) of wind, [Ec 1:6](https://ref.ly/Eccl1:6) *goeth* (הוֺלֵךְ)*toward* the south, and turneth about (סוֺבֵב) unto the north, *the wind turneth about and about continually* (סוֺבֵב סֹבֵב הוֺלֵךְ הָרוּחַ); of sea הַיָּם הוֺלֵךְ וְסֹעֵר[Jon 1:11](https://ref.ly/Jonah1:11), [1:13](https://ref.ly/Jonah1:13) *the sea was growing more and more stormy;* also וַיְהִי קוֺל הַ שֹּׁפָר חוֺלֵח וְחָזֵק מְאֹד [Ex 19:19](https://ref.ly/Exod19:19) *and the sound of the trumpet kept growing louder and louder;* \& in metaph. אֹרַח צַדִּיקִים כְּאוֺר נָֹגַהּ הוֺלֵךְ וָאוֺר עַד־נְכוֺן הַיּוֺם [Pr 4:18](https://ref.ly/Prov4:18) *the pth of just men is like a shining light, growing lighter and lighter until the full day.* **5\.** In combination with other verbal forms: **a.** sq. Inf. of purpose (c. לְ) [Gn 25:22](https://ref.ly/Gen25:22), [31:19](https://ref.ly/Gen31:19), [37:25](https://ref.ly/Gen37:25) (all JE) [Nu 14:38](https://ref.ly/Num14:38) (P) [24:1](https://ref.ly/Num24:1) (JE), Ju [8:1](https://ref.ly/Judg8:1), [9:8](https://ref.ly/Judg9:8), [9:9](https://ref.ly/Judg9:9), [9:11](https://ref.ly/Judg9:11), [9:13](https://ref.ly/Judg9:13) \+ oft.; esp. לִקְרַאת ´הּ *go to meet* [Jos 9:11](https://ref.ly/Josh9:11) (JE), [2 S 19:16](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:16), [1 K 18:16](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:16), [18:16](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:16) \+; sometimes with hostile sense [1 S 23:28](https://ref.ly/1Sam23:28), [1 K 20:27](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:27), [2 K 23:29](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:29); also לָּשּׁובּ ´הּ *go to return,* [Ex 4:21](https://ref.ly/Exod4:21) (RV *go back*), cf. also שּׁובּ לָּלֶּכֶּתּ *return again* [Ec 1:7](https://ref.ly/Eccl1:7), [5:14](https://ref.ly/Eccl5:14); לָּבּ־וֺ ´הּ *go to come* (*enter* etc.) [Je 41:17](https://ref.ly/Jer41:17) cf. [2 Ch 26:8](https://ref.ly/2Chr26:8). **b.** sq. finite vb. *go and do so* and so: [Gn 22:13](https://ref.ly/Gen22:13), [27:14](https://ref.ly/Gen27:14), [Ex 2:8](https://ref.ly/Exod2:8), [4:27](https://ref.ly/Exod4:27), [4:29](https://ref.ly/Exod4:29) (all JE), [Dt 13:7](https://ref.ly/Deut13:7), [13:14](https://ref.ly/Deut13:14), [17:3](https://ref.ly/Deut17:3), [24:2](https://ref.ly/Deut24:2), [Is 2:3](https://ref.ly/Isa2:3) \= [Mi 4:2](https://ref.ly/Mic4:2), [Je 11:12](https://ref.ly/Jer11:12) \+ oft.; וַיֵּלֶךְ וַּיָשָּׁבּ [Ex 4:18](https://ref.ly/Exod4:18) (E), יֵלֵךְ וְיָשֹׁב [Dt 20:5](https://ref.ly/Deut20:5), [20:6](https://ref.ly/Deut20:6), [20:7](https://ref.ly/Deut20:7), [20:8](https://ref.ly/Deut20:8), no fix (where is there a J or j in it?) Ju [21:23](https://ref.ly/Judg21:23) cf. [Ho 2:9](https://ref.ly/Hos2:9), [5:15](https://ref.ly/Hos5:15); וַיָּבוֺא ´ה *went \& came* [Nu 13:26](https://ref.ly/Num13:26) (P), [Ru 2:3](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:3), [1 S 22:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam22:5), [2 S 11:22](https://ref.ly/2Sam11:22), [2 K 4:25](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:25); notably וַיֵּלֶךְ וַיִּשְׁלַךְ [2 K 3:7](https://ref.ly/2Kgs3:7) *and he went and sent.* **c.** following other verbs: וַיָּקֻמוּ וַיֵּלְכוּ [Gn 22:19](https://ref.ly/Gen22:19) (E) cf. [24:10](https://ref.ly/Gen24:10) (J) [43:8](https://ref.ly/Gen43:8) (J) [Nu 16:25](https://ref.ly/Num16:25) (JE) Ju [4:9](https://ref.ly/Judg4:9); נִדְעָה סְעָה וְנֵלֵָכָה [Gn 33:12](https://ref.ly/Gen33:12) (J). **d.** esp. as result of action expr. in preced. vb., such as vb. of sending, etc.: וַיְשַׁלַּח אֶת־אֶחָיו וַיֵּלֵָכוּ [Gn 45:24](https://ref.ly/Gen45:24) (E) cf. [Jos 1:16](https://ref.ly/Josh1:16) (D) [1 S 6:8](https://ref.ly/1Sam6:8), [Jb 38:35](https://ref.ly/Job38:35); also יִששּׁאֵהוּ קָדִים וְיֵלַָךְ [27:21](https://ref.ly/Job27:21) *an east wind shall carry him off, and he shall depart;* וַתְ גָֽרֲושׁהוּ וַיֵּלַָךְ [Psalm 34:1](https://ref.ly/Ps34:1) (title). (Cf. *depart* also of inanimate things **3\.** supr.) **e.** in longer series: וַיֵּלֶךְ וִיִּבְרַח וַיִּמָּלֵט [1 S 19:12](https://ref.ly/1Sam19:12); וַיֵּשֶׁב…וַיִּסַּע וַיֵּלֶךְ וַיָשָׁב [2 K 19:36](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:36) \= [Is 37:37](https://ref.ly/Isa37:37); cf. [Nu 24:25](https://ref.ly/Num24:25) (JE); וַיָּקָם וַיֵּלֶךְ וַיָּבאֹ Ju [19:10](https://ref.ly/Judg19:10); also וַיָּקָם וַיָּבאֹ וַיֵּלֶךְ [2 K 10:12](https://ref.ly/2Kgs10:12). **f.** oft; in Imv. foll by 2nd Imv. or equiv., (1\) לֵךְ קַח־לִי [Gn 27:13](https://ref.ly/Gen27:13) (J) *go, bring me* (them), cf. [Ho 1:2](https://ref.ly/Hos1:2); לְכוּ רְעוּ [Gn 29:7](https://ref.ly/Gen29:7) (J) *go, feed* (them), cf. [37:14](https://ref.ly/Gen37:14), [42:19](https://ref.ly/Gen42:19), [Ex 4:19](https://ref.ly/Exod4:19) \+ (in all c. 57 t.; only JED in Hex); but also (2\) weakened to mere introductory word (as also supr. passim: esp. [2 K 3:7](https://ref.ly/2Kgs3:7) etc.), *go to, or come, do (let us do)* so \& so; לְכָה נִכְרְתָה בְרִית [Gn 31:44](https://ref.ly/Gen31:44) *come, let us make a compact,* cf. נַשְׁקֶה לְכָה [19:32](https://ref.ly/Gen19:32) (where subj. fem.); לְכוּ וְנֵלְכָה [1 S 9:9](https://ref.ly/1Sam9:9) *come, and let us go,* so v:[10](https://ref.ly/1Sam9:10), [11:14](https://ref.ly/1Sam11:14), [Is 2:3](https://ref.ly/Isa2:3) \= [Mi 4:2](https://ref.ly/Mic4:2), cf. [Zc 6:7](https://ref.ly/Zech6:7) \+; even לֵךְ בּאֹ וְאֶשְׁלְחָה [2 K 5:5](https://ref.ly/2Kgs5:5) *go to, come, and let me send,* cf. [Is 22:15](https://ref.ly/Isa22:15), [26:20](https://ref.ly/Isa26:20), Ez [3:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek3:4), [3:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek3:11); further Ju [19:11](https://ref.ly/Judg19:11), [19:13](https://ref.ly/Judg19:13), [1 S 9:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam9:5), [1 K 1:12](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:12), [Psalm 34:5](https://ref.ly/Ps34:5) \+ (in all c. 49 t.; only JE in Hex). (3\) apparently intermediate, and shewing transition fr. (1\) to (2\) are: לָח־רֵד [Ex 19:24](https://ref.ly/Exod19:24), [32:7](https://ref.ly/Exod32:7) cf. Ju [18:2](https://ref.ly/Judg18:2); לְכוּ סֻרוּ רְדוּ [1 S 15:6](https://ref.ly/1Sam15:6); לֵךְ עֲלֵה מִוֶּךְ [Ex 33:1](https://ref.ly/Exod33:1); לְכוּ וְהִתְהלְּכוּ בָּאָרֶץ [Jos 18:8](https://ref.ly/Josh18:8) \& לְכוּ בּאֹוּ אַרְצָה [Gn 47:17](https://ref.ly/Gen47:17) cf. [1 S 22:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam22:5), [1 K 1:13](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:13), [2 K 7:9](https://ref.ly/2Kgs7:9); v. further לֵכְנָה שֹׁבְנָה [Ru 1:8](https://ref.ly/Ruth1:8) \& שֹׁבְנָה לֵכְןָ v:[12](https://ref.ly/Ruth1:12); also [2 S 3:16](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:16), [1 K 19:15](https://ref.ly/1Kgs19:15), [19:20](https://ref.ly/1Kgs19:20) \+ (in all c. 36 t.) II. Fig.; the most common uses follow; in most the origin in a literal meaning is evident: † **1\.** *pass away, die;* in phrases denoting or implying death (cf. Ar. هَلَكَ *perish*); וְהִנֵּה אָנֹכִּי הוֺלֵךְ הַיּוֺם בְּדֶרֶךְ בָּל־הָאָרֶץ [Jos 23:14](https://ref.ly/Josh23:14) (D) *lo, I am going to\-day the way of all the eath* cf. [1 K 2:2](https://ref.ly/1Kgs2:2); perh. also [Gn 15:2](https://ref.ly/Gen15:2) (JE) *I pass away childless,* but possibly sub **2** infr.; also [2 S 12:23](https://ref.ly/2Sam12:23) *I am going* (אֲנִי הֹלֵךְ) *to him* [2 S 12:23](https://ref.ly/2Sam12:23); so of mankind דּוֺרהֹלֵךְ וְרוֺר בָּא [Ec 1:4](https://ref.ly/Eccl1:4); more explicitly, הַּכֹּל הוֺלֵךְ אֶל־מָקוֺםָ אֶחָד הַכֹּל הָיֶה מִן הֶעָפָר וְהַכֹּל שָׁב אֶל־הֶעָפָר [Ec 3:20](https://ref.ly/Eccl3:20) cf. [6:6](https://ref.ly/Eccl6:6); also בִּשְׁאוֺל אֲשֶׁר אַתָּה הֹלֵךְ שָׁמָּה [9:10](https://ref.ly/Eccl9:10), \& הֹלֵךְ הָאָדָם אֶל־בֵּית עוֺלָמוֺ [12:5](https://ref.ly/Eccl12:5); cf. בְּמֶרֶם אֵלֵךְ וְאֵינֶנִּי [Psalm 39:14](https://ref.ly/Ps39:14) *before I depart and be not;* so acc. to some [2 Ch 21:20](https://ref.ly/2Chr21:20) but cf. infr. **3 a** (2\); once with weaker sense sq. inf., חִנֵּה אָנֹכִי הוֺלֵךְ לָמוּת [Gn 25:32](https://ref.ly/Gen25:32) (J). **2\.** *live (‘walk’)*, in general (cf. **Pi. Hithp.**) (usu. poet., with modifying words): כִּי־אֵלֵךְ בְּנֵיא צַלְמָוֶת [Psalm 23:4](https://ref.ly/Ps23:4) *though I walk in a valley of deep darkness;* cf. [138:7](https://ref.ly/Ps138:7) \& אֵ֫לֶחהשֶׁךְ [Jb 29:8](https://ref.ly/Job29:8); קֹדֵר אֵלֵךְ[Psalm 42:10](https://ref.ly/Ps42:10); חֲשֵׁבִים´ה [Is 50:10](https://ref.ly/Isa50:10), v. also Ez [3:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek3:14); perh. also [Gn 15:2](https://ref.ly/Gen15:2) cf. **1** supr.; יֵלְכוּ מֵחַיִל אֶל־חָיִל [Psalm 84:8](https://ref.ly/Ps84:8) *they go from strength to strength;* הוֺלֵךְ בַּתֹּם יֵלָךְ בֶּטַח [Pr 10:9](https://ref.ly/Prov10:9); esp. **3\.** of moral and religious life; יְיַשֶּׁר־לָכֶת [Pr 15:21](https://ref.ly/Prov15:21) *he maketh straight to walk* (waleth straight, upright); partic. **a.** *walk in* (בְּ) *ways* (counsels, statutes, etc.), (1\) in good sense: *(a)* בְּרֶרֶךְ תָּמִים ´ה [Psalm 101:6](https://ref.ly/Ps101:6), cf. [Is 30:21](https://ref.ly/Isa30:21), [Je 6:16](https://ref.ly/Jer6:16), [1 K 8:36](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:36) \= [2 Ch 6:27](https://ref.ly/2Chr6:27); (β) in (בְּ) ways of ´י esp. D: [Dt 8:6](https://ref.ly/Deut8:6) (‖לְיִרְאָה אֹתוֺ) cf. [10:12](https://ref.ly/Deut10:12), [11:22](https://ref.ly/Deut11:22) (‖´לְאַהֲבָה אֶת־י etc.), [28:9](https://ref.ly/Deut28:9) (‖´שָׁמַרמִחְוֺתי); v. also [13:15](https://ref.ly/Deut13:15) \+; [1 K 11:33](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:33) (sq. לַעֲשוֺת), v:[38](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:38) (sq. וְעָשִיתָ) [2 K 21:22](https://ref.ly/2Kgs21:22), [Psalm 119:3](https://ref.ly/Ps119:3), [128:1](https://ref.ly/Ps128:1); בְּאֹרְחֹתָיו [Is 2:3](https://ref.ly/Isa2:3) \= [Mi 4:2](https://ref.ly/Mic4:2); further (sq. דֶּרֶךְ) Ju [2:17](https://ref.ly/Judg2:17); (γ) † in commandments of ´י [2 Ch 17:4](https://ref.ly/2Chr17:4); (δ) in his law)s) (תּוֺרָה) [Ex 16:4](https://ref.ly/Exod16:4), [16:14](https://ref.ly/Exod16:14) (J) [2 K 10:31](https://ref.ly/2Kgs10:31), [Je 9:12](https://ref.ly/Jer9:12), [44:23](https://ref.ly/Jer44:23) (\+ בְּתֻקֹּת \& בְּעֵדְוֺת), [2 Ch 6:16](https://ref.ly/2Chr6:16), [Dn 9:10](https://ref.ly/Dan9:10), cf. [Ne 10:30](https://ref.ly/Neh10:30) (of אֱלֽהִים); (ε) † in his ordinances, statutes (חֻקּוֺת) [Lv 26:3](https://ref.ly/Lev26:3), [1 K 6:12](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:12), [Je 44:10](https://ref.ly/Jer44:10), [44:23](https://ref.ly/Jer44:23) (v. supr.), Ez [5:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek5:6), [5:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek5:7), [11:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek11:20), [18:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek18:9), [18:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek18:17), [20:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:13), [20:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:16), [20:19](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:19), [20:21](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:21), cf. בְּחֻקּוֺת חַיִּים [33:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek33:15); also בְּחֻקֵּי [1 K 8:61](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:61), Ez [11:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek11:12), [36:27](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:27); (ζ) † in his judgments (מִשְׁפָּטִים) [Psalm 89:31](https://ref.ly/Ps89:31), Ez [37:24](https://ref.ly/Ezek37:24); (η) ordinances of David [1 K 3:3](https://ref.ly/1Kgs3:3), cf. also infr.; (φ) in way(s) of good man [1 S 8:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam8:3), [8:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam8:5), [2 Ch 21:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr21:12), of David [2 Ch 11:17](https://ref.ly/2Chr11:17), [34:2](https://ref.ly/2Chr34:2), of David \& Sol. 17:3\. (2\) in bad sense, (α) in (evil) ways c. בְּ [2 K 21:21](https://ref.ly/2Kgs21:21), Ez [16:47](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:47), [23:31](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:31), [2 Ch 22:3](https://ref.ly/2Chr22:3); cf. also (prob.) [2 Ch 21:20](https://ref.ly/2Chr21:20) וַיֵּלֶךְ בְּלאֹ חֶמְדָּה *and he lived undesirably* (cf. LXX Vulgate Kmp Öt); \> others *he departed* (died) *unregretted,* so AV RV; (β) sq. acc. [2 K 21:21](https://ref.ly/2Kgs21:21), [Je 18:15](https://ref.ly/Jer18:15) (נְתִיבוֺת ´ה); (γ) c. adverb. acc. הּוֺלֵךְ עִקְּשׁוּת פֶּה [Pr 6:12](https://ref.ly/Prov6:12) *one walking in perversity* (falseness) *of mouth* (cf. צְדָקוֺת [Is 33:15](https://ref.ly/Isa33:15)); (δ) in (בְּ)ordinances, statutes (חֻקּוֺת) of bad men [Lv 18:3](https://ref.ly/Lev18:3), [20:23](https://ref.ly/Lev20:23) (both H) [2 K 17:8](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:8), [17:19](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:19), Ez [20:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:18) (בְּחוּקֵּי). **b.** *walk with* (אֶת, עִם) i.e. associate, be familiar with: בְּשָׁלוֺם אִתִּי ´וּבְמִישׁוֺר ה [Mal 2:6](https://ref.ly/Mal2:6) *in peace and uprightness he walked with me* (´י); הַצְנֵעַ לֶכֶת עִס־אֱלֽהֶיךָ [Mi 6:8](https://ref.ly/Mic6:8); but also of people at variance with ´י וַהֲלְכְתֶּם עִמִּי קֶָרִי [Lv 26:23](https://ref.ly/Lev26:23) cf. v:[27](https://ref.ly/Lev26:27), v:[40](https://ref.ly/Lev26:40); \& of ´י at variance with people עִמָּכֶם בְּקֶָרִי…וְהָלַכְתִּי v:[24](https://ref.ly/Lev26:24) cf. v:[28](https://ref.ly/Lev26:28) (all H); with men, אֶת־הֲּכָמִים[13:20](https://ref.ly/Lev13:20); עִם אַנְשֵׁי רֶשַׁע [Jb 34:8](https://ref.ly/Job34:8); cf. *walk with* (עִם) *vanity* [Jb 31:5](https://ref.ly/Job31:5). **c.** *walk before* ´י (´לִפְנֵי י; c. בֶּאֱמֶת, בְּכָל־לִֹבָּם etc.) [1 K 2:4](https://ref.ly/1Kgs2:4), [3:6](https://ref.ly/1Kgs3:6), [8:23](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:23), [8:25](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:25), [9:4](https://ref.ly/1Kgs9:4), [2 Ch 6:16](https://ref.ly/2Chr6:16), [7:17](https://ref.ly/2Chr7:17) cf. ´קְדֹרַנִּית מִפְּנֵי י´ה [Mal 3:14](https://ref.ly/Mal3:14); *walk before the living* (i.e. walk *aright* before them) [Ec 6:8](https://ref.ly/Eccl6:8). **d.** *walk, go, after* (אַהֲּדֵי) (1\) ´י [Dt 13:5](https://ref.ly/Deut13:5), [1 K 14:8](https://ref.ly/1Kgs14:8), [Je 2:2](https://ref.ly/Jer2:2), [Ho 11:10](https://ref.ly/Hos11:10) \+; (2\) other gods, Ju [2:12](https://ref.ly/Judg2:12) esp. [84:12](https://ref.ly/Ps84:12), [Pr 10:9](https://ref.ly/Prov10:9), v. also תָּמִים ´ה [Psalm 15:2](https://ref.ly/Ps15:2), הֹלְכֵי תֹס [Pr 2:7](https://ref.ly/Prov2:7); (2\) bad sense, in (בְּ) sin [1 K 15:3](https://ref.ly/1Kgs15:3), [15:26](https://ref.ly/1Kgs15:26), [15:34](https://ref.ly/1Kgs15:34), [16:2](https://ref.ly/1Kgs16:2), [16:26](https://ref.ly/1Kgs16:26), [2 K 13:6](https://ref.ly/2Kgs13:6), [13:11](https://ref.ly/2Kgs13:11) \+ (esp. of wicked kings); (evil) counsel [2 Ch 22:5](https://ref.ly/2Chr22:5) cf. [Psalm 1:1](https://ref.ly/Ps1:1). **f.** (1\) לֵב as subj., *(a) \= thoughts,* לאֹ לִבִּי הָלַךְ [2 K 5:26](https://ref.ly/2Kgs5:26) *did not my thoughts go forth;* (β) *evil inclination,* [Jb 31:7](https://ref.ly/Job31:7) לִבָּם הֹלֵךְ…אֶל־לֵב שִׁקּוּצֵיהֶם Ez [11:21](https://ref.ly/Ezek11:21); opp. קָרָא); *go into,* הָֽלְכוּ בַכְּ לִמָּה [Is 45:16](https://ref.ly/Isa45:16)  *they have gone into confusion* (judgment on idol\-makers); etc. † **Niph.** *Pf.* 1 s. כְּצֵל כִּנְטוֺתוֺ נֶהֱלָכְּ תִּי [Psalm 109:23](https://ref.ly/Ps109:23) *l1 Ke a shadow when it stretches out I am gone* (cf. היה **Niph.**) † **Pi.** (chiefly poet. \& late) *Pf.* הִלַּכְּתִּי [Jb 30:28](https://ref.ly/Job30:28), [Psalm 131:1](https://ref.ly/Ps131:1); הִלָּכְּתִּי [Psalm 38:7](https://ref.ly/Ps38:7); 3 pl. הִלְּכוּ [Jb 24:10](https://ref.ly/Job24:10), [La 5:18](https://ref.ly/Lam5:18); *Impf.* יְהַלֵךְ Ez [18:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek18:9) \+ 2 t.; וַיְהַלֵּךְ [1 K 21:27](https://ref.ly/1Kgs21:27); *walk, tread on coals* עַל־הַגֶּחָלִים [Pr 6:28](https://ref.ly/Prov6:28); of idols [Psalm 115:7](https://ref.ly/Ps115:7) *they do not walk,* have not the power of walking (cf. יִצְעָדוּ [Je 10:5](https://ref.ly/Jer10:5)); of one *tramping,* formidable, sim. of poverty וּבָא־כִמְהַלֵּךְ רֵאשֶָׁךָ [Pr 6:11](https://ref.ly/Prov6:11), —a highwayman acc. to Ges Ew Hi Now, cf. LXX; ‖מַחְסֹרְךָ כְּ אִישׁ מָנֵן cf. also Hithp. [24:34](https://ref.ly/Prov24:34)); of ´י *walking* (in majesty), *marching,* לְַ־כַּנְפֵי רוּחַ [Psalm 104:3](https://ref.ly/Ps104:3) *on the wings of the wind;* of righteousness, personified, צֶדֶק לְפָנָיו יְהַלֵךְ [Psalm 85:14](https://ref.ly/Ps85:14); of wisdom בְּאֹרַה צְדָקָה אֲהַלֵךְ [Pr 8:20](https://ref.ly/Prov8:20) *in the path of righteousness do I walk* (stedfastly, unswervingly); of animals, *go about, prowl,* עַל הַר־צִיּוֺן שֶׁח֯שָּׁמֵם שׁוּעָלִים הִלְּכוּ בָוֺ (Qr שֶּׁשָּׁמֵם) [La 5:18](https://ref.ly/Lam5:18), of ships, in (stately) movement [Psalm 104:26](https://ref.ly/Ps104:26); of springs [104:10](https://ref.ly/Ps104:10) *between mountains they go (run, flow); of arrows* Hb [3:11](https://ref.ly/Hab3:11)  *at the light of thine arrows* that *go (fly, shoot;* cf. **Hithp.** [Psalm 77:18](https://ref.ly/Ps77:18)). **2\.** also of *walking about* \= living הַמְַהַלְּבִים תַּחַת הַשַָּׁמֶשׁ [Ec 4:15](https://ref.ly/Eccl4:15); עָרוֺם הִלְּכוּ בְּלִי לְבוּשׁ [Jb 24:10](https://ref.ly/Job24:10) *nacked they keep going about* (freq.), *without clothing;* so of going about as mourner קֹדֵר הִלַּכְּתִּי [Jb 30:28](https://ref.ly/Job30:28), [Psalm 38:7](https://ref.ly/Ps38:7) (cf. **Qal** [Psalm 42:10](https://ref.ly/Ps42:10)); בַּאֲפֵלוֺת נְהַלֵךְ [Is 59:9](https://ref.ly/Isa59:9) *in darkness we walk* (of distress); on the other hand בְּאוֺר פָּנֶיךָ יְהַלֵּכָוּן [Psalm 89:16](https://ref.ly/Ps89:16) (of blessedness \& prosperity). **3\.** *depart, go entirely away* (מִן) מִמְּקוֺם קָדוֺשׁ יְהַלֵָּכוּ [Ec 8:10](https://ref.ly/Eccl8:10) *from the holy place they must depart* (on text cf. VB; ‖וְיִשְׁתַּכְּחוּ בָעִיר). **4\.** fig. of mode of life, action, etc.: of particular path of one's life בְּאֹרַצ־זוּאֲהַלֵךְ [Psalm 142:4](https://ref.ly/Ps142:4); specif. in good sense וַיְהַלֵּךְ אַָט [1 K 21:27](https://ref.ly/1Kgs21:27) *and he* (Ahad) *walked softly* (as a penitent); oftener c. בְּ of path in which: בִּדְרָכַי יְהַלֵָּכוּ [Psalm 81:14](https://ref.ly/Ps81:14) *that in my ways they would walk !* cf. [86:11](https://ref.ly/Ps86:11) (בַּאֲמִתֶָּךָ), Ez [18:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek18:9) (בְּחֻקּוֺתַי); in bad or doubtful sensse וְהַלֵךְ בְּדַרְכֵי לִבְּךָ וּבְמַרְאֵי עֵינֶ\+יךָ [Ec 11:9](https://ref.ly/Eccl11:9); cf. also לאֹ הִלַּכְּתּי בִּגְדֹלֽת [Psalm 131:1](https://ref.ly/Ps131:1) *I do not walk (move, tread) among great things* (‖וּבְנִפְלָאוֺת מִמֶּנִּי; fig. of humility, cf. also va). † **Hithp.** *Pf.* הִתְהַלָּ־ח [Gn 6:9](https://ref.ly/Gen6:9), [1 S 30:31](https://ref.ly/1Sam30:31); וְהִתְהַלֵךְ consec.
