[Jb 1:7](https://ref.ly/Job1:7) \= [2:2](https://ref.ly/Job2:2) (both ‖שׁוּט) [Zc 1:10](https://ref.ly/Zech1:10), [1:11](https://ref.ly/Zech1:11); similarly also [1 Ch 21:4](https://ref.ly/1Chr21:4); of fiery appearance in Ezek.'s vision בֵּין הַחַיּוֺת ´מִתְה Ez [1:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:13); of horses in Zec.'s vision [Zc 6:7](https://ref.ly/Zech6:7) (בָּאָרֶץ ´לָלֶכֶת לְהִתְ), v:[7](https://ref.ly/Zech6:7) (´לְכוּ הִתְהַלְּכוּ בָּא), v:[7](https://ref.ly/Zech6:7); *go in different directions* אִישׁ לְשִׁבְטוֺ…מִשָּׁם בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל ´וַיִּתְה Ju [21:24](https://ref.ly/Judg21:24) (‖ וַיֵּצְאוּ מִשָּׁם etc.); so of lightning flashes as *arrows* of ´י [Psalm 77:18](https://ref.ly/Ps77:18) *went hither \& thither;* of water running off different ways [58:8](https://ref.ly/Ps58:8) (sim. of vanishing of wicked). **b.** *walk, walk about* הִתְהַלֵךְ בַּחוּץ עַל־מִשְׁעַנְתּוֺ [Ex 21:19](https://ref.ly/Exod21:19) (JE) i.e. leaning upon his staff; [Pr 6:22](https://ref.ly/Prov6:22) (distinguished fr. שָׁבַב, חֵקִיץ); [2 S 11:2](https://ref.ly/2Sam11:2) (עַל־גַנּ בֵּית הַמֶּלָךְ); וּבְחֵקֶר תְּהוֺם הִתְהֵלָּכְתָּ [Jb 38:16](https://ref.ly/Job38:16) *and in the* farthest *ground of* *the deep hast thou walked ?* בְּתוֺךְ אַבְנֵי־אֵשׁ הִתְהַלָּכְתָּ Ez [28:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek28:14); so also of ´י walking on earth, in anthropomorphic conception אֱלֽהִים מִתְהַלֵךְ ´קוֺל י בַּגָּן [Gn 3:8](https://ref.ly/Gen3:8) (J) *the sound of* ´י *Elohim walking about in the garden;* of God (*Ēl*) וְחוּג שָׁמַיִם מִתְהַלָּךְ [Jb 22:14](https://ref.ly/Job22:14) *and the vault of heaven he walketh; walk about in front of* (לִפְנֵי) [Est 2:11](https://ref.ly/Esth2:11); *attend, follow,* of retainers הַמִּתְהַלְּכִים בְּדַגְלֵי אֲרֹנִי [1 S 25:27](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:27); *wander,* of Israel מִגּוֺי אֶלאגּוֺי [Psalm 105:13](https://ref.ly/Ps105:13) \= [1 Ch 16:20](https://ref.ly/1Chr16:20); *rove about,* of David and his band אֲשֶׁר־הִתְהַלֶּח־שָׁם [1 S 30:31](https://ref.ly/1Sam30:31); וַיִּתְהַלְּכוּ בַּאֲשֶׁר יִתְּהָלָּכוּ [23:13](https://ref.ly/1Sam23:13) (on this phr. cf. LagPsalt. Hleron. 1874, 156 f. DrSm); *walk about, prowl,* of Jehoiachin under fig. of lion, ´נויִתְה בְּתוֺח־אֲרָיוֺת Ez [19:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek19:6); cf. also וּבָא־מִתְהַלֵךְ רֵישֶָׁךָ [Pr 24:34](https://ref.ly/Prov24:34) *and thy poverty cometh marching* (cf. **Pi.** [6:11](https://ref.ly/Prov6:11)); *glide, flow* of wine בְּמֵישָׁרִים ´יִתְה [Pr 23:31](https://ref.ly/Prov23:31) *glideth down smoothly* (VB; cf. **Qal 3** Ct [7:10](https://ref.ly/Song7:10)); note esp. the foll., where emphasis lies not on verb, but on an auxiliary word; these cases form the natural transition to the figurative use:—[Psalm 12:9](https://ref.ly/Ps12:9) *on every side wicked men are walking;* בְּצֶלֶם יִתְהָלֶּח־אִישׁ [39:7](https://ref.ly/Ps39:7) *in appearance (semblance,* lit. *image* cf. Checrit. n.) *walketh a man;* also לָמָּה קֹדֵר אֶתְהַלֵךְ [43:2](https://ref.ly/Ps43:2) (cf. **Qal** [42:10](https://ref.ly/Ps42:10), **Pi.** [38:7](https://ref.ly/Ps38:7), [Jb 30:28](https://ref.ly/Job30:28)). **2\.** fig. *walk about \= live;* the king before (לִפְּנֵי) his people [1 S 12:2](https://ref.ly/1Sam12:2), so of Samuel v:[2](https://ref.ly/1Sam12:2); of priest [2:35](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:35) (לִפְנֵי מְשִׁיחִי); of friendly intercourse with (אֶת) men [1 S 25:15](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:15); cf. בְּרֵעַ כְּאָח־לִי הִתְהַלָּכְתִּי [Psalm 35:14](https://ref.ly/Ps35:14); of man's relation to God: especially sq. לִפְנֵי, לְפָנָי וֶהְיֵהתָמִים ´ה [Gn 17:1](https://ref.ly/Gen17:1) (P) *walk before me* (live constantly in my presence) *and* (*so*) *be perfect,* cf. [24:40](https://ref.ly/Gen24:40) (J), [48:15](https://ref.ly/Gen48:15) (E); לְפָנֶיךָ בֶּאֱמֶת וּבְלֵבָכ שָׁלֵם ´ה [2 K 20:3](https://ref.ly/2Kgs20:3) \= [Is 38:3](https://ref.ly/Isa38:3); as token of divine favour יִתְהַלְּכוּ לְפָנַי עַד־עוֺלָם [1 S 2:30](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:30) (v. esp. Dr, on sense here \& elsewh.); [Psalm 116:9](https://ref.ly/Ps116:9) בְּאַרְצוֺת הַחַיִּים ´אֶתְּהַלֵּךְ לִפְנֵ י, [56:14](https://ref.ly/Ps56:13) (בְּאוֺר הַחַיִּים); בִּשְׁמוֺ ´יִתְה [Zc 10:12](https://ref.ly/Zech10:12) *they shall walk in his name;* c. אֶת, of Enoch and Noah אֶת־הָאֱלֽחִים ´ה [Gn 5:22](https://ref.ly/Gen5:22), [5:24](https://ref.ly/Gen5:24), [6:9](https://ref.ly/Gen6:9) (denoting intimacy, fellowship: all P); further בַּאֲמִתֶָּךָ ´ה [Psalm 26:3](https://ref.ly/Ps26:3) *I have walked in thy faithfulness;* בְּתֻמּוֺ ´מִתְה צַדִּיק [Pr 20:7](https://ref.ly/Prov20:7) *he that walketh justly in his* (own) *integrity;* בְּתָם־לְבָבִי בְּקֶרֶב בֵּיתִּי ´אֶתְה [Psalm 101:2](https://ref.ly/Ps101:2); cf. also [Psalm 119:45](https://ref.ly/Ps119:45) *and let me walk in a large place* (untrammelled, undismayed); of the wicked מִתְהַלֵּךְ בַּאֲשָׁמָיו [Psalm 68:22](https://ref.ly/Ps68:22) *one going on in his guilty deeds;* ´בַּחֲשֵׁכָה יִתְה [Psalm 82:5](https://ref.ly/Ps82:5) they *walk in darkness* (‖לאֹ יָֽדְעוּ וְלאֹ יָבִינוּ); of their exposure ´עַל־שְׁבָכָה יִתְה [Jb 18:8](https://ref.ly/Job18:8) *he walketh on net\-work,* (snare, cf. שבכה; ‖שֻׁלַּח בְּרֶשֶׁת בְּרַגְלָיו); esp. of ´י as accompanying Israel בְּקֶרֶב מַחֲנֶךָ ´מִתְח [Dt 23:15](https://ref.ly/Deut23:15) *walk in the midst,* etc., בְּתוֺכְכֶם [Lv 26:12](https://ref.ly/Lev26:12); [2 S 7:7](https://ref.ly/2Sam7:7) *wherever* (בְּכֹל אֲשֶׁר) *I have walked with (among* בְּ) *all (the sons of) Isr.* \= [1 Ch 17:6](https://ref.ly/1Chr17:6); as inhabiting a tent, on the march, וָּאֶהְיֶה מִתְהַלֵּךְ בְּאֹהֶל וּבְמִשְׁכָּן [2 S 7:6](https://ref.ly/2Sam7:6) *and I was walking in a tent and in a tabernacle*. † **Hiph.** *Pf.* 3 ms. הוֺלִיךְ (as if from ילך, i.e. a verb ו“פ, cf. reff. sub **Qal** *Impf.* supr.) [2 K 24:15](https://ref.ly/2Kgs24:15); sf. הוֺלִיכְךָ [Dt 8:2](https://ref.ly/Deut8:2), וְהוֺלִיכוֺ consec. [Pr 16:29](https://ref.ly/Prov16:29), etc.; *Impf.* יוֺלִיח [Ec 10:20](https://ref.ly/Eccl10:20), יוֺלִח [Je 32:5](https://ref.ly/Jer32:5); juss. יוֺלֵח [Dt 28:36](https://ref.ly/Deut28:36); שֶׁיֹּלֵח [Ec 5:14](https://ref.ly/Eccl5:14); וַיּוֺ֫לֶח [Ex 14:21](https://ref.ly/Exod14:21) וַיֹּ֫לֶח [2 K 6:19](https://ref.ly/2Kgs6:19) \+ 2 t.; וַיֹּל֭ח [La 3:2](https://ref.ly/Lam3:2); sf. וִיּוֺלִכֵּ נִי Ez [40:24](https://ref.ly/Ezek40:24), [43:1](https://ref.ly/Ezek43:1), [47:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:6) 1 s. אוֺלִיח [2 S 13:13](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:13), Ez [32:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek32:14), cohort. וְאוֺלִיכָה [2 K 6:19](https://ref.ly/2Kgs6:19), וָאוֺלֵח [Am 2:10](https://ref.ly/Amos2:10) \+ 3 t., etc.; *Imv.* הוֺלֵח [Nu 17:11](https://ref.ly/Num17:11); fs. הֵילִיכִי [Ex 2:9](https://ref.ly/Exod2:9) (Böii. 461, § 1103;; but rd. prob. הוֺלִיכִי O1:§ 256, b Ges§ 69, 2, R 8\); mpl. הֹלִיכוּ [2 K 17:27](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:27); *Inf.* sf. לְהֹלִיכוֺ [2 Ch 36:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr36:6); *Pt.* מוֺלִיח [Je 2:6](https://ref.ly/Jer2:6) \+ 4 t.; sf. מוֺלִיכְךָ [Dt 8:15](https://ref.ly/Deut8:15), מוֺלִכֵח [Je 2:17](https://ref.ly/Jer2:17); מוֺלִיכָם [Is 63:13](https://ref.ly/Isa63:13); fpl. מוֺלִכוֺת [Zc 5:10](https://ref.ly/Zech5:10); —**1\.** *lead, bring:* sq. acc. usually of person; **a.** c. בְּ of space or region through which, [Dt 8:15](https://ref.ly/Deut8:15) (Isr. in wilderness) [Je 2:6](https://ref.ly/Jer2:6), [Psalm 136:16](https://ref.ly/Ps136:16), so also [Is 48:21](https://ref.ly/Isa48:21); בַּדָּרֶח [Je 2:17](https://ref.ly/Jer2:17); cf. [Jos 24:3](https://ref.ly/Josh24:3) (E), [Is 63:13](https://ref.ly/Isa63:13), [Psalm 106:9](https://ref.ly/Ps106:9); also fig. [Is 42:16](https://ref.ly/Isa42:16) *and I will lead* (*the*) *blind in a way* (בְּדֶרֶח) *they know not;* **b.** c. acc. Pers., בְּ of space \+ acc. of time וָאוֺלֵח אֶתְכֶם אַרְבָּעִים שָׁנָה בַּמִּדְ בָּר [Dt 29:4](https://ref.ly/Deut29:4); so [Am 2:10](https://ref.ly/Amos2:10) (sq. Inf. of purpose c. לְ). **c.** sq. acc. Pers. \+ acc. of *way, road,* [Dt 8:2](https://ref.ly/Deut8:2), cf. דֶּרֶח הַדָּרוֺם Ez [40:24](https://ref.ly/Ezek40:24) (acc. of direction). **d.** sq. acc. Pers. \+ acc. of place or condition to which …אֶת־נְשֵׁי הַמֶּלֶךְ הוֺלִיךְ גּוֺלָה [2 K 24:15](https://ref.ly/2Kgs24:15) *the women of the king,* etc., *he led into exile* (מִירוּשָׁלְם בָבֶָלָה); so also, of exile וְהוֺלַכְּתִּיהָ הַמִּדְבָּר [Ho 2:16](https://ref.ly/Hos2:16); בָּבֶל [Je 32:5](https://ref.ly/Jer32:5); cf. of Yahwesh's chastisement, [La 3:2](https://ref.ly/Lam3:2) *me hath he driven and brought,* into *darkness and not* into *light;* sq. acc. Pers. \+ אל:אֶל־גוֺי etc. [Dt 28:36](https://ref.ly/Deut28:36) (of exile); אֶל־נַחֲלֵי־מַיִם [Je 31:9](https://ref.ly/Jer31:9); אֶל־הַשַּׁעַר Ez [43:1](https://ref.ly/Ezek43:1); אֶל־הָעֵדָה [Nu 17:11](https://ref.ly/Num17:11) (P; implic. obj. being censer); אֶל of Pers. [2 K 6:19](https://ref.ly/2Kgs6:19), [Je 52:26](https://ref.ly/Jer52:26), עַל in ‖ [2 K 25:20](https://ref.ly/2Kgs25:20); עַל also [1 K 1:38](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:38) וַיֹּלִכוּ אֹתוֺ עַל־גִּיחוֺן (see sub עַל **7 c** β). **e.** sq. acc. Pers. \+ ה—ָ loc.: שֹׁמְרֹ֫נָה [2 K 6:19](https://ref.ly/2Kgs6:19); בָּבֶּ֫לָה [2 Ch 33:11](https://ref.ly/2Chr33:11), [36:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr36:6) (cf. [2 K 24:15](https://ref.ly/2Kgs24:15) supr.); cf. שָׁ֫מָּה [2 K 17:27](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:27). **2\.** *lead away* sq. acc. Pers. \+ appos.; מוֺלִיח יוֺעֲצִים שׁוֺלָל [Jb 12:17](https://ref.ly/Job12:17) *he* (´י) *leadeth away counsellors despoiled* cf. v:[19](https://ref.ly/Job12:19); of Yahweh's judgment on the wicked [Psalm 125:5](https://ref.ly/Ps125:5) ´י *will lead them away with* (אֶת) *the workers of iniquity.* **3\.** *carry, bring:* c. obj. helpless, or inanimate: **a.** \+ acc. loc., Josiah to Jerusalem (יְרוּשָׁלַם) [2 Ch 35:24](https://ref.ly/2Chr35:24) (וַיְבִיאֻהוּ in ‖ [2 K 23:30](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:30)); cf. [Zc 5:10](https://ref.ly/Zech5:10) *whither are these carrying the ephah?* אָ֫נָה אוֺלִיח אֶת־חֶרְפָּתִי [2 S 13:13](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:13). **b.** *carry, take, take away,* חֵילִיכִי אֶת־הַיֶּלֶד [Ex 2:9](https://ref.ly/Exod2:9) (E; on form cf. supr.); also carry away בְּתָדוֺ, in his hand, [Ec 5:14](https://ref.ly/Eccl5:14); of the spread of a careless speech [Ec 10:20](https://ref.ly/Eccl10:20) *fowl of the heavens shall carry the voice* (‖בַּעַל ה֯כְּנָפַיִם יַגֵּיד דָּבָר). **4\.** fig., of influence on character: c. acc. Pers. \+ בְּ *into:* [Pr 16:29](https://ref.ly/Prov16:29) *and he will lead him into a way* that is *not good* (i.e. sinful). **5\. a.** *cause to walk, go,* men (i.e. עַמִּי יִשְרָאֵל) on (עַל) the mountains of Israel Ez [36:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:12) (‖וִירֵשׁוּךָ, וְהָיִיתָ לָהֶם לְנַחֲלָה); cf. וָאוֺלֵח אֶתְכֶם קוֺמֲמִיּוּת [Lv 26:13](https://ref.ly/Lev26:13) *and I caused you to walk upright* (fig.; opp. to being bowed down by a yoke); [Is 63:12](https://ref.ly/Isa63:12) *that caused his glorious arm to go at* (לְ) *the right hand of Moses.* **b.** *cause to flow, run* Ez [32:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek32:14) *and rivers like oil will I cause to flow.* **c.** *cause to depart, retire, go back,* ´י, the sea, [Ex 14:21](https://ref.ly/Exod14:21) (בְּ instr., בְּרוּחַ קָדִים). 

> \[Str1982] † הֵ֫לֶךְ **n.m.** traveller (properly *a going, journey,* whence *wayfarer, traveller*) only וַיָּבאֹ הֵלֶךְ לְאִישׁ הֶעָשִׁיר [2 S 12:4](https://ref.ly/2Sam12:4) (‖הָאֹרֵחַ הַבָּא לוֺ, הָאִישׁ הֵבָּא לוֺ);—in MT [1 S 14:26](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:26) הֵלֶךְ דְּבָשׁ, ´ה must be cstr. \& \= *flowing,* or *dropping* (*of honey*), but rd. rather הָלַח LXX The Klo We Dr (\& דְּבֹּרוֺ for דְּבַשׁ q.v., LXX We Dr).

> \[Str1978] † \[הָלִיךְ] **n.\[m.]** *step,* only pl. sf. בִּרְחֹץ הֲלִיכַי בְּחֵמָה [Jb 29:6](https://ref.ly/Job29:6) *when my steps were bathed in cream* (חֵמָה \= חֶמְאָה) heperb. of abundance wealth.

> \[Str1979] † \[הֲלִיכָה] **n.f. a going, way, travelling\-company,** sf. הֲלִיכָתָם [Na 2:6](https://ref.ly/Nah2:6) Qr (הלוכתם Kt); elsewh. only pl., cstr. הֲלִיכוֺת [Psalm 68:25](https://ref.ly/Ps68:25), so [Pr 31:27](https://ref.ly/Prov31:27) Qr (הילכות Kt), הֲלִכוֺת Hb [3:6](https://ref.ly/Hab3:6), הֲלִיכֹת [Jb 6:19](https://ref.ly/Job6:19); sf. הֲלִיכוֺתֶיךָ [Psalm 68:25](https://ref.ly/Ps68:25); —**1\.** *going, walk,* **a.** [Na 2:6](https://ref.ly/Nah2:6) *they shall stumble in their going;* elsewh. pl. *goings:* **b.** [Psalm 68:25](https://ref.ly/Ps68:25), [68:25](https://ref.ly/Ps68:25) of *going, marching, progress of God into the sanctuary* (בַּקֹּדֶשׁ; so Hup Pe Che; Ew Hi De *in holiness* ref. either to solemn processions of worshippers (e.g. De) or, perh. better, to the theophanic progress of God himself (e.g. Che); also **c.** of goings \= *doings* צוֺפִיָּה בֵּיתָהּ ´ה [Pr 31:27](https://ref.ly/Prov31:27) *watching the doings of her household*; of God Hb [3:6](https://ref.ly/Hab3:6) הֲלִכוֺת עוֺלָם לוֺ *ways of eternity* (al *of old*) *are his.* **2\.** *travelling\-company, caravan* הֲלִיכֹת שְׁבָא [Jb 6:19](https://ref.ly/Job6:19) (‖אָרְהוֺת תֵּמָא).

> \[Str4108–09] † \[מַהֲלָךְ] **n.m.**:[Ne 2:6](https://ref.ly/Neh2:6) *walk, journey, going* (late); cstr. מַהֲלַח Ez [42:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek42:4) \+ 2 t.; sf. מַהֲלָֽכְךָ [Ne 2:6](https://ref.ly/Neh2:6); pl. מַהְלְכִים [Zc 3:7](https://ref.ly/Zech3:7); —**1\.** *walk* ‘of 10 cubits’ breadth before the chambers' in Ezekiel's temple Ez [42:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek42:4) (cf. Da). **2\.** *journey* fr. Bab. to Jerusalem [Ne 2:6](https://ref.ly/Neh2:6); of dimension of Nineveh, *a journey of three days* [Jon 3:3](https://ref.ly/Jonah3:3) i.e. diameter or length, cf. v:[4](https://ref.ly/Jonah3:4); (acc. to Herodotv. 58 one day's journey \= 150 stadia, \= length of Nineveh acc. to Diodii. 3\). **3\.** pl. *goings,* i.e. *free access* (VB) [Zc 3:7](https://ref.ly/Zech3:7).

> \[Str8418] † \[תַּהֲלוּכָה] **n.f.** procession, only pl. תַּהֲלֻכֹת [Ne 12:31](https://ref.ly/Neh12:31) of thanksgiving\-processions at dedication of wall.

## I הָלַל (id: BDB02407)

\[Str1984] † I. \[הָלַל] **vb.** shine (acc. to Thes al.\= II. הלל (*splenduit,* from *sonuit acute, clare,* so Thes; these mngs., however, merely assumed); but v. LagOr. ii. 19 \& sub foll.; cf. Ar. هَلَّ *begin to shine,* هِلَالٌ *new moon;* As. *êllu, bright* COTGloss)—**Qal** only *Inf.* sf. בְּהִלּוֺ נֵרוֺ עֲלֵי ראֹשִׁי [Jb 29:3](https://ref.ly/Job29:3) *when it,* viz. *his lamp, shone upon my head* (cf. Ew§ 309 c), fig. of God's favour. **Hiph.** *Impf.* יָהֵל [Jb 31:26](https://ref.ly/Job31:26), 3 fs. תָּהֶל [Jb 41:10](https://ref.ly/Job41:10); 3 mpl. יָהֵ֫לּוּ [Is 13:10](https://ref.ly/Isa13:10); —*flash forth light,* of heavenly bodies, אוֺר \= *sun* [Jb 31:26](https://ref.ly/Job31:26) (‖יָרֵחַ יָקָר הֹלֵח); sq. acc. cogn. בּוֺכְבֵי הַשָּׁמַיִם וּכְּ סִילֵיהֶם אָֹ יָהֵלּוּ אוֺרָם [Is 13:10](https://ref.ly/Isa13:10) (‖חָשַׁח הַשֶּׁמֶשׁ, \& יָרֵחַ לאֹ־יַגִּיהּ אוֺרוֺ); also of crocodile עֲטִישֹׁתָיו תָּ֫הֶל אוֺר [Jb 41:10](https://ref.ly/Job41:10) *his sneezings flash forth light* (shining water\-drops).

> \[Str1966] † הֵילֵל **n.m.** appell. shining one, epith. of king of Babylon, בֶּן־שָׁחַר ´אֵיךְ נָפַלְתָּ מִשָּׁמַיִם ה [Is 14:12](https://ref.ly/Isa14:12) *how art thou fallen, shinning one, son of dawn !* i.e. star of the morning. (Cf. As. *muštilil,* epith. of (Venus a) morning\-star III R 57:60 OppJAS 1871, 448 SchrSK 1874, 337 COTad. loc.)

* **Associated Passages:** Job 29:3; Isaiah 13:10; Job 31:26; Isaiah 14:12; Job 41:10


## II הָלַל (id: BDB02409)

\[Str1984,430,3050] † II. \[הָלַל] **vb.** be boastful, **Pi. praise** (Ar. هَلَّ *shout*, both in joy \& (if conject. of Nö RS be right, v. cit. infr.) in terror; esp. II. in formula of worship; Syr. Pa. ܗܰܥܶܠ *cecinit, laudavit;* cf. NH הַלּוּל *praise,* הַלֵּל *id., Hallel* (Psalm 113–118); Aram. הִילּוּלָא *marriage\-song,* \& c.; on As. *alâlu, shout for joy, rejoice* v. LyonSargontexte 66 (Cylinder. 1\. 36\) also WincklerSargontexte 134, 1\. 194; 156\. 1\. 149 KB44. 1\. 36; 80\. 1\. 194; all variations of *cry aloud,* cf. NöZMG 1887, 723 RS Sem. 411; perh. orig. *break through,* or *out* (in a cry), cf. WeSkizzen iii. 107 ff., esp. 109; comp. also (acc. to PrätBAS i. 369 f.) Eth. ተማህለለ፡ *implore* (cf. Amhar. Tigr.), \& መሀለ፡ *swear*)—**Qal** *Impf.* 2 mpl. תָּהָֹלּוּ [Psalm 75:5](https://ref.ly/Ps75:5); *Pt.* הוֺלֲלִים [Psalm 5:6](https://ref.ly/Ps5:6) \+ 2 t.;—*be boastful* אַל־תָּהָֹלּוּ [Psalm 75:5](https://ref.ly/Ps75:5) (‖אַ־ל (תָּרִימוּקָרֶן); Pt. *boastful ones, boasters* [Psalm 5:6](https://ref.ly/Ps5:6) (‖פֹּעֲלֵי אָוֶן); [73:3](https://ref.ly/Ps73:3), [75:5](https://ref.ly/Ps75:5) (both ‖רְשָׁעִים). **Pi.** *Pf.* הִלֵּל [Psalm 10:3](https://ref.ly/Ps10:3), 1 s. sf. הִלַּלְתִּיךָ [Psalm 119:164](https://ref.ly/Ps119:164); 3 mpl. וְהִלֲלן consec. [Is 62:9](https://ref.ly/Isa62:9), sf. הִלֲלוּךָ [Is 64:10](https://ref.ly/Isa64:10). etc.; *Impf.* יְהַלֶּ־ל [Psalm 63:6](https://ref.ly/Ps63:6), [102:19](https://ref.ly/Ps102:19); sf. יְהַלֶּלְךָ [Pr 27:2](https://ref.ly/Prov27:2), יְהַלֲלֶָךָּ [Is 38:18](https://ref.ly/Isa38:18), וַיְהַלֲלָהּ [Pr 31:38](https://ref.ly/Prov31:38): 1 s. אֲהַלֵּל [Psalm 56:5](https://ref.ly/Ps56:5) \+ 2 t., cohort. אֲהַלֲלָה [Psalm 69:31](https://ref.ly/Ps69:31) \+ 2 t., etc.; *Imv.* fs. הַלֲלִי [Psalm 146:1](https://ref.ly/Ps146:1), [147:12](https://ref.ly/Ps147:12); mpl. הַלְלוּ [Psalm 104:35](https://ref.ly/Ps104:35) \+ 30 t. Psalm \+ [Je 20:13](https://ref.ly/Jer20:13), [31:7](https://ref.ly/Jer31:7); sf. הַלֲלוּ֫הוּ [Psalm 22:24](https://ref.ly/Ps22:24) \+ 15 t. Psalm; *Inf. abs.* הַלֵּל [1 Ch 16:36](https://ref.ly/1Chr16:36); *cstr.* הַלֵּל [2 S 14:25](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:25) \+ 16 t.Chr; *Pt.* pl. מְהַלֲלִים [1 Ch 23:5](https://ref.ly/1Chr23:5) \+ 4 t. Ch;—**1\.** *praise* man or woman, acc. וַיְהַלֲלוּ אֹתָהּ אֶל־פַּרְעֹה [Gn 12:15](https://ref.ly/Gen12:15) (J) *and they praised her to Pharaoh*, cf. [Pr 27:2](https://ref.ly/Prov27:2), [31:28](https://ref.ly/Prov31:28), [31:31](https://ref.ly/Prov31:31), Ct [6:9](https://ref.ly/Song6:9); וְהַמְהַלֲלִים אֶת־הַמֶּלָךְ [2 Ch 23:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr23:12) *and those praising* (shouting acclamations to) *the king*; v. also לְהַלֵּל מְאֹד [2 S 14:25](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:25) (of Absalom's beauty); in bas sense עֹזְבֵי תוֺרָה יְהַלֲלוּ רָשָׁע [Pr 28:4](https://ref.ly/Prov28:4) *deserters of law praise a wicked man*. **2\.** usually praise אֱלֽהִים, ´י etc.; **a.** sq. acc. of heathen god Ju [16:24](https://ref.ly/Judg16:24). **b.** obj. ´י(אֱלֽהִים ); [Psalm 119:164](https://ref.ly/Ps119:164) (עַל *on account of*), subj. heaven, earth, seas, etc., [Psalm 69:35](https://ref.ly/Ps69:35); תְּחִי נַפְשִׁי ותְהַלֲלֶךָּ [Psalm 119:175](https://ref.ly/Ps119:175); in summons to all creatures to praise [148:5](https://ref.ly/Ps148:5), [148:13](https://ref.ly/Ps148:13); [Is 38:18](https://ref.ly/Isa38:18) לאֹ שְׁאוֺל תּוֺדֶךָּ מָוֶת יְהַלֲלֶָךָּ (cf. [Psalm 115:17](https://ref.ly/Ps115:17)); often of Public worship in sanctuary, [Is 62:9](https://ref.ly/Isa62:9) (thanks giving in sanctuary after harvest), cf. [64:10](https://ref.ly/Isa64:10), [Psalm 22:23](https://ref.ly/Ps22:23) (‖אֲסַפְּרָה שִׁמְךָ), v:[27](https://ref.ly/Ps22:27), [35:18](https://ref.ly/Ps35:18), [109:30](https://ref.ly/Ps109:30) (both ‖אוֺדְךָ) [107:32](https://ref.ly/Ps107:32) (‖יְרוֺמֲמוּהוּ) [84:5](https://ref.ly/Ps84:5), v. also [146:2](https://ref.ly/Ps146:2) (‖´אֲוַמְּרָה לֵא) [149:3](https://ref.ly/Ps149:3) (c. בְּ instr.; ‖יְוַמְּרוּ לוֺ); also obj. ´שֵׁם י (in some cases of public worship) [Psalm 74:21](https://ref.ly/Ps74:21), [148:5](https://ref.ly/Ps148:5), [Jo 2:26](https://ref.ly/Joel2:26) (thanksgiving after harvest); שֵׁם־אֱלֽהִים בְּשִׁיר [Psalm 69:31](https://ref.ly/Ps69:31) (‖וִאֲגַדְּלֶנּוּ בְתוֺדָה), [145:2](https://ref.ly/Ps145:2) (‖אֲבָֽרֲכֶָךָּ); further, [Psalm 56:5](https://ref.ly/Ps56:5) בֵּאלֽהִים אֲהַלֵּל דְּבָרוֺ, also v:[11](https://ref.ly/Ps145:11) \+ v:[11b](https://ref.ly/Ps145:11) ´ביהוה וגו, but cf. LagOr.ii.16 ff., who limits this technical הלל to priests, using חֲצֹצְרוֺת, for a signal to the people; v. e.g. [Ne 12:24](https://ref.ly/Neh12:24) cf. v:[27](https://ref.ly/Neh12:27)), [1 Ch 16:4](https://ref.ly/1Chr16:4) (with instrumental music, cf. בִּכְלֵי כְבָלִים וּבְכִנֹּרוֺת \& בַּמְצִלְתַּיִ ם, all v:[5](https://ref.ly/1Chr16:5), and [Ne 12:27](https://ref.ly/Neh12:27)) [23:30](https://ref.ly/1Chr23:30), [25:3](https://ref.ly/1Chr25:3) (all ‖הוֺדוֺת); exercised (apparently) by both priests and Levites [Ezr 3:11](https://ref.ly/Ezra3:11) (‖הֹדוֺת) cf. v:[11](https://ref.ly/1Chr25:11); by Levites [2 Ch 20:19](https://ref.ly/2Chr20:19) (בְּקֹל גָּדוֺל), [29:30](https://ref.ly/2Chr29:30) (בְּדִבְרֵי דָוִיד) in which the people also joined [1 Ch 16:36](https://ref.ly/1Chr16:36); also [2 Ch 5:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr5:13) (בַּחֲצֹצְרוֺת וּבִמְצִלְתַּיִם וּבִכְלֵי הַשִּׁיר cf. also v:[12](https://ref.ly/2Chr5:12); ‖הֹדוֺת, v:[13](https://ref.ly/2Chr5:13) appar. of Levites \& priests), בַּכֵּלִים ´מְהַלֲלִים לי [1 Ch 23:5](https://ref.ly/1Chr23:5) (Levites) cf. [2 Ch 30:21](https://ref.ly/2Chr30:21) (Levites \& priests ´בִּכְלֵיעֹז לי); sq. לְשֵׁם תִּפְאַרְתֶָּךָ [1 Ch 29:13](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:13) (‖מוֺדִים; David speaks in name of people); sq. לְהַדְרַת־קָֹדֶשׁ [2 Ch 20:11](https://ref.ly/2Chr20:11) (before the army; ‖´מְשֹׁרֲרִים לי; prob. of Levites, cf. v:[19](https://ref.ly/2Chr20:19)). **f.** sq. acc. ´י [Ezr 3:10](https://ref.ly/Ezra3:10) (priests \& Levites: עַל־יְדֵי דָּוִיד), [Ne 5:13](https://ref.ly/Neh5:13) (people). **g.** other forms, with like technical sense, but abs.: לְהַלֵּל לְהוֺדוֺת בְּמִצְוַת דָּויד [Ne 12:24](https://ref.ly/Neh12:24) (Levites) cf. [1 Ch 23:5](https://ref.ly/1Chr23:5), [2 Ch 8:14](https://ref.ly/2Chr8:14), [29:30](https://ref.ly/2Chr29:30) (עַד־לְשִמְחָה) [2 Ch 31:2](https://ref.ly/2Chr31:2) (appar. priests \& Levites; ´בְּשַׁעֲדֵי מֵחֲנוֺת י; ‖לְשָׁרֵת וּלְהֹוֺדוֺת); v. also מוֺדִיעִים לְהַלֵּל [2 Ch 23:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr23:13) (‖מְשׁוֺרֲרִים בִּכְלַי הַשִּׁיר בְּהַלֵּל דָּוִיד בְּיָדָם ). [2 Ch 7:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr7:6) (‖הֹדוֺת ליהוּה) **3\.** appar. *boast, male one's boast* cf. **Qal 2** (sq. בְּ *in, of*), בֵּאלֽהִים [Psalm 44:9](https://ref.ly/Ps44:9) (‖שִׁמְךָ לְעוֺלָס נוֺדֶה); so acc. to most, in bad sense, רָשָׁע עַל־תַּאֲוַת נַפְשׁוֺ ´ה [Psalm 10:3](https://ref.ly/Ps10:3) *a wicked man boasteth of the desire of his soul*, but Che *praiseth* (´י) *for* (i.e. in a mercenary spirit). **Pu.** *Pf.* 3 pl. הוּלָּלוּ [Psalm 78:63](https://ref.ly/Ps78:63); *Impf.* יְהֻלַּ־ל [Pr 12:8](https://ref.ly/Prov12:8); *Pt.* מְהֻלָּל [2 S 22:4](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:4) \+ 6 t.; f. הַהֻלָּלָה Ez [26:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek26:17) (cf. (v. supr.) of city, *renowned* Ez [26:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek26:17). **2\.** of ´י only pt. \= gerundive, *to be praised, worthy* *of praise* [2 S 22:4](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:4) \= Psalm 181:4; מְאֹד ´וּמְה ´גָּדוֺל י [Psalm 48:2](https://ref.ly/Ps48:2), [96:4](https://ref.ly/Ps96:4) \= [1 Ch 16:25](https://ref.ly/1Chr16:25), [Psalm 145:3](https://ref.ly/Ps145:3); so ´שֵׁם י ´מְה [113:3](https://ref.ly/Ps113:3). **Hithpa.** *Impf.* יִתְהַלֵּל [1 K 20:11](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:11) \+ 5 t.; יִתְהַלָּל [Pr 20:14](https://ref.ly/Prov20:14); 3 fs. תִּתְּהַלֵּל [Psalm 34:3](https://ref.ly/Ps34:3), תִּתְּהַלָּל [Pr 31:30](https://ref.ly/Prov31:30); 2 ms. תִּתְהַלֵּל [Psalm 52:3](https://ref.ly/Ps52:3), [Pr 27:1](https://ref.ly/Prov27:1); תִּתְהַלָּל [Is 41:16](https://ref.ly/Isa41:16); 2 fs. תִּתְהַלֲלִי [Je 49:4](https://ref.ly/Jer49:4); 3 mpl. יִתְהַלֲלוּ [Psalm 64:11](https://ref.ly/Ps64:11), [Is 45:25](https://ref.ly/Isa45:25); יִתְהַלָּלוּ [49:7](https://ref.ly/Isa49:7), [Je 4:2](https://ref.ly/Jer4:2); *Imv.* mpl. הִתְהַלֲלוּ [1 Ch 16:10](https://ref.ly/1Chr16:10), [Psalm 105:3](https://ref.ly/Ps105:3); *Inf.* לְהִתְהַלֵּל [Psalm 106:5](https://ref.ly/Ps106:5); *Pt.* מִתְהַלֵּל [Pr 25:14](https://ref.ly/Prov25:14), [Je 9:23](https://ref.ly/Jer9:23); pl. מִתְהַלֲלִים [Psalm 97:7](https://ref.ly/Ps97:7); —*glory, boast, make one's boast:*—**1\.** of self\-confident boasting, abs. [1 K 20:11](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:11) cf. [Pr. 20:14](https://ref.ly/Prov20:14); sq. בְּ of ground of boast [Je 9:22](https://ref.ly/Jer9:22), [9:22](https://ref.ly/Jer9:22), [9:22](https://ref.ly/Jer9:22), [49:4](https://ref.ly/Jer49:4), [Psalm 49:7](https://ref.ly/Ps49:7), [52:3](https://ref.ly/Ps52:3), [Pr 25:14](https://ref.ly/Prov25:14), אַל־תִּתְהַלֵּל בְּיוֺם מָחָר [Pr 27:1](https://ref.ly/Prov27:1); of glorying in idols הַמִּתְהַלֲלִים בָּאֱלִלִים [Psalm 97:7](https://ref.ly/Ps97:7). **2\.** of glorying, making one's boast in (בְּ, on the ground of) ´י:תִּתְהַלֵּל ´בי נַפְשִׁי [Psalm 34:3](https://ref.ly/Ps34:3), cf. [Is 45:25](https://ref.ly/Isa45:25), [Je 4:2](https://ref.ly/Jer4:2) (‖וְהִתְבָּֽרְכוּ בוֺ); sq. בִּקְדוֺשׁ יִשְרָאֵל [Is 41:16](https://ref.ly/Isa41:16); v. also [Psalm 105:3](https://ref.ly/Ps105:3) \= [1 Ch 16:10](https://ref.ly/1Chr16:10) sq. בְּשֵׁם קָדְשׁוֺ (‖ ´יִשְמַה לֵב מְבֵקְשֵׁי י), cf. further [Je 9:23](https://ref.ly/Jer9:23); less oft. abs. [Je 9:23](https://ref.ly/Jer9:23), [Psalm 63:12](https://ref.ly/Ps63:12), [64:11](https://ref.ly/Ps64:10) \& [106:5](https://ref.ly/Ps106:5) sq. עִם־נַחֲלָתֶָךָ *together with thine heritage* (‖לִשְמֹהַ בְּשִמְהַת נּוֶֺ \+יךָ). **3\.** once, late \= pass.*be praised, commended*, of God\-fearing woman [Pr 31:30](https://ref.ly/Prov31:30). **Po‘el** *Impf.* יְהוֺלֵל [Is 44:25](https://ref.ly/Isa44:25) \+ 2 t.;—*make into a fool, make fool of*, obj. שֹׁפְטִים [Jb 12:17](https://ref.ly/Job12:17) (subj. ´י; ‖מוֺלִיךְ יוֺעֲצִים שׁוֺלָל); obj. קֹסְמִים [Is 44:25](https://ref.ly/Isa44:25) (subj. ´י ‖מֵפֵר אֹתוֺת בַּדִּים; obj. הָכָם [Ec 7:7](https://ref.ly/Eccl7:7) (subj. הָעשֶׁק; ‖וִיאַבֵּד אֶת־לֵב מַתָּנָה). **Po‘al** *Pt.* לִשְהוֺק אָמַרְתִּי מְהוֺלָל [Ec 2:2](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:2) *of laughter I said*, It is *mad* (folly); מְוֺלָלַי [Psalm 102:9](https://ref.ly/Ps102:9) *those mad against me* (‖אוֺיְבָי). **Hithpo.** *Impf.* ויִּתְהֹלֵל [1 S 21:14](https://ref.ly/1Sam21:14); 3 mpl. יִתְהֹלֲלוּ [Je 51:7](https://ref.ly/Jer51:7), יִתְהוֺלֲלוּ [Na 2:5](https://ref.ly/Nah2:5), יִתְהֹלֶָלוּ [Je 50:38](https://ref.ly/Jer50:38); *Imv.* mpl. הִתְהֹלֲלוּ [Je 49:9](https://ref.ly/Jer49:9), הִתְהֹלָלוּ [Je 25:16](https://ref.ly/Jer25:16); —*act madly*, or *like a madman*, וַיִּתְהֹלֵל בְּיָרָם [1 S 21:14](https://ref.ly/1Sam21:14) (of David) *and he acted like a madman in their hands;* of idolatrous worship by Chaldeans ´וּבָאֵימִים יִתְה [Je 50:38](https://ref.ly/Jer50:38); of nations, as drunken men, fig. of terror at Yahweh's judgements, [Je 25:16](https://ref.ly/Jer25:16) (‖הִתְגֹּעֲשׁוּ) [51:7](https://ref.ly/Jer51:7); also of madly driving \& jolting chariots [Na 2:5](https://ref.ly/Nah2:5), [Je 46:9](https://ref.ly/Jer46:9).

> \[Str1985] † הִלֵּל **n.pr.m.** (*he hath praised;* cf. NH n.pr. הִלֵּל, *Hillel*) father of a judge of Israel Ju [12:13](https://ref.ly/Judg12:13), [12:15](https://ref.ly/Jer12:15), called הַפִּרְעָתוֺנִי v. פִּרְעָתוֺן.

> \[Str1974] † \[הִלּוּל] **n.\[m.]** (NH *id.*) only pl. הִלּוּלִים rejoicing, praise: **1\.** וִיַּעֲשוּ הִלּוּלִים Ju [9:27](https://ref.ly/Judg9:27) i.e. a vintage\-rejoicing, merry\-making, connected with thanksgiving וַיָּבאֹוּ בֵּית אֱלֽהֵיהֶם etc. (i.e. god baal\-Berith, see v:[4](https://ref.ly/Judg9:4)). **2\.** of praise to ´י, ´פקדֶשׁ ה [Lv 19:24](https://ref.ly/Lev19:24) (H) *holiness of praise*, i.e. a consecrated thing in token of thanksgiving for fruit, offered in 4th year (cf. NH).

> \[Str1947] † \[הוֺלֵלָה] **n.f.** madness (on txt. v. infr.), only pl. הֹלֵלוֺת, הוֺלֵלוֺת, and only Ec: [Ec 1:17](https://ref.ly/Eccl1:17) *and I set my heart* (וָאֶתְּנָה לִבִּי) *to know wisdom, and to know madness and folly* (הֹלֵלוֺת וְשִכְלוּת), cf. הוֺלֵלוֺת [2:12](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:12) (‖ *id.*); ´וְלָדַעַת רֶשַׁע כֶּסֶל וְהַסִּכְלוּת ה [7:25](https://ref.ly/Eccl7:25); i.e. to know folly to be madness; possibly rd. חוֺלֵלוּת, cf. סִכְלוּת \& foll.; the moral evil of it is specif. recognized in [Ec 9:3](https://ref.ly/Eccl9:3) (‖רָע).

> \[Str1948] † הוֺלֵלוּת **n.f.** id. (formed unusually from the ptcp., an Aram. formation, cf. BaNB 414 f.) תְּהִּלַּת דִּבְּרֵי־פִיהוּ סִכְלוּת וְאַחֲרִית פִּיהוּ הוֺלֵלוּת רָעָה [Ec 10:13](https://ref.ly/Eccl10:13) *the beginning of the words of his mouth is folly, and the end of his mouth* (his speech) *is evil madness.*

> \[Str1984,3050] הַלֲלוּיָהּ, הַלֲלוּיָהַ v. הלל **Pi** p.238

> \[Str3094] † יְהַלֶּלְאֵל **n.pr.\[m.]** (*he shall praise God*, cf. מַהֲלַלְאֵל infr.);—**1\.** a man of Judah [1 Ch 4:16](https://ref.ly/1Chr4:16); **2\.** a Levite [2 Ch 29:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr29:12).

> \[Str4110] † \[מַהֲלָל] **n.\[m.]** praise;—only in וְאִיש לְפִי מַהֲלָלוֺ [Pr 27:21](https://ref.ly/Prov27:21) the refining pot silver, and the furnace for gold, *and a man according to his praise*, i.e. prob. acc. to the praise of him by others, which tests him; so De Str al.; perh. however *so let a man be to the mouth of his praise*, i.e. *that praise him*,—testing the praise to determine its worth, Thes RobGes; other views are: *according to the measure of his boasting* Ew, i.e. is judged according to his success or failure in that of which he boasts; Hi *according to the thing of which he boasts.*

> \[Str4111] † מַהֲלַלְאֵל **n.pr.m.** (*praise of God*, cf. יְהַלֶּלְאֵל supr.);—**1\.** great\-grandson of Seth acc. to genealogy of P, [Gn 5:12](https://ref.ly/Gen5:12), [5:13](https://ref.ly/Gen5:13), [5:15](https://ref.ly/Gen5:15), [5:16](https://ref.ly/Gen5:16), [5:17](https://ref.ly/Gen5:17), [1 Ch 1:2](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:2). **2\.** a man of Judah [Ne 11:4](https://ref.ly/Neh11:4).
> 
> 239:b. מַהֲלַלְאֵל: rd. מְהַלֶּלְאֵל GrayProp. N. 201\.

> \[Str8416] † תְּהִלָּה **n.f.** praise, song of praise (cf. Ar. يَهْلِيلٌ, the shouting of sacred formula; SprengerMuḥammad iii.527 WeSkizzen iii.107 ff. 114\. 117\. 214 NöZMG 1887, 723 RS Sem. i. 411 CheOP 460\) abs. ´ת [Is 60:18](https://ref.ly/Isa60:18) \+ 19 t. \+ [Je 49:25](https://ref.ly/Jer49:25) Kt, v. ifr.; cstr. תְּהִלַּת [Je 48:2](https://ref.ly/Jer48:2) \+ 2 t.; sf. תְּהִלָּתִי [Je 17:14](https://ref.ly/Jer17:14) \+ 6 t.; תְּהִלָּתְֽךָ [Dt 10:21](https://ref.ly/Deut10:21), [Psalm 48:11](https://ref.ly/Ps48:11), תְּהִלָּתֶָךָ [Psalm 35:88](https://ref.ly/Ps35:88) \+ 6 t.; תְּהִלָּתֶּיךָ [Psalm 9:15](https://ref.ly/Ps9:15); תְּהִלָּתוֺ [Is 42:10](https://ref.ly/Isa42:10) \+ 10 t.; pl. abs. תְּהִלּֽת [Ex 15:11](https://ref.ly/Exod15:11); cstr. תְּהִלּוֺת [Psalm 22:4](https://ref.ly/Ps22:4) \+ 3 t.;—**1\.** *praise, adoration, thanksgiving*, paid to ´י:—[Psalm 48:11](https://ref.ly/Ps48:11) כשׁמך אלהים כן תהלתך לְ קצוי ארץ *as thy name, O God, so is thy praise to the ends of the earth*, [111:10](https://ref.ly/Ps111:10) תהלתו עמדת לעד, תָּמִיד תְּהִלָּתוֺ בְּפִי [Psalm 34:2](https://ref.ly/Ps34:2) *continually is his praise in my mouth* (‖´אֲבָֽרֲכָה אֶת־י) cf. [71:6](https://ref.ly/Ps71:6), also תְהִלָּתֶָךָ ´ימָּלֵא פִ [71:8](https://ref.ly/Ps71:8), ´פִּי יַגִּיד ת [51:7](https://ref.ly/Ps51:7), תַּכַּעְנָה ´שְפָתַי ת [119:171](https://ref.ly/Ps119:171), ´יְדֵבֶּר פִּ ´י ´ת [145:21](https://ref.ly/Ps145:21) (‖וִיבָרֵח כָּ־ל בָּשָר שֵׁם קָדְשׁוֺ); as sung, *song of praise* יָשִׁירוּ תִּהִלָּתוֺ [106:12](https://ref.ly/Ps106:12); הַמְשֹׁרֲרִים וְשִׁיו־תְּהִלָּה וְהוֺדוֺת לֵאלֽהִים [Ne 12:46](https://ref.ly/Neh12:46); cf. לֵאלֽהֵינוּ ´ת [Psalm 40:4](https://ref.ly/Ps40:4) (appos. שֹׁיר תָדָשׁ) \& [Is 42:10](https://ref.ly/Isa42:10) (appos. *id.*), also ´לַיְשָׁרִים נָאוָה ת [33:1](https://ref.ly/Isa33:1) (‖´רַנְנוּ צַדִּיקִים בי); vid. perh. [Psalm 148:14](https://ref.ly/Ps148:14) (Che; yet see De VB); note further אֱלֽהֵי תְהִלָּתִי [Psalm 109:1](https://ref.ly/Ps109:1); יוֺשֵׁב תְּהלּוֺת יִשְרָאֵל [Psalm 22:4](https://ref.ly/Ps22:4) *inhabiting the praises of Isr.;* וּמְרוֺמָם עַל־כֶּל־בְּרָכָה וּתְהִלָּה [Ne 9:5](https://ref.ly/Neh9:5) *exalted above all blessing and praise;* cf. also תְּהִלָּתִי [Is 48:9](https://ref.ly/Isa48:9) (‖שְׁמִי), \& of *praise due* to ´י [Is 42:8](https://ref.ly/Isa42:8). **2\.** the act of *general, public, praise* (cf. also supr. esp. [Psalm 22:4](https://ref.ly/Ps22:4), [33:1](https://ref.ly/Ps33:1), [106:12](https://ref.ly/Ps106:12), [Ne 12:46](https://ref.ly/Neh12:46));—בּאֹוּ שְׁעָרָיו בְּתוֺדָה ´חֲצֵרֹתָיו בִּת [Psalm 100:4](https://ref.ly/Ps100:4) *enter his gates with thanksgiving, his courts with praise;* שִׁיר חָדָשׁ ´שִׁירוּ לי תְּהִלָּתוֺ בִּקְהִל חֲסִידִים Psalm [149:1](https://ref.ly/Ps149:1); cf. [Psalm 22:26](https://ref.ly/Ps22:26), [65:2](https://ref.ly/Ps65:2), [66:2](https://ref.ly/Ps66:2), [147:1](https://ref.ly/Ps147:1); before the army הֵחֵלּוּ בְרִנָּה וּתְחִלָּה [2 Ch 20:22](https://ref.ly/2Chr20:22); in exhortation to the peoples הַשְׁמִיעוּ קוֺל תְּהַלָּתוֺ [Psalm 66:8](https://ref.ly/Ps66:8) (‖בָּֽרֲבוּ עֵמִּים אֱלֽהֵינוּ), cf. [Is 42:12](https://ref.ly/Isa42:12). **3\.** *praise\-song*, as title, תְּהִלָּה לְדָוִד [Psalm 145:1](https://ref.ly/Ps145:1) (so NH ספר תהלות \& תְּהִלִּים, תִּילִּים, \= *Psalms*). **4\.** qualities, deeds, etc., of ´י, demanding praise:—נוֺרָא תְהִלּֽת [Ex 15:11](https://ref.ly/Exod15:11) (song), i.e. terrible in attributes that call for praise (‖נֶאְדָּר בַּקֹּדֶשׁ \& עשֵֹׁה פֶלֶא); oft. (both sg. and pl.) of Yahweh's deeds c. vb. סִפֵּר [Psalm 9:15](https://ref.ly/Ps9:15), [78:4](https://ref.ly/Ps78:4), [79:13](https://ref.ly/Ps79:13), [Is 43:21](https://ref.ly/Isa43:21), also \+ ´שֵׁם י Psalm [102:22](https://ref.ly/Ps102:22); c. יַשְׁמִיעַ [Psalm 106:2](https://ref.ly/Ps106:2) (‖´יְמַלֵּל גְּבוּרוֺת י); \+ צִדְקֶָךָ c. יֶהְגֶּה [Psalm 35:28](https://ref.ly/Ps35:28); c. יְבַשֵָּרוּ [Is 60:6](https://ref.ly/Isa60:6); \+ ´חַסְדֵי י, c. אַוְכִּיר [Is 63:7](https://ref.ly/Isa63:7); vid also לְהֹדוֺת לְשֵׁם קָדְשֶָׁךָ לְהִשְׁתַּבֵחַ בִּתְהִלָּתֶָךָ [Psalm 106:47](https://ref.ly/Ps106:47) \= [1 Ch 16:35](https://ref.ly/1Chr16:35) *to give thanks unto thy holy name, to glory in thy praises* (\= praiseworthy deeds). **5\.** *renown, fame, glory:* **a.** of Damascus עִיר תְּהִלָּה֯ [Je 49:25](https://ref.ly/Jer49:25) (on Qr תְּהִלָּת v. Hi Ges§ 80\. 2 b); comp. perhaps מַעֲטֵה תְהִלָּה Is [61:3](https://ref.ly/Isa61:3) *mantle of renown (renown as a mantle*, cf. De VB; \> Di *splendid garment;* but BI Che Du read מַעֲטֵה אֵ֔בֶל and translate ´ת *praise, song of praise*, cf. **1\. 2**). **b.** of ´י, מָֽלְאָה הָאָרֶץ ´כִּסָּה שָׁמַים הוֺדוֺ וּת Hb [3:3](https://ref.ly/Hab3:3) *his splendour hath covered (the) heavens, and of his renown the earth is full.* Also **c.** fig., *praise, renown*, \= obj. of praise, possessor of renown; used (*a*) of Israel [Dt 26:19](https://ref.ly/Deut26:19) (abs.), [Je 13:11](https://ref.ly/Jer13:11) (\+ לִי) [33:9](https://ref.ly/Jer33:9) (\+ לִי and לְכֹל גּוֺיֵי הָאָרֶץ)—all ‖שֵׁם, תִּפְאֶרֶת; v. also [Is 61:11](https://ref.ly/Isa61:11), \& perh. [Psalm 148:14](https://ref.ly/Ps148:14) (see sub **1** supr.); (*b*) of Jerusalem [Is 62:7](https://ref.ly/Isa62:7), also [Zp 3:19](https://ref.ly/Zeph3:19), [3:20](https://ref.ly/Zeph3:20) (both ‖שֵׁם), all these c. vb. הָיָה or שִים; cf. וְקָרָאת ´יְשׁוּעָה חוֺמֹתַיִח וּשְׁעָרַיִח ת [Is 60:18](https://ref.ly/Isa60:18); (*c*) of Babylon וַתִּתָּפֵש תְּהִלַּת כָּל־הָאָרֶץ [Je 51:41](https://ref.ly/Jer51:41); (*d*) of Moab אֵין מוֺאָב ´עוֺד ת [Je 48:2](https://ref.ly/Jer48:2); (*e*) of ´י, הוּא תְהִלָּֽתְךָ וְהוּא אֱלֽהֶיךָ [Dt 10:21](https://ref.ly/Deut10:21), כִּי תְהִלָּתִי אָתָּה [Je 17:14](https://ref.ly/Jer17:14) (no vb.)

## הָלַם (id: BDB02419)

\[Str1986] † \[הָלַם] **vb.** smite, hammer, strike down (cf. Ph. מהלם infr. sub מַהֲלֻמוֺת);—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 fs. וְהָֽלְמָה consec. Ju [5:26](https://ref.ly/Judg5:26); 3 pl. הָֽלְמוּ Ju [5:22](https://ref.ly/Judg5:22), [Is 16:8](https://ref.ly/Isa16:8); sf. הֲלָמוּנִי [Pr 23:25](https://ref.ly/Prov23:25); *Impf.* 3 ms. sf. יֶהֶלְמֵנִי [Psalm 141:5](https://ref.ly/Ps141:5); 3 mpl. יַהֲלֽמָוּן [Psalm 74:6](https://ref.ly/Ps74:6); *Pt. act.* ה֫וֺלָם פָּעַם [Is 41:7](https://ref.ly/Isa41:7) (cf. Ges§ 29, 3 b); *pass.* cstr. הֲלוּמֵי [Is 28:1](https://ref.ly/Isa28:1); —*smite* (with hammer), sq. acc. Pers. Ju [5:26](https://ref.ly/Judg5:26) (fatally; ‖מָחַק, מָחַץ); also fig. of correction, discipline [Psalm 141:5](https://ref.ly/Ps141:5) (‖הוֺכִּיחַ); of intoxicating, wine גֶּפֶן שִבְמָה בַּעֲלֵי גוֺיִם הָֽלְמוּ שְרוּקֶּיהָ [Is 16:8](https://ref.ly/Isa16:8) *the vine of Sibma, its choice branches smote down lords of nations* (so Hi De Che Brd Di al., RVm; *lords* subj. acc. to Ges Ew al. AV RV); also הֲלוּמֵי יַיִן *smitten down by wine* [Is 28:1](https://ref.ly/Isa28:1); cf. further [Pr 23:35](https://ref.ly/Prov23:35); sq. acc rei. ה֫וֺלָם־פָּעְם [Is 41:7](https://ref.ly/Isa41:7) *one striking an anvil; smite down*, or *strike off* [Psalm 74:6](https://ref.ly/Ps74:6) (obj. פִתּוּחִים *carved work*); also abs. of horse' hoofs Ju [5:22](https://ref.ly/Judg5:22).—On וַהֲלֽם [1 S 14:16](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:16) v. וַהֲלֽם.

> \[Str1987] † הֶ֫לֶם **n.pr.m.** a man of Asher [1 Ch 7:35](https://ref.ly/1Chr7:35).

> \[Str1989] † הַלְמוּת **n.f.** hammer, mallet, only cstr. עֲמֵלִים ´ה Ju [5:26](https://ref.ly/Judg5:26) *a workman's hammer*.

> \[Str3095] † יַהֲלֽם **n.\[m.]** a precious stone, jasper? (so LXX Vulgate), or onyx? (cf. Di [Ex 28:18](https://ref.ly/Exod28:18)) (appar. fr. √ הלם; owing to its hardness, as able to *smite, hammer*?)—mentioned in lists of precious stones [Ex 28:18](https://ref.ly/Exod28:18), [39:11](https://ref.ly/Exod39:11), Ez [28:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek28:13).

> \[Str4112] † מַהֲלֻמַוֺת **n.f.pl.** strokes, blows (cf. Ph. מהלם *stroke, stamp, impression*, once on a coin מהלם אהדר *coinage of Gader* (Gades), \= *percussura*, κόμμα, GesMon. pl. 40, xv. c also p. 307\)—[Pr 18:6](https://ref.ly/Prov18:6) (cf. Baer) [19:29](https://ref.ly/Prov19:29), both times of blows on the body.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 28:1; Exodus 39:11; Isaiah 16:8; Ezekiel 28:13; Proverbs 18:6; Proverbs 23:35; Isaiah 41:7; 1 Samuel 14:16; Psalms 74:6; Proverbs 19:29; Judges 5:26; Exodus 28:18; Judges 5:22; 1 Chronicles 7:35; Proverbs 23:25; Psalms 141:5


## הֲלֽם (id: BDB02424)

\[Str1988] † הֲלֽם **adv.** of place, hither (Ar. هَلُمَّ *hither !*)—[Ex 3:5](https://ref.ly/Exod3:5) אַל־תִּקְרַב הֲלֽס approach not *hither*, Ju [18:3](https://ref.ly/Judg18:3), [20:7](https://ref.ly/Judg20:7), [1 S 10:22](https://ref.ly/1Sam10:22), [14:16](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:16) and lo, the camp melted away וַיֵּלֶךְ וַהֲלֽם rd. with LXX (ἔνθεν καίἔνθεν) Th We Dr הֲלֽם וַהֲלֽם *hither and thither* i.e. in different directions (cf. הֵנָּה וָהֵנָּה [Jos 8:20](https://ref.ly/Josh8:20)), v:[36](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:36), v:[38](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:38), [Ru 2:14](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:14); עַד הֲלֽם fig. *hitherto, thus far* (i.e. to this point of dignity and greatness) [2 S 7:18](https://ref.ly/2Sam7:18) (\= [1 Ch 17:16](https://ref.ly/1Chr17:16)). [Psalm 73:10](https://ref.ly/Ps73:10), if text be sound, וַהֲלֽם must mean *thither* (sc. after the persons described v:[3–9](https://ref.ly/Ps73:3-Ps73:9)); but such sense questionable; rd. prob. with Houb La for ישיב עמו הלם, יַשְבִּעֵמוֺ לָחֶם satisfies them with bread: cf. Che HupNow. [Gn 16:13](https://ref.ly/Gen16:13) \= *here;* but here also text is dub.: v Di. Ju [14:15](https://ref.ly/Judg14:15) rd. וַהֲלֽם for הֲלאֹ: v. הֲ **1** *end*.

> \[Str1990] † הָם **n.pr.loc.** only in הַוּוּזִים בְּהָם [Gn 14:5](https://ref.ly/Gen14:5); not otherwise known; Vrss בָּהֶם; JerQuaest. in libr. Genes. בְּחֹם; Ol conj. בַּחֲמָת; Tu Di think perh. old name of Rabbath Ammon.

> \[Str1991] † \[הם?, המה?] only in מֵהֱמֵהֶם Ez [7:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek7:11) Ew nothing *of their moaning, wailing;* Thes RV *wealth,* cf. הָמוֺן **5,** but precarious; form very suspicious; prob. dittogr. for preceding מֵהֶם; del. B Co.

> \[Str4099] † הַמְּדָתָא **n.pr.m.** father of Haman (Pers. *mâh, moon* (*mâha, month,* SpiegAPK 236\) \+ *data, given?* so (written *hamaodata* Öt; LXX Ἁμαδαθου (alw. Genit.); in Gk. perh. Μαδάτης \[Μαδάτας, Μαδέτης], cf. XenophCyr, v. 3\. 41 Diodxvii, 67 PottZMG 1859, 424\);—הַמְּדָתָא הָֽאֲגָגִי [Est 3:1](https://ref.ly/Esth3:1), [3:10](https://ref.ly/Esth3:10), [8:5](https://ref.ly/Esth8:5), [9:24](https://ref.ly/Esth9:24); without האגגי [9:10](https://ref.ly/Esth9:10).

## הֵ֫מָּה (id: BDB02428)

\[Str1992,2004,3860–61] † הֵ֫מָּה and הֵם (without appreciable distinction in usage, except prob. in so far as the longer or shorter from was better adapted to the rhythm of particular sentences: on the whole הֵמָּה (alone) is somewhat more freq. than הֵם, הָהֵם on the contrary is said regularly, הָהֵמָּה occurring only 12 t., viz. [Nu 9:7](https://ref.ly/Num9:7) (P), [Je 14:15](https://ref.ly/Jer14:15), [Zc 14:15](https://ref.ly/Zech14:15), and in the phr. מִָים הָהֵמָּה(הַ)בַּ [2 K 18:4](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:4), [Je 3:16](https://ref.ly/Jer3:16), [3:18](https://ref.ly/Jer3:18), [5:18](https://ref.ly/Jer5:18); [50:4](https://ref.ly/Jer50:4), [Jo 3:2](https://ref.ly/Joel2:29), [4:1](https://ref.ly/Joel3:1), [Zc 8:23](https://ref.ly/Zech8:23), [Ne 13:15](https://ref.ly/Neh13:15), though בַּיָּמִים הָהֵם is much more common) **pron. 3 pl. masc.** (הֵמָּה used incorrectly for the fem. [Zc 5:10](https://ref.ly/Zech5:10), [Ru 1:22](https://ref.ly/Ruth1:22), Ct [6:8](https://ref.ly/Song6:8)), **fem.** הֵ֫נָּה \[NH also הֵן, which occurs however in Bibl. Heb. only after a prefix: v. infr. **8**], **pr. 3 pl.** they (BAram. הִמּוֺ, q.v.; هُمْ, هُنَّ; Eth. (one form) እሙንቱ፡: እምኣንቲ፡: *’emūn\-tū*, *emān\-tū*; cf. also for the dissyl. form, the sf. *\-ōmū* \[fem. *\-ōn];* perh. also As. suff *šunu*, *šina* \[*ni*], cf. reff. sub הוּא). In usage not different in genl. from הוּא, הִיא (which see, under the corresponding headings, for fuller explanations), thus: —

 **1\. a.** [Gn 6:4](https://ref.ly/Gen6:4), [Ex 5:7](https://ref.ly/Exod5:7) הֵוּ יֵֽלְכוּ let *them* (emph.) go and gather straw, [Nu 31:16](https://ref.ly/Num31:16), [Is 24:14](https://ref.ly/Isa24:14), [Ho 4:14](https://ref.ly/Hos4:14), [7:3](https://ref.ly/Hos7:3) etc.; after כִּי [Je 5:5](https://ref.ly/Jer5:5), [34:7](https://ref.ly/Jer34:7). (For the use in circumst. clauses v. Dr§ 160, 169\.) **b.** Ju [1:22](https://ref.ly/Judg1:22) גַם הֵם…ויעלו, [Dt 2:11](https://ref.ly/Deut2:11) (אַף הֵם), [3:20](https://ref.ly/Deut3:20), [Jos 9:4](https://ref.ly/Josh9:4), Ez [30:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek30:17). **c.** [Ex 18:22](https://ref.ly/Exod18:22) every small matter יִשְׁפְּטוּ־הֵם let them judge *themselves,* v:[26](https://ref.ly/Exod18:26), [Je 15:19](https://ref.ly/Jer15:19), [17:18](https://ref.ly/Jer17:18), [Psalm 109:28](https://ref.ly/Ps109:28); [Est 9:1](https://ref.ly/Esth9:1). **d.** [Gn 33:6](https://ref.ly/Gen33:6), [44:3](https://ref.ly/Gen44:3), [Jud 6:5](https://ref.ly/Judg6:5) etc. **e.** cf. [Ne 10:38](https://ref.ly/Neh10:38). **2\. a.** (Resuming the subj. with emph.) [Gn 14:24](https://ref.ly/Gen14:24) (note accents), [Dt 1:39](https://ref.ly/Deut1:39), [Psalm 23:4](https://ref.ly/Ps23:4), [27:2](https://ref.ly/Ps27:2) (accents), [37:9](https://ref.ly/Ps37:9), [107:24](https://ref.ly/Ps107:24), Ez [36:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:7), [44:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek44:15). **b.** [Is 30:7](https://ref.ly/Isa30:7), Ez [27:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:13), [27:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:17), [27:21](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:21); cf. [Is 44:11](https://ref.ly/Isa44:11). **c.** [Nu 14:27](https://ref.ly/Num14:27) a, [Je 27:9](https://ref.ly/Jer27:9), Ez [43:19](https://ref.ly/Ezek43:19) אֲשֶׁר הֵם מִוֶּרַע צָדוֺק, [Ec 4:2](https://ref.ly/Eccl4:2), [Ne 2:13](https://ref.ly/Neh2:13). **3\. a.** [Gn 3:7](https://ref.ly/Gen3:7), [Ex 5:8](https://ref.ly/Exod5:8) כִּי נִרְפִּים הֵם for *they* are idle, [14:3](https://ref.ly/Exod14:3), [15:23](https://ref.ly/Exod15:23), [29:33](https://ref.ly/Exod29:33) etc. (the opp. order rare: [Nu 11:16](https://ref.ly/Num11:16), [Is 61:9](https://ref.ly/Isa61:9), cf. De:[Psalm 94:11](https://ref.ly/Ps94:11)). **b.** [Gn 34:21](https://ref.ly/Gen34:21), [34:23](https://ref.ly/Gen34:23), [41:26](https://ref.ly/Gen41:26), [48:5](https://ref.ly/Gen48:5) לִי הֵם…שׁני בניך thy two sons…, they are mine, [Ex 32:16](https://ref.ly/Exod32:16) \+ oft.: [Is 41:22](https://ref.ly/Isa41:22) הָרִאשֹׁנוֺת מָה הֵנָּה, [49:21](https://ref.ly/Isa49:21) אֵלֶּה אֵיפֹה הֵם these—where were they? [Zp 2:12](https://ref.ly/Zeph2:12) after אַתֶּם, peculiarly (cf. Dr§ 198 Obs. 2\). **c.** [Dt 20:15](https://ref.ly/Deut20:15) אֲשֶׁר לאֹ־מֵעָרֵי הַגֹּויִם הָאֵלֶּה הֵנָּה, Ju [19:12](https://ref.ly/Judg19:12), [1 K 9:20](https://ref.ly/1Kgs9:20). **d.** [2 K 7:10](https://ref.ly/2Kgs7:10) כַּאֲשֶׁר הֵמָּה. ([Pr 19:7](https://ref.ly/Prov19:7) Kt לאֹ־הֵמָּה is prob. corrupt: v. Comm.) **4\. a.** [Pr 30:24](https://ref.ly/Prov30:24) אַרְבָּעָה הֵם קְטַנֵּי־אָרֶץ four things are *they,* the little ones of the earth, v:[29](https://ref.ly/Prov30:29) (cf. before a rel. clause v:[15](https://ref.ly/Prov30:15), v:[18](https://ref.ly/Prov30:18), [6:16](https://ref.ly/Prov6:16)) Ct [6:8](https://ref.ly/Song6:8), [Is 51:19](https://ref.ly/Isa51:19) שְׁתַּיִם הֵנָּה קֹרְאֹתַיִךְ, [1 Ch 9:26](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:26) (v. הוא **4 a**). **b.** (β) [Gn 21:29](https://ref.ly/Gen21:29) …מָה הֵנָּה שֶׁבַע *lit.* What are they—these seven sheep? [Zc 1:9](https://ref.ly/Zech1:9), [4:5](https://ref.ly/Zech4:5). (γ) …אֵלֶּה הֵם † [Gn 25:16](https://ref.ly/Gen25:16) (\= [1 Ch 1:36](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:36)) [Lv 23:2](https://ref.ly/Lev23:2), [Nu 3:20](https://ref.ly/Num3:20), [3:21](https://ref.ly/Num3:21), [3:27](https://ref.ly/Num3:27), [3:33](https://ref.ly/Num3:33), [1 S 4:8](https://ref.ly/1Sam4:8), [1 Ch 8:6](https://ref.ly/1Chr8:6), [12:15](https://ref.ly/1Chr12:15) (v. Dr: § 201, 3\). **6\. a.** In a neuter sense (rare), [Jb 6:7](https://ref.ly/Job6:7) הֵמָּה כִּדְוֵי לַחמִי *they* (i.e. such sufferings as mine) are as loathsomeness in my meat, [Je 7:4](https://ref.ly/Jer7:4) ´הֵיכַל י הֵמָּה *they* (i.e. these buildings) are the temple of ´י; and in the fem., [Lv 5:22](https://ref.ly/Lev5:22) of all that a man doeth to sin בָּהֵנָּה *therein,* [1 Ch 21:10](https://ref.ly/1Chr21:10) מֵהֵנָּה (‖ [2 S 24:12](https://ref.ly/2Sam24:12) מֵהֶם); [2 S 12:8](https://ref.ly/2Sam12:8), [Jb 23:14](https://ref.ly/Job23:14) כָּהֵנָּה things *such as these*. **7\.** With art. הָהֵם, [Gn 6:4](https://ref.ly/Gen6:4), Ex 2:11m [Nu 14:38](https://ref.ly/Num14:38) \+ oft.; הָהֵמָּה 12 t. (v. supr.); הָהֵנָּה † [1 S 17:28](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:28). N.B.—Ez [8:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek8:6) Kt מָהֵם stands for מָה הֵם (so Qr), cf. מַוֶּה for מַה־וֶּה [Ex 4:2](https://ref.ly/Exod4:2); [2 K 9:18](https://ref.ly/2Kgs9:18) עַד־הֵם is irreg. for עֲדֵיהֶם; [2 S 4:6](https://ref.ly/2Sam4:6) ´וְהֵנָּה וג is textual error for ´וְהִנֵּה שֹׁעֶרֶת הַבַּיִת סֹקְלָה חִטִּים וג v. We Dr (LXX \& RVm). **8\.** With preps. (in lieu of the normal, and more usual, forms with suffixes בָּהֶם, לָהֶם, מֵהֶם etc. \[כָּהֶם \& כָּהֶן, however, each once only, כְּמוֺהֶם thrice: v. בְּ])—**a.** בָּהֵמָּה † [Ex 30:4](https://ref.ly/Exod30:4), [36:1](https://ref.ly/Exod36:1) (both P), Hb [1:16](https://ref.ly/Hab1:16); כָּהֵמָּה † [Je 36:32](https://ref.ly/Jer36:32); לְהֵמָּה † [Je 14:16](https://ref.ly/Jer14:16) מֵהֵמָּה † [Je 10:2](https://ref.ly/Jer10:2), [Ec 12:12](https://ref.ly/Eccl12:12). **b.** כָּהֵם † [2 S 24:3](https://ref.ly/2Sam24:3) Yahweh add to the people כָּהֵם וְכָהֵם מֵאָה פְעָמִים *the like of themselves* and *the like of themselves* 100 times, \= [1 Ch 21:3](https://ref.ly/1Chr21:3) (but כָּהֵם here only once, cf. [Dt 1:11](https://ref.ly/Deut1:11) בָּכֶם), [2 Ch 9:11](https://ref.ly/2Chr9:11), [Ec 9:12](https://ref.ly/Eccl9:12). **c.** בָּהֵנָּה † [Lv 5:22](https://ref.ly/Lev5:22) (P) [Nu 13:19](https://ref.ly/Num13:19) (JE) [Je 5:17](https://ref.ly/Jer5:17): כָּהֵנָּה † [Gn 41:19](https://ref.ly/Gen41:19), [2 S 12:8](https://ref.ly/2Sam12:8) וְאֹסִפָה לְךָ כָּהֵנָּה וְכָהֵנָּה, [Jb 23:14](https://ref.ly/Job23:14): לָהֵנָּה † Ez [1:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:5), [1:23](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:23), [1:23](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:23), [42:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek42:9), [Zc 5:9](https://ref.ly/Zech5:9): מֵהֵנָּה † [Lv 4:2](https://ref.ly/Lev4:2) (P) [Is 34:16](https://ref.ly/Isa34:16), [Je 5:6](https://ref.ly/Jer5:6), Ez [16:51](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:51), [42:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek42:5), [Psalm 34:21](https://ref.ly/Ps34:21), [1 Ch 21:10](https://ref.ly/1Chr21:10) (‖ [2 S 24:12](https://ref.ly/2Sam24:12) מֵהֶם). **d.** בָּהֵן † [Gn 19:29](https://ref.ly/Gen19:29) (P) [30:26](https://ref.ly/Gen30:26), [30:37](https://ref.ly/Gen30:37), [Ex 25:29](https://ref.ly/Exod25:29), [37:16](https://ref.ly/Exod37:16) (both P) [Lv 10:1](https://ref.ly/Lev10:1), [11:21](https://ref.ly/Lev11:21), [14:40](https://ref.ly/Lev14:40), [Nu 10:3](https://ref.ly/Num10:3), [16:7](https://ref.ly/Num16:7) (all P) [Dt 28:52](https://ref.ly/Deut28:52), [Je 4:29](https://ref.ly/Jer4:29), [48:9](https://ref.ly/Jer48:9), [51:43](https://ref.ly/Jer51:43), [51:43](https://ref.ly/Jer51:43) (בָּהֶן thrice only, v.p. 88\); כָּהֵן † Ez [18:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek18:14) (edd.; Baer כָּהֶן); לָהֵן ( \= *therefore*) † [Ru 1:13](https://ref.ly/Ruth1:13), [1:13](https://ref.ly/Ruth1:13) (also BAram.); מֵהֵן † Ez [16:47](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:47), [16:52](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:52) (edd.; Baer מֵהֶן). With the exception of כָּחֵנָּה, these forms are found mostly in P and writers not earlier than Je.; **b** and **d,** however, depend solely on the punctuation.)

## הָמָה (id: BDB02429)

\[Str1993] † \[הָמָה] **vb.** murmur, growl, roar, be boisterous ( Targum הֲמָא, but rare, and perh. not genuine Aram.; cf. Ar. هَمْهَمَ *murmur*);—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 mpl. הָמוּ [Psalm 46:7](https://ref.ly/Ps46:7) \+ 2t.; וְהָמוּ consec. [Je 5:22](https://ref.ly/Jer5:22) \+ 2 t.; *Impf.* יֶהֱמֶה [Je 6:23](https://ref.ly/Jer6:23) \+ 3 t.; 2 fs. תֶּהֱמִי [Psalm 42:12](https://ref.ly/Ps42:12), [43:5](https://ref.ly/Ps43:5); וַתֶּהֱמִי [42:6](https://ref.ly/Ps42:6); אֶהֱמֶה [55:18](https://ref.ly/Ps55:18); יֶהֱמָיָה [77:4](https://ref.ly/Ps77:4); יֶהֱמוּ [46:4](https://ref.ly/Ps46:4) \+ 3 t.; וַיֶּהֱמוּ [Is 51:15](https://ref.ly/Isa51:15), [Je 31:35](https://ref.ly/Jer31:35); יֶהֱמָי\+וּן [Psalm 39:7](https://ref.ly/Ps39:7) \+ 2 t.; 1 pl. נֶהֱמֶה [Is 59:11](https://ref.ly/Isa59:11); *Inf.* הֲמוֺת [Is 17:12](https://ref.ly/Isa17:12); *Pt.* הֹמֶה [Pr 20:1](https://ref.ly/Prov20:1), [Je 4:19](https://ref.ly/Jer4:19); f. הוֺמָה [1 K 1:41](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:41), הֹמִיָּה [Pr 7:11](https://ref.ly/Prov7:11), [9:13](https://ref.ly/Prov9:13), הוֺמִיָּה [Is 22:2](https://ref.ly/Isa22:2); fpl. הֹמִיּוֺת [Pr 1:21](https://ref.ly/Prov1:21), הֹמוֺת Ez [7:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek7:16) (Co הַמָּוֶת cf. LXX SyrVer);—**1\.** *growl* like a dog כַּכָּלֶב [Psalm 59:7](https://ref.ly/Ps59:7), [59:15](https://ref.ly/Ps59:15), subj. wicked men; *groan* (in distress, lamentation), like bears כַּדֹּבִים [Is 59:11](https://ref.ly/Isa59:11) (‖כיונה הגה); of doves (also in sim.) Ez [7:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek7:16) cf. Da (si vera l., vid. supr.). **2\.** *murmur,* fig. of soul (נֶפֶשׁ) in discouragement [Psalm 42:6](https://ref.ly/Ps42:6), [42:12](https://ref.ly/Ps42:12), [43:5](https://ref.ly/Ps43:5); in prayer [55:18](https://ref.ly/Ps55:18), [77:4](https://ref.ly/Ps77:4) (both ‖אָשִיחָה); also subj. מֵעִים of the *thrill* of deep\-felt compassion or sympathy, sq. לְ of person pitied, [Je 31:20](https://ref.ly/Jer31:20) (‖רחם ארחמנו), sq. עַל Ct [5:4](https://ref.ly/Song5:4); further כַּכִּנּוֺר *like the lyre (zither)* [Is 16:11](https://ref.ly/Isa16:11); כַּחֲלִילִים *like flutes,* subj. לֵב [Je 48:36](https://ref.ly/Jer48:36) (sq. לְ); v:[36](https://ref.ly/Jer48:36) (sq. אֶל); cf. [Je 4:19](https://ref.ly/Jer4:19) לִי לִבִּי הֹמֶ־ה. **3\.** *roar,* of waves, subj. גַּלִּים [Je 5:22](https://ref.ly/Jer5:22), [31:35](https://ref.ly/Jer31:35), [Is 51:15](https://ref.ly/Isa51:15) cf. [Psalm 46:4](https://ref.ly/Ps46:4); sim. of roar of multitudes [Is 17:12](https://ref.ly/Isa17:12), [Je 6:23](https://ref.ly/Jer6:23), [50:42](https://ref.ly/Jer50:42); metaph. *id.,* [Je 51:55](https://ref.ly/Jer51:55); *be tumultuous,* of peoples [Psalm 46:7](https://ref.ly/Ps46:7) (‖מָטוּ); [83:3](https://ref.ly/Ps83:3) (‖נָֽשְאוּ ראֹשׁ). **4\.** *be in commotion, stir,* of city [1 K 1:41](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:41), [Is 22:2](https://ref.ly/Isa22:2); so pt. fpl. as subst. הֹמִיּוֺת ראֹשׁ [Pr 1:21](https://ref.ly/Prov1:21) *head of stirring, bustling* streets (‖פתחי שׁערים); of man, אַח־הֶבֶל יֶהֱמָ\+יוּן [Psalm 39:7](https://ref.ly/Ps39:7) *surely to no purpose they bustle about* (‖יִתְהָלֶּח־אִישׁ אַח־בְּצֶלֶם) **5\.** *be boisterous, turbulent,* as with wine [Zc 9:5](https://ref.ly/Zech9:5) (but rd. perh. דָּמָם cf. FIHexapl. ii. 3, 1024 KloTh LZ, 1879, 564 StaZAW 1881, 18\); also pt. as adj., of שֵׁכָר (by meton.) [Pr 20:1](https://ref.ly/Prov20:1); of shameless woman [Pr 7:11](https://ref.ly/Prov7:11), [9:13](https://ref.ly/Prov9:13).

## הָמַם (id: BDB02435)

[Ex 14:24](https://ref.ly/Exod14:24), [23:27](https://ref.ly/Exod23:27), [Jos 10:10](https://ref.ly/Josh10:10), Ju [4:15](https://ref.ly/Judg4:15), [1 S 7:10](https://ref.ly/1Sam7:10), [Psalm 18:15](https://ref.ly/Ps18:15) \= [2 S 22:15](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:15) (‖הפיץ), [Psalm 144:6](https://ref.ly/Ps144:6) (‖ *id.*); *discomfit, vex,* [2 Ch 15:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr15:6) c. acc. \& בְּכָל־צָרָה of instr.; c. acc. also [Dt 2:15](https://ref.ly/Deut2:15) (sq. עַד תֻּמָּם), [Est 9:24](https://ref.ly/Esth9:24) (sq. לְאַבְּדָם); prob. also [Je 51:34](https://ref.ly/Jer51:34) (‖אָכַל), cf. הוּם [Dt 7:23](https://ref.ly/Deut7:23).

> † \[הָמַן] **vb.** rage, be trubulent (? denom. fr. המון Thes, after Rabb.; cf. Ke RV; AV *multiply;* but very dub.)—only **Qal** *Inf.* sf. יַעַן הֲמָנְכֶם מִן־הַגּוֺיִם Ez [5:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek5:7) (Sm queries; Ew, after SyrVer Aq, הִמָּנֵכֶם fr. מנה); rd. rather, with Bö Co Da, המרותכם (fr. מרה) *because ye rebelled.*

> \[Str2001] הָמָן **n.pr.m.** Haman, favourite of Ahasuerus (etym. dub.; acc. to JenVOJ 1892, 58 ff. \= Elamit. n.pr.div. *Humban,* or *Humman*)—[Est 3:1](https://ref.ly/Esth3:1) \+ 53 t. in Esther.

\[Str2004,3860] I. \[הֵן] **pron. 3 pl. fem.,** oft. in NH, in Bibl. Heb. only with prefixes, בָּהֵן, etc.: see under הֵמָּה **8 d.** p. 241 f



## II הֵן (id: BDB02444)

\[Str2005,518,2006] II. הֵן:100 (הֶ־ן † [Nu 23:9](https://ref.ly/Num23:9), [23:24](https://ref.ly/Num23:24), [Jb 8:19](https://ref.ly/Job8:19), [8:20](https://ref.ly/Job8:20), [13:1](https://ref.ly/Job13:1), [26:14](https://ref.ly/Job26:14), [33:6](https://ref.ly/Job33:6), [33:12](https://ref.ly/Job33:12), [33:29](https://ref.ly/Job33:29), [36:5](https://ref.ly/Job36:5), [36:22](https://ref.ly/Job36:22), [36:26](https://ref.ly/Job36:26); הֵ־ן [Jb 13:15](https://ref.ly/Job13:15), [36:30](https://ref.ly/Job36:30), [41:1](https://ref.ly/Job41:1) \+) **demonstr. adv.** or **interj.** lo ! behold (on etym. v. הִנֵּה), less widely used than הִנֵּה, and in prose mostly confined to calling attention to some fact upon which action is to be taken, or a conclusion based; **a.** [Gn 3:22](https://ref.ly/Gen3:22) הֵן הָאָדָם הָיָה *behold* the man is become as one of us, \& now lest, etc., [4:14](https://ref.ly/Gen4:14) *behold,* thou hast driven me forth etc., [11:6](https://ref.ly/Gen11:6), [15:3](https://ref.ly/Gen15:3), [19:34](https://ref.ly/Gen19:34), [27:11](https://ref.ly/Gen27:11), [29:7](https://ref.ly/Gen29:7), [30:34](https://ref.ly/Gen30:34) (nearly \= *yea*), [Ex 5:5](https://ref.ly/Exod5:5), [6:12](https://ref.ly/Exod6:12), [6:30](https://ref.ly/Exod6:30), [Lv 10:18](https://ref.ly/Lev10:18), [10:19](https://ref.ly/Lev10:19), [Nu 17:27](https://ref.ly/Num17:27), [Dt 5:21](https://ref.ly/Deut5:21) (in prose only in Pent. (23 t.) and Ez [18:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek18:4) in this usage); in poetry, used more freely, but chiefly in Is2 \& Jb,—[Nu 23:9](https://ref.ly/Num23:9), [23:24](https://ref.ly/Num23:24) (Balaam) [Is 23:13](https://ref.ly/Isa23:13), [32:1](https://ref.ly/Isa32:1), [33:7](https://ref.ly/Isa33:7), [Psalm 51:7](https://ref.ly/Ps51:7), [51:8](https://ref.ly/Ps51:8), [68:34](https://ref.ly/Ps68:34), [78:20](https://ref.ly/Ps78:20), [139:4](https://ref.ly/Ps139:4), [Pr 11:31](https://ref.ly/Prov11:31) (stating the premiss to a conclusion introduced by אַף כִּי, q.v.), [24:12](https://ref.ly/Prov24:12): elsewhere (except in senses **b, c**) only in Is:2 (23 t.) \& Jb (31 t), as [Is 40:15](https://ref.ly/Isa40:15), [40:15](https://ref.ly/Isa40:15), [41:11](https://ref.ly/Isa41:11), [41:24](https://ref.ly/Isa41:24), [41:29](https://ref.ly/Isa41:29), [50:1](https://ref.ly/Isa50:1), [50:2](https://ref.ly/Isa50:2), [50:9](https://ref.ly/Isa50:9), [50:9](https://ref.ly/Isa50:9), [50:11](https://ref.ly/Isa50:11), [Jb 4:18](https://ref.ly/Job4:18), [15:15](https://ref.ly/Job15:15), [25:5](https://ref.ly/Job25:5) (in these three passages before אַף or אַף כִּי), [9:1](https://ref.ly/Job9:1), [9:12](https://ref.ly/Job9:12) etc. (v. supr.). † **b.** as a **hypoth. part.,** propounding a possibility, if (so NH BAram., Mand. Palm. (:ZMG 1888, 404\); on the contr. Syr. ܐܶܢ Pal. Targum אִין, also Palm. (*ib.* p. 415\), Ar. إِنْ with א), a special application or development of the use **a:** [Ex 4:1](https://ref.ly/Exod4:1) והן לא יאמינו לי and *behold* ( \= and *suppose*) they will not believe me ? etc., [Lv 25:20](https://ref.ly/Lev25:20) and if (וְכִי) ye say, What shall we eat the 7th year ? הֵן לאֹ נִזְרַע *behold,* we shall not sow etc. (i.e. *supposing* we do not sow), [Is 54:15](https://ref.ly/Isa54:15), [Jb 40:23](https://ref.ly/Job40:23), [2 Ch 7:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr7:13), [7:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr7:13) (v:[13](https://ref.ly/2Chr7:13) b וְאִם); stating the ground on which a qu. is based, [Ex 8:22](https://ref.ly/Exod8:22) (וְלאֹ \= הֲלאֹ), [Je 3:1](https://ref.ly/Jer3:1), [Hg 2:12](https://ref.ly/Hag2:12) (v. הֵן Aram. **b**). † **c.** if, whether, in an indirect question, [Je 2:10](https://ref.ly/Jer2:10) רְאוּ הֵן הָֽיְתָה כָּזאֹת (but Gr הֲנִהְיְתָה); cf. אִם **2 b,** and הֵן Aram. **3\.** p. 50, 1090

> \[Str2009] הִנֵּה, once הִנֶּ־ה:[Gn 19:2](https://ref.ly/Gen19:2), **demonstr. part.** lo ! behold ! (إِنَّ *certainly, surely,* lit. *lo !*), with sf. (the pron. being conceived as accus., Ew§ 262 c.; cf., إِنَّ, which takes an accus., whether of a noun or pron. sf.) הִנְנִי [Gn 6:13](https://ref.ly/Gen6:13) \+ oft. (also הִנְּנִּי [Gn 22:7](https://ref.ly/Gen22:7)), הִֵ\+נּנִי [Gn 22:1](https://ref.ly/Gen22:1), [22:11](https://ref.ly/Gen22:11) \+, (הִנֶּ֔נִּי [Gn 27:18](https://ref.ly/Gen27:18)), † [Is 65:1](https://ref.ly/Isa65:1) הִנֵּ֣נִי הִנֵּ֔נִי (but הִנֵּה אָנֹכִי is also said [Gn 24:13](https://ref.ly/Gen24:13), [24:43](https://ref.ly/Gen24:43), [25:32](https://ref.ly/Gen25:32), [48:21](https://ref.ly/Gen48:21), [Ex 4:23](https://ref.ly/Exod4:23), [7:17](https://ref.ly/Exod7:17) \+; and, more rarely, הִנֵּה אֲנִי Ez [37:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek37:5), [37:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek37:12), [37:19](https://ref.ly/Ezek37:19), [37:21](https://ref.ly/Ezek37:21), [2 Ch 2:3](https://ref.ly/2Chr2:3), cf. [2 K 10:9](https://ref.ly/2Kgs10:9), [Je 32:27](https://ref.ly/Jer32:27)); הִנְּךָ [Gn 16:11](https://ref.ly/Gen16:11) \+ 6 t.; הִנּוֺ † [Nu 23:17](https://ref.ly/Num23:17), [Jb 2:6](https://ref.ly/Job2:6), [1 Ch 11:25](https://ref.ly/1Chr11:25), הִנֵּ֫הוּ † [Je 18:3](https://ref.ly/Jer18:3) Kt (הִנֵּה הוּא is more usual, as [Gn 20:16](https://ref.ly/Gen20:16), [42:27](https://ref.ly/Gen42:27), [1 S 10:22](https://ref.ly/1Sam10:22), [Je 38:5](https://ref.ly/Jer38:5), [Ru 3:2](https://ref.ly/Ruth3:2)): 1 pl. הִנְנוּ † [Jos 9:25](https://ref.ly/Josh9:25), [2 S 5:1](https://ref.ly/2Sam5:1), [Je 3:22](https://ref.ly/Jer3:22), [Ezr 9:15](https://ref.ly/Ezra9:15), הִנֶּ֫נּוּ † [Gn 44:16](https://ref.ly/Gen44:16), [50:18](https://ref.ly/Gen50:18), [Nu 14:40](https://ref.ly/Num14:40), הִנֵּ\+נוּ [Jb 38:35](https://ref.ly/Job38:35); הִנְּכֶם † [Dt 1:10](https://ref.ly/Deut1:10), [Je 16:12](https://ref.ly/Jer16:12); הִנָּם [Gn 40:5](https://ref.ly/Gen40:5) \+ oft. (37 t.)—*lo ! behold !* **a.** pointing to persons or things, [Gn 12:19](https://ref.ly/Gen12:19) and now הִנֵּה אִשְׁתְּךָ *behold* thy wife ! [18:9](https://ref.ly/Gen18:9) הִנֵּה בָאֹהֶל *behold* (she is) in the tent (the suffix, when the noun to which הנה refers has immediately preceded, being not unfrequently omitted, [16:14](https://ref.ly/Gen16:14): cf. Dr§ 135\. 6, 2\), [30:3](https://ref.ly/Gen30:3), [31:51](https://ref.ly/Gen31:51), [31:51](https://ref.ly/Gen31:51), [Ex 24:8](https://ref.ly/Exod24:8) etc. With sf. of 1 ps., esp. in response to a call, indicating the readiness of the person addressed to listen or obey, *Here I am !* (lit. *Behold me !)* [Gn 22:1](https://ref.ly/Gen22:1), [22:7](https://ref.ly/Gen22:7), [22:11](https://ref.ly/Gen22:11), [27:1](https://ref.ly/Gen27:1), [27:18](https://ref.ly/Gen27:18), [31:11](https://ref.ly/Gen31:11), [37:13](https://ref.ly/Gen37:13), [46:2](https://ref.ly/Gen46:2), [Ex 3:4](https://ref.ly/Exod3:4), [1 S 3:4](https://ref.ly/1Sam3:4), [3:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam3:5), [3:6](https://ref.ly/1Sam3:6), [3:8](https://ref.ly/1Sam3:8), [3:16](https://ref.ly/1Sam3:16), [22:12](https://ref.ly/1Sam22:12), [2 S 1:7](https://ref.ly/2Sam1:7), [Is 6:8](https://ref.ly/Isa6:8), cf. [1 S 12:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam12:3) הִנְנִי עֲנוּ בִי *here I am,* answer against me, [14:43](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:43) (cf. Drp. 292\), [2 S 15:26](https://ref.ly/2Sam15:26) (in resignation: cf. [Gn 44:16](https://ref.ly/Gen44:16), [50:18](https://ref.ly/Gen50:18), [Jos 9:25](https://ref.ly/Josh9:25), [Ezr 9:15](https://ref.ly/Ezra9:15)): of God [Is 52:6](https://ref.ly/Isa52:6), [58:9](https://ref.ly/Isa58:9), [65:1](https://ref.ly/Isa65:1), [65:1](https://ref.ly/Isa65:1) (repeated for emphasis). In the pl. הננו [Nu 14:40](https://ref.ly/Num14:40), [Je 3:22](https://ref.ly/Jer3:22), [Jb 38:35](https://ref.ly/Job38:35).—Emph. הִנְנִי ןָאנִי Ez [34:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:11), [34:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:20), cf. [6:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek6:3). on …חִנְנִי אֶל, v. אל **4,** p. 40\. **b.** introducing clauses involving predication: (*a*) with ref. to the past or present, it points generally to some truth either newly asserted, or newly recognized, [Gn 1:29](https://ref.ly/Gen1:29) *behold !* I have given to you all herbs etc. [17:20](https://ref.ly/Gen17:20), [27:6](https://ref.ly/Gen27:6), [1 S 14:33](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:33) etc.; often one upon which some proposal or suggestion is to be founded, [Ex 1:9](https://ref.ly/Exod1:9) (cf. הן [Gn 11:6](https://ref.ly/Gen11:6)) [1 S 20:2](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:2), [20:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:5), [2 K 5:20](https://ref.ly/2Kgs5:20). When the proposal is to be of the nature of an entreaty or request, הִנֵּה־נָא is often used, instead of the simple הִנֵּה [Gn 12:11](https://ref.ly/Gen12:11), [16:2](https://ref.ly/Gen16:2), [18:7](https://ref.ly/Gen18:7), [1 K 20:31](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:31), [22:13](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:13) al. (v. נָא). (*b*) with ref. to the future. Here it serves to introduce a solemn or important declaration [Ex 32:34](https://ref.ly/Exod32:34), [34:10](https://ref.ly/Exod34:10), [Is 7:14](https://ref.ly/Isa7:14); and is used esp. with the ptcp. (the *fut. instans,* Dr§ 135\. 3\) in predictions or threats, [Gn 20:3](https://ref.ly/Gen20:3) הִנְּךָ מֵת (lit.) *behold* thee (accus.) about to die, thou art about to die, [Ex 4:23](https://ref.ly/Exod4:23), [7:17](https://ref.ly/Exod7:17), [9:3](https://ref.ly/Exod9:3), [Dt 31:17](https://ref.ly/Deut31:17), [1 S 3:11](https://ref.ly/1Sam3:11), [1 K 20:36](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:36), [22:25](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:25), [Is 3:1](https://ref.ly/Isa3:1), [10:33](https://ref.ly/Isa10:33), [17:1](https://ref.ly/Isa17:1), [19:1](https://ref.ly/Isa19:1), [22:17](https://ref.ly/Isa22:17), [24:1](https://ref.ly/Isa24:1) \+ oft.; in the phrase הנה ימים באים † [1 S 2:31](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:31), [2 K 20:17](https://ref.ly/2Kgs20:17) \= [Is 39:6](https://ref.ly/Isa39:6), [Am 4:2](https://ref.ly/Amos4:2), [8:11](https://ref.ly/Amos8:11), [9:13](https://ref.ly/Amos9:13) \+ Je 15 t.; very often with the suffix of 1 ps. sg., as הִנְנִי מֵבִיא *Behold,* I bring (lit. *behold me bringing,* or *about to bring*)…[Gn 6:17](https://ref.ly/Gen6:17), [Ex 10:4](https://ref.ly/Exod10:4) \& often, esp. in Je; [Gn 9:9](https://ref.ly/Gen9:9), [Ex 8:17](https://ref.ly/Exod8:17), [9:18](https://ref.ly/Exod9:18), [34:11](https://ref.ly/Exod34:11), [2 K 22:20](https://ref.ly/2Kgs22:20), [Is 13:17](https://ref.ly/Isa13:17), [29:14](https://ref.ly/Isa29:14), [43:19](https://ref.ly/Isa43:19), [Je 8:17](https://ref.ly/Jer8:17), [11:22](https://ref.ly/Jer11:22) (הִנְנִי פֹקֵד; so [23:2](https://ref.ly/Jer23:2) \+) [16:16](https://ref.ly/Jer16:16), [20:4](https://ref.ly/Jer20:4), etc.; anomalously, with change of person, [Is 28:16](https://ref.ly/Isa28:16) הִנְנִי יִסַּד (acc. to points) *behold me,* one who *has founded,* [29:14](https://ref.ly/Isa29:14) הִנְנִי יוֺסִף *behold me,* one who *will add* (so [38:5](https://ref.ly/Isa38:5)); but it is dub. whether the ptcp. יֹסֵד, יוֺסֵף should not be read. **c.** …וְהִנֵּה very freq. in historical style, esp. (but not exclusively) after verbs of *seeing* or *discovering,* making the narrative graphic and vivid, and enabling the reader to enter into the surprise or satisfaction of the speaker or actor concerned: [Gn 1:31](https://ref.ly/Gen1:31) and *behold,* it was very good, [6:12](https://ref.ly/Gen6:12), [8:13](https://ref.ly/Gen8:13), [15:12](https://ref.ly/Gen15:12), [18:2](https://ref.ly/Gen18:2), [37:29](https://ref.ly/Gen37:29), [Ex 2:6](https://ref.ly/Exod2:6), [Dt 9:13](https://ref.ly/Deut9:13) etc.: in the description of a dream [Gn 37:7](https://ref.ly/Gen37:7), [37:9](https://ref.ly/Gen37:9), [40:9](https://ref.ly/Gen40:9), [40:16](https://ref.ly/Gen40:16), [41:1](https://ref.ly/Gen41:1), [41:2](https://ref.ly/Gen41:2), [41:3](https://ref.ly/Gen41:3), [Is 29:8](https://ref.ly/Isa29:8), or of a vision [Am 7:1](https://ref.ly/Amos7:1), [7:4](https://ref.ly/Amos7:4), [8:1](https://ref.ly/Amos8:1) etc. With a ptcp. (the context fixing the sense to the past), [Gn 24:30](https://ref.ly/Gen24:30), [37:15](https://ref.ly/Gen37:15) ,(both without suffix); Ju [9:43](https://ref.ly/Judg9:43), [11:34](https://ref.ly/Judg11:34), [1 K 19:5](https://ref.ly/1Kgs19:5), [19:11](https://ref.ly/1Kgs19:11) \+. **d.** like II. הֵן (**b**), nearly \= *if* (rare): [Lv 13:5](https://ref.ly/Lev13:5), [13:6](https://ref.ly/Lev13:6), [13:7](https://ref.ly/Lev13:7), [13:8](https://ref.ly/Lev13:8) (\& elsewhere in this and the next ch.) וְהִנֵּה *and behold \= and if,* [Dt 13:15](https://ref.ly/Deut13:15) and [17:4](https://ref.ly/Deut17:4) וְהִנֵּה אֱמֶת *and behold* it is true \= *and if* it be true, [19:18](https://ref.ly/Deut19:18), [1 S 20:12](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:12); cf. [1 S 9:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam9:7), [2 S 18:11](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:11), [Ho 9:6](https://ref.ly/Hos9:6).

> \[Str2008] I. הֵ֫נָּה:49 **adv.** hither (perh. from II. הֵן, used δεικτικῶς, with ה\- *loc.,* prop. *lo hitherwards !* or perh. akin to هُنَا, هَنَّا, هِنَّا *here*)—**a.** of *place: (a)* hither [Gn 15:16](https://ref.ly/Gen15:16) יָשׁוּבוּ הֵנָּךְ, [42:15](https://ref.ly/Gen42:15), [45:5](https://ref.ly/Gen45:5) that you have sold me הֵנָּה *hither,* v:[8](https://ref.ly/Gen45:8), v:[13](https://ref.ly/Gen45:13), [Jos 2:2](https://ref.ly/Josh2:2), [3:9](https://ref.ly/Josh3:9), [18:6](https://ref.ly/Josh18:6), [2 S 1:10](https://ref.ly/2Sam1:10), [Is 57:3](https://ref.ly/Isa57:3) וְאַתֶּם קִרְבוּ הֵנָּה, [Je 31:8](https://ref.ly/Jer31:8), [51:5](https://ref.ly/Jer51:5) \+; [1 S 20:21](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:21) מִמְּךָ וָהֵנָּךְ from thee *and hitherwards,* i.e. on this side of thee (opp. מִמְּךָ וָהָֽלְאָה): repeated [2 K 4:35](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:35) אַחַת הֵנָּה וְאַחַת הֵנָּה once *hither* and once *thither* \= once to *and fro;* הֵנָּה וָהֵנָּה *hither and thither* i.e. in different (or opposite) directions, † [Jos 8:20](https://ref.ly/Josh8:20), [2 K 2:8](https://ref.ly/2Kgs2:8), [2:14](https://ref.ly/2Kgs2:14), [1 K 20:40](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:40) strangely וַיְהִי עַבְדְּךָ עשֵֹׁה הֵנָּח וָהֵנָּה *lit.* and thy servant was a *doer of hitherwards and thitherwards,* i.e. was engaged in different directions (Ew§ 360, c treats ´ה here as the pron. 3 pl. fem. construed irregularly, cf. עשֵֹׁה אֵלֶּה: but rd. prob. with LXX Targum SerVer Vulgate Th Klo פֹּנֶה *was turning* or *looking* ([Ex 2:12](https://ref.ly/Exod2:12)) for עשֵֹׁךְ); עַד־הֵנָּה *even hither,* [Nu 14:19](https://ref.ly/Num14:19), [1 S 7:12](https://ref.ly/1Sam7:12), [2 S 20:16](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:16) קְרַב עַד־הֵנָּה, [2 K 8:7](https://ref.ly/2Kgs8:7), *to this point* (in a book) [Je 48:47](https://ref.ly/Jer48:47), [51:64](https://ref.ly/Jer51:64) (note of compiler or scribe). (β) *here* (cf. שָׁמָּה \= *there*) [Gn 21:23](https://ref.ly/Gen21:23) swear to me הֵנָּה *here,* [Dn 12:5](https://ref.ly/Dan12:5) ´הֵנָּה לְ… ´חֵנָּה לְ *on this side* of…*on that side* of…† **b.** of *time,* in עַד הֵנָּה *hitherto* [Gn 15:16](https://ref.ly/Gen15:16) (with a neg., \= not *yet*) [44:28](https://ref.ly/Gen44:28), Ju [16:13](https://ref.ly/Judg16:13), [Psalm 71:17](https://ref.ly/Ps71:17) \& *until now* do I keep declaring thy wonders, [1 Ch 9:18](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:18), [12:9](https://ref.ly/1Chr12:9) *until now* (the point reached in the narrative). In late Heb., contracted to עֲדֶ֫נָה *hitherto, still* † [Ec 4:2](https://ref.ly/Eccl4:2), עֲדֶן † [Ec 4:3](https://ref.ly/Eccl4:3) אֲשֶׁר עֲדֶן לאֹ הָיָה \= who has not *yet* been (cf. Mish. עֲדַיִן *hitherto, still,* עֲדַיִן לאֹ \= *not yet*).

> \[Str2011] † הִנֹּם **n.pr.m.** (deriv. \& mng. dub.; acc. to Sim Bö Gf al. \= *wailing,* Ar. هَنَّ fr. cries of children (v. infr.), but this improbable)—only in ´בֶן־ה(גֵּיא)גֵּי, \& abbrev. ´ה ´ג, **n.pr.loc.** of valley S. of Jerusalem, (LXX φάραγγα Ὀνομ [Jos 15:8](https://ref.ly/Josh15:8), [15:8](https://ref.ly/Josh15:8) Σονναμ [18:16](https://ref.ly/Josh18:16); Ταιεννα v:[16](https://ref.ly/Josh18:16); Ταιβενθομ [2 Ch 28:3](https://ref.ly/2Chr28:3), LXXL φάρ. Βενεννομ; γὲ βανὲ Ἑννομ) [33:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr33:6), LXXL γν͂ Βενεννομ; elsewh. usu. φάρ. (νἱοῦ) Ἑννομ)—cf. sub גַּיְא;—as mere topographical term גֵּי בֶן־הִנֹּם, boundary between Judah \& Benjamin [Jos 15:8](https://ref.ly/Josh15:8), [18:16](https://ref.ly/Josh18:16); also ´גֵּי ה [15:8](https://ref.ly/Josh15:8), [18:16](https://ref.ly/Josh18:16) (all P) cf. [Ne 11:30](https://ref.ly/Neh11:30); elsewhere alw. ´בֶן־ה ´גּ ([2 K 23:10](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:10) Kt has הנם גי בני, Qr \& LXX Vulgate sing.), \& alw. with ref. to the sacrifice of children by fire, [2 K 23:10](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:10), [2 Ch 28:3](https://ref.ly/2Chr28:3), [33:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr33:6), [Je 7:31](https://ref.ly/Jer7:31), [7:32](https://ref.ly/Jer7:32), [19:2](https://ref.ly/Jer19:2), [19:6](https://ref.ly/Jer19:6), [32:35](https://ref.ly/Jer32:35); on account of this inhuman \& idolatrous practice it was abhorred by priest \& prophet, \& defiled by Josiah; this conception afterward developed, through [Is 66:24](https://ref.ly/Isa66:24), into Jewish *Gehinnom* (cf. WeberSynagog. Theol. 326 ff. DIBuch Henoch. 131 f.) \& NT γέεννα. On locality cf. RobBR ii. 273 f. ToblerTopogr. ii 39 ff. BDPal 103; mod. name (of lower half of valley, toward SE.) *Wady er\-Rabâbi.*

> \[Str2012] † הֵנַע **n.pr.** *loc.* named betw. *Sepharvaim* and *Iwwa;* site unknown, yet cf. DlPa 279; [2 K 18:34](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:34), [19:13](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:13) \= [Is 37:13](https://ref.ly/Isa37:13).

> \[Str5130] הֲנָפָה v. sub נוף. p. 632

## הַס (id: BDB02452)

\[Str2013,2013] † הַס, הָס **interj.** hush! keep silence (prob. onomatop.) Ju [3:19](https://ref.ly/Judg3:19) and he said, I have a secret errand unto thee, O king, וַיּאֹמֶר הָ֔ס and he said, *Keep silence!* [Am 6:10](https://ref.ly/Amos6:10) וְאָמַר הָ֔ס (while burying men in a pestilence), [8:3](https://ref.ly/Amos8:3) (ellipt.) in every place: הִשְׁלִיח הָֽס they cast them (the corpses) forth, (saying) *Hysh !* sq. ´מִפְּנֵי י (מִפָּנָיו) Hb [2:20](https://ref.ly/Hab2:20), [Zp 1:7](https://ref.ly/Zeph1:7), [Zc 2:17](https://ref.ly/Zech2:17). Inflected, as though a verb, *imv. pl.* הַ֔סּוּ [Ne 8:11](https://ref.ly/Neh8:11) (Ges compares Arab. هَلُمَّ \[\= הֲלֽם] *hither!* inflected as an imv., e.g. هَلُمِّى, هَلُمُّوا: WAG i. § 368\). Hence as denom.

> \[Str2014] הֲפֻגָה v. sub פוג. p. 806
