## הָפַךְ (id: BDB02454)

\[Str2015] † הָפַךְ **vb.** turn, overturn (NH *id.,* Aram. הֲפַח, ܗܦܰܟ, Ar. أَفَكَ WSG 47, As. *abâku, apâku* DlW)—**Qal** *Pf.* [Lv 13:3](https://ref.ly/Lev13:3) \+ 13 t.; וְהָפַח consec. [2 K 21:13](https://ref.ly/2Kgs21:13), [Jb 34:25](https://ref.ly/Job34:25); 3 mpl. הָֽפְכוּ [Psalm 78:9](https://ref.ly/Ps78:9); הֲפַכְתֶּם [Am 6:12](https://ref.ly/Amos6:12), [Je 23:36](https://ref.ly/Jer23:36), etc.; *Impf.* יַהֲפֹח [La 3:3](https://ref.ly/Lam3:3), [Je 13:23](https://ref.ly/Jer13:23); וַיַּהֲפֹח [Gn 19:25](https://ref.ly/Gen19:25) \+ 6 t.; יַּהֲפָ־ח [1 S 10:9](https://ref.ly/1Sam10:9); sf. וַיַּהַפְכֵהוּ Ju [7:13](https://ref.ly/Judg7:13); 3 fs. וַתַּהֲפֹח [2 Ch 9:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr9:12); אֶהְפֹּח [Zp 3:9](https://ref.ly/Zeph3:9); וַיַּ הַפְכוּ [1 S 25:12](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:12), [Jb 12:15](https://ref.ly/Job12:15); *Imv.* הֲפֹח [1 K 22:34](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:34) \= [2 Ch 18:33](https://ref.ly/2Chr18:33); *Inf. abs.* הָפוֺח [Pr 12:7](https://ref.ly/Prov12:7); *cstr.* הֲפֹח [Gn 19:29](https://ref.ly/Gen19:29), [1 Ch 19:3](https://ref.ly/1Chr19:3); sf. הָפְכִּי [Gn 19:21](https://ref.ly/Gen19:21); הָפְכָהּ [2 S 10:3](https://ref.ly/2Sam10:3); —on הַפְכְּכֶם (so Baer, q. v.) [Is 29:16](https://ref.ly/Isa29:16), v. הֶפֶח; *Pt. act.* הֹפֵח [Am 5:8](https://ref.ly/Amos5:8); הַהֹפְכִי [Psalm 114:8](https://ref.ly/Ps114:8); pl. הַהֹפְכִים [Am 5:7](https://ref.ly/Amos5:7); *pass.* הֲפוּכָה [Ho 7:8](https://ref.ly/Hos7:8), [La 4:6](https://ref.ly/Lam4:6).—**1\.** trans. **a.** *turn, turn about, turn over,* c. acc.: e.g. turn the back (עֹרֶף) [Jos 7:8](https://ref.ly/Josh7:8) (JE); a dish (צַלַּחַת) עַל־פָּנֶיהָ [2 K 21:13](https://ref.ly/2Kgs21:13); the hand, \& so the horses of a chariot [1 K 22:34](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:34) \= [2 Ch 18:33](https://ref.ly/2Chr18:33), [2 K 9:23](https://ref.ly/2Kgs9:23); but also (sq. בְּ) of hostility [La 3:3](https://ref.ly/Lam3:3); turn a cake (pass.) [Ho 7:8](https://ref.ly/Hos7:8); of ´י, שָפָה בְּרוּרָה ´ה [Zp 3:9](https://ref.ly/Zeph3:9) i.e. restore speech of a pure kind, sq. אֶל־עַמִּים; לֵב אַחֵר [1 S 10:9](https://ref.ly/1Sam10:9) *another heart,* sq. לוֺ; turn a wind (רוּחַ; i.e. bring from different quarter) [Ex 10:19](https://ref.ly/Exod10:19) (J). **b.** *overturn, overthrow,* sq. acc. Sodom, etc., [Gn 19:21](https://ref.ly/Gen19:21), [19:25](https://ref.ly/Gen19:25), [19:29](https://ref.ly/Gen19:29) (J), [Dt 29:22](https://ref.ly/Deut29:22), [Je 20:16](https://ref.ly/Jer20:16), [La 4:6](https://ref.ly/Lam4:6) (cf. ´מַהְפֵּכַת וגו [Am 4:11](https://ref.ly/Amos4:11) v. infr.; also הֲפֵכָה, \[מַהְפֵּכָה]); city of Ammon [2 S 10:3](https://ref.ly/2Sam10:3) \= [1 Ch 19:3](https://ref.ly/1Chr19:3) (obj. om.); mountains [Jb 9:5](https://ref.ly/Job9:5), [28:9](https://ref.ly/Job28:9); throne [Hg 2:22](https://ref.ly/Hag2:22) (‖הִשְׁמִיד), chariots v:[22](https://ref.ly/Hag2:22); tent (אֹהֶל), לְמַ֫עְלָה ´ה Ju [7:3](https://ref.ly/Judg7:3); obj. earth (´ה \= *devastate*) [Jb 12:15](https://ref.ly/Job12:15); wicked men [Am 4:11](https://ref.ly/Amos4:11) (יֲמֹרָה הָפַכְּ תִּי בָּכֶם כְּמַהְפֵּכַת אֱלֽהִים אֶת־סְדֹם וְאֶ־ת) [Pr 12:7](https://ref.ly/Prov12:7), [Jb 34:25](https://ref.ly/Job34:25) (obj. om.). **c.** *turn \= change, transform* (1\) sq. acc. \= *alter;* a colour (עֵינוֺ) [Lv 13:55](https://ref.ly/Lev13:55) (P); skin [Je 13:33](https://ref.ly/Jer13:33); bed (מִשְׁכָּבוֺ v. ´מ; Che *his lying down*) [Psalm 41:4](https://ref.ly/Ps41:4) i.e. restore to health; (2\) *pervert* דִבְרֵי אֱלֽהִים [Je 23:36](https://ref.ly/Jer23:36); (3\) sq. acc. c. Inf. [Psalm 105:25](https://ref.ly/Ps105:25) *he turned their heart to hate his people;* (4\) *turn to, into,* sq. two acc. [Lv 13:10](https://ref.ly/Lev13:10), [Psalm 114:8](https://ref.ly/Ps114:8) rock into pool of water; usu. sq. acc. \+ לְ, [Psalm 66:6](https://ref.ly/Ps66:6) sea into dry land, [Psalm 78:44](https://ref.ly/Ps78:44) rivers into blood, cf. [105:29](https://ref.ly/Ps105:29); [Je 31:13](https://ref.ly/Jer31:13) mourning into joy, so [Psalm 30:12](https://ref.ly/Ps30:12); [Am 5:7](https://ref.ly/Amos5:7) justice into wormwood, cf. [6:12](https://ref.ly/Amos6:12); [8:10](https://ref.ly/Amos8:10) feasts into mourning; [Dt 23:6](https://ref.ly/Deut23:6) \= [Ne 13:2](https://ref.ly/Neh13:2) curse into blessing; [Am 5:8](https://ref.ly/Amos5:8) death\-shade into morning. **2\.** *intrans.* **a.** *turn, turn back,* Ju [20:39](https://ref.ly/Judg20:39), [20:41](https://ref.ly/Judg20:41), [2 K 5:26](https://ref.ly/2Kgs5:26), [Psalm 78:9](https://ref.ly/Ps78:9), [2 Ch 9:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr9:12); sq. לדרך [1 S 25:12](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:12); **b.** *turn \= change, change into,* sq. pred. adj. לָבָן ´ה turn white [Lv 13:3](https://ref.ly/Lev13:3), [13:4](https://ref.ly/Lev13:4), [13:13](https://ref.ly/Lev13:13), [13:20](https://ref.ly/Lev13:20) (all P). **Niph.** *Pf.* נֶהְפַּח [Ex 7:15](https://ref.ly/Exod7:15) \+ 10 t.; נֶהְפָּח [Jb 20:14](https://ref.ly/Job20:14); וְנֶהְפַּךְ consec. [Lv 13:16](https://ref.ly/Lev13:16); נֶהֶפְכָה [La 5:2](https://ref.ly/Lam5:2); 3 mpl. נֶהְפְּכוּ [Jb 19:19](https://ref.ly/Job19:19) \+ 3 t., נֶהֶפְכוּ [1 S 4:19](https://ref.ly/1Sam4:19), [Dn 10:16](https://ref.ly/Dan10:16), וְנֶהֶפְכוּ consec. [Ex 7:17](https://ref.ly/Exod7:17), [Is 34:9](https://ref.ly/Isa34:9), etc.; *Impf.* יֵהָפֵח [Is 60:5](https://ref.ly/Isa60:5), [Jo 3:4](https://ref.ly/Joel3:4); וַיֵּהָפֵח [Ex 14:5](https://ref.ly/Exod14:5), [Is 63:10](https://ref.ly/Isa63:10); 2 ms. תֵּהָפֵח [Jb 30:21](https://ref.ly/Job30:21), Ez [4:8](https://ref.ly/Ezek4:8); וַיֵּהָֽפְכוּ [Ex 7:20](https://ref.ly/Exod7:20); *Inf. abs.* נַהֲפוֺח [Est 9:1](https://ref.ly/Esth9:1); *Pt.* נֶהְפָּח [Pr 17:20](https://ref.ly/Prov17:20); נֶהְפָּכֶת [Jon 3:4](https://ref.ly/Jonah3:4); —**1\.** reflex. \& intrans. **a.** *turn oneself, turn, turn back* (cf. **Qal 2 a**) [Jos 8:20](https://ref.ly/Josh8:20) (JE); *turn aside* [Psalm 78:57](https://ref.ly/Ps78:57); *turn* from side to side Ez [4:8](https://ref.ly/Ezek4:8); *pains turned upon* (עַל) *her* [1 S 4:19](https://ref.ly/1Sam4:19) (i.e. came suddenly upon her) cf. [Dn 10:16](https://ref.ly/Dan10:16); *turn against* sq. בְּ [Jb 19:19](https://ref.ly/Job19:19); *turn to* (*ward*), sq. עַל, [Is 60:5](https://ref.ly/Isa60:5) (‖בא לְ). **b.** *turn \= change* (oneself) sq. pred. adj. [Lv 13:25](https://ref.ly/Lev13:25) (P); sq. pred. noun (?), vine turns into degenerate plant [Je 2:21](https://ref.ly/Jer2:21); no pred. expressed [Jb 20:14](https://ref.ly/Job20:14) (cf. vb); of heart turned in compassion [Ho 11:8](https://ref.ly/Hos11:8) (‖נִכְמְרוּ נִחוּמָי), in sorrow, distress, [La 1:20](https://ref.ly/Lam1:20); of heart changed, so as to be favourably disposed *toward* (sq. אֶל) [Ex 14:5](https://ref.ly/Exod14:5) (J) \= change of mind; sq. לְ, of plague spots changing colour [Lv 13:16](https://ref.ly/Lev13:16), [13:17](https://ref.ly/Lev13:17) (P); rod changing to serpent [Ex 7:15](https://ref.ly/Exod7:15) (P); faces to paleness [Je 30:6](https://ref.ly/Jer30:6); dance to mourning [La 5:15](https://ref.ly/Lam5:15); comeliness to corruption [Dn 10:8](https://ref.ly/Dan10:8); וַיֵּהָפֵח לָהֶם לְאוֺיֵב [Is 63:10](https://ref.ly/Isa63:10), [Jb 30:21](https://ref.ly/Job30:21). **c.** *be perverse,* only pt. used subst. בִּלְשׁוֺנוֺ ‖נֶהְפָּ [Pr 17:20](https://ref.ly/Prov17:20) *he that is perverse with his tongue.* **2\.** pass., **a.** *be turned, turned over to* sq. לְ, an inheritance to strangers [La 5:2](https://ref.ly/Lam5:2). **b.** *be reversed* [Est 9:1](https://ref.ly/Esth9:1). **c.** *be turned, changed,* sq. לְ, waters into blood [Ex 7:17](https://ref.ly/Exod7:17), [7:20](https://ref.ly/Exod7:20) (E); Saul into another man [1 S 10:6](https://ref.ly/1Sam10:6); streams into pitch [Is 34:9](https://ref.ly/Isa34:9); sun to darkness [Jo 3:4](https://ref.ly/Joel3:4); stones to chaff [Jb 41:20](https://ref.ly/Job41:20); month changed from sorrow to gladness [Est 9:22](https://ref.ly/Esth9:22); cf. [Psalm 32:4](https://ref.ly/Ps32:4) where complem. om. **d.** *be overturned, overthrown,* of city Nineveh [Jon 3:4](https://ref.ly/Jonah3:4). **e.** *be upturned,* of subterran. work of miners [Jb 28:5](https://ref.ly/Job28:5) \= *there is an upturning.* **Hithp.** *Impf.* 3 fs. תִּתְהַפֵּח [Jb 38:14](https://ref.ly/Job38:14); *Pt.* מִתְהַפֵּח Ju [7:13](https://ref.ly/Judg7:13), [Jb 37:12](https://ref.ly/Job37:12); מִתְהַפֶּכֶת [Gn 3:24](https://ref.ly/Gen3:24); —reflex. \& intrans.: **1\.** *turn this way* \& *that, every way,* of the flaming sword [Gn 3:24](https://ref.ly/Gen3:24) (J); storm\-cloud [Jb 37:12](https://ref.ly/Job37:12); *turn over \& over* Ju [7:13](https://ref.ly/Judg7:13), of bread\-cake tumbling into the host of Midian. **2\.** *transform oneself,* [Jb 38:14](https://ref.ly/Job38:14) of earth under rising dawn. **Hoph.** *Pf.* הָהְפַּח עָלַי בַּלָּהוֺת [Jb 30:15](https://ref.ly/Job30:15) *there have been turned upon me terrors* (cf. הִתְגַּלְָ\+גּלוּ v:[14](https://ref.ly/Job30:14), of foes).

> \[Str2016–17] † הֶ֫פֶךְ **n.m.** the contrary, contrariness, perversity;—abs. ´ה Ez [16:34](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:34); הֶָפֶךְ v:[34](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:34); sf. הַפְכְּכֶם [Is 29:16](https://ref.ly/Isa29:16) (v. Baer);—**1\.** *the contrary, opposite thing* וַיְהִי־בָח הֶפֶךְ מִן־הֵנָּשִׁים Ez [16:34](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:34) *\& there hath occurred in thee the contrary from other women;* v:[34](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:34) וַתְּהִי לְהֶָפֶח *so thou hast become the contrary.* **2\.** הַפְכְּכֶם [Is 29:16](https://ref.ly/Isa29:16) *Oh, your perversity !*

> \[Str2018] † הֲפֵכָה **n.f.** overthrow (cf. esp. As. *abiktu* DlW), of the cities (of the plain) [Gn 19:29](https://ref.ly/Gen19:29); cf. \[מַהְפֵּכָה] and הָפַח **1 b.**

> \[Str2019] † הֲפַכְפַּךְ **adj.** crooked, הֲפַכְפַּךְ דֶּרֶךְ אִישׁ וָזָרָ [Pr 21:8](https://ref.ly/Prov21:8) *crooked is the way of a guilty man.*

> \[Str4114] † \[מַהְפֵּכָה] **n.f.** overthrow—always cstr. מַהְפֵּכַת; \& always of overthrow of Sodom, Gomorrah etc., exc. [Is 1:7](https://ref.ly/Isa1:7) זָרִים ´מ, where rd. סְדֹם (Ew Che RSProph. 345 Di al.; also LagSem. i. 3\); yet even so prob. gloss, cf. Stud.:JPTh 1877, 714; סְדֹם ´מ [Dt 29:22](https://ref.ly/Deut29:22), [Je 49:18](https://ref.ly/Jer49:18); and with force of verbal noun, governing acc., אלהים את־סדם ´מ [Is 13:19](https://ref.ly/Isa13:19), [Je 50:40](https://ref.ly/Jer50:40), [Am 4:11](https://ref.ly/Amos4:11). Cf. also הָפַח **1 b,** הֲפֵכָה.

> \[Str4115] † מַהְפֶּ֫כֶת **n.f.** stocks or similar instrum. of punishment (compelling *crooked* posture, or *distorting*), mentioned rather late; as punishment for Jeremiah אֲשֶׁר בְּשֹׁעַר בִּנְיָמִן ´הַמּ [Je 20:2](https://ref.ly/Jer20:2) (LXX εἱς τὸν καταράκτην κ.τ.λ.), cf. ´אֶל־הַמּ [29:26](https://ref.ly/Jer29:26) (LXX εἰς τὸ ἀπόκλεισμα; ‖אֶל־הַצִּינֹק, LXX εἰς τὸν καταράκτην, but order of words perhaps reversed in LXX); הַמַּהְפָּכֶת [20:3](https://ref.ly/Jer20:3); בֵּית הַמַּהְפֶּכֶת assigned also to Asa's time [2 Ch 16:10](https://ref.ly/2Chr16:10) (LXX εἰς φυλακήν; LXXL εἰς οἶκον φυλακῆς; cf. [Acts 16:24](https://ref.ly/Acts16:24), where εἰς τὴν ἐσωτέραν φυλακήν, \+ εἰς τὸ ξύλον).

> \[Str5419] † \[תַּהְפֻּכָה] **n.f.** perversity, perverse thing (only Pr exc. [Dt 32:20](https://ref.ly/Deut32:20))—Pl. abs. תַּהְפֻּכוֺת [Dt 32:20](https://ref.ly/Deut32:20) \+ 8 t.; cstr. id. [Pr 2:14](https://ref.ly/Prov2:14); —*perverse things,* particularly utterances [Pr 2:12](https://ref.ly/Prov2:12), [10:32](https://ref.ly/Prov10:32), [23:33](https://ref.ly/Prov23:33), cf. ´פִּי ת [8:13](https://ref.ly/Prov8:13), ´לְשׁוֺן ת [10:31](https://ref.ly/Prov10:31), and even ´אִישׁ ת [Pr 16:28](https://ref.ly/Prov16:28) (‖נִרְגָּן *slanderer*); but also thoughts, devices [Pr 6:14](https://ref.ly/Prov6:14), [16:30](https://ref.ly/Prov16:30); ´דּוֺר ת [Dt 32:20](https://ref.ly/Deut32:20) \= *perverse generation,* (‖לאֹ־אֵמֻן בָּם), רָע ´ת [Pr 2:14](https://ref.ly/Prov2:14) (‖עֲשוֺת רָע).

> \[Str2020] הַצָּלָה v. sub נחל. p. 665

> \[Str2021] † הֹ֫צֶן **n.\[m.]** deriv. \& mng. dub.; only Ez [23:24](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:24) וּבָאוּ עָלַיִח הֹצֶן רֶכֶב וְגַלְגַּל; txt. prob. in error: LXX מִצָּפוֺן so Co (cf. [26:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek26:7)); SyrVer Targum *with arms;* Hi הָצֵן, Inf. Hiph. of √ צנן, *with rattling;* Ew חֹצֶן (so Codd.) *with shoulder* as place of carrying weapon; Bö Sm חֲמוֺן *multitude;* DlBaer's Ezech. xi. gains like sense without emend. by comparing As. *êṣin* (*\-ši*) *collect, gather,* Flood Tabl.:ii. 25 ff., but this very dubious.

> \[Str2023] † הֹר **n.pr.mont. 1\.** mt. on border of Edom, alw. הֹר הָהָר, named as stage in Isr.'s journey to Canaan [Nu 20:22](https://ref.ly/Num20:22), [21:4](https://ref.ly/Num21:4), [33:37](https://ref.ly/Num33:37), [Dt 32:50](https://ref.ly/Deut32:50); as place of Aaron's death [Nu 20:23](https://ref.ly/Num20:23), [20:25](https://ref.ly/Num20:25), [20:27](https://ref.ly/Num20:27), [33:38](https://ref.ly/Num33:38), [33:39](https://ref.ly/Num33:39), [33:41](https://ref.ly/Num33:41), [Dt 32:50](https://ref.ly/Deut32:50) (all P); \= mod. *Jebel Nebi Hârûn* c. 50 miles S. of Dead Sea, just S. (SW.) of Petra, acc. to RobBR ii. 125, 152, cf. 519 ff. BDPal 153; disputed by Ew Kn Di, \& esp. TrumbullKadesh Barnea 128 ff., who thinks of *J. Madurah,* NW. of Edom. **2\. a** NE. spur of Lebanon [Nu 34:7](https://ref.ly/Num34:7), [34:8](https://ref.ly/Num34:8) (P); mod. *Jebel Akkar* cf. PorterDamascus, ed. 2, p. 333 NbrGeogr. du Talm. p. 9 FurrerZPV viii. 27; yet v. Di.

[Nu 11:15](https://ref.ly/Num11:15), [11:15](https://ref.ly/Num11:15) (JE), [22:33](https://ref.ly/Num22:33) (J), [Is 14:30](https://ref.ly/Isa14:30), [26:21](https://ref.ly/Isa26:21) (pass. *the slain,* so [27:7](https://ref.ly/Isa27:7)), [Je 15:3](https://ref.ly/Jer15:3); fig. הֲרַגְתִּים בְּאִמְרֵי־פִָי [Ho 6:5](https://ref.ly/Hos6:5) (‖בַּנְּבִיאִים חָצַבְתִּי). **3\.** rarely of judicial killing by men (at God's command), [Ex 32:27](https://ref.ly/Exod32:27) (JE), [Lv 20:15](https://ref.ly/Lev20:15), [20:16](https://ref.ly/Lev20:16) (H), [Nu 25:5](https://ref.ly/Num25:5) (JE), [Dt 13:10](https://ref.ly/Deut13:10), [13:10](https://ref.ly/Deut13:10) cf. Ez [9:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek9:6) תַּהַרְגוּ לְמַשְׁחִית. **4\.** of killing beasts, [Nu 22:29](https://ref.ly/Num22:29) (J; Balaam's ass), [Lv 20:15](https://ref.ly/Lev20:15) cf. supr., [Is 22:13](https://ref.ly/Isa22:13) (oxen; ‖שָׁחֹט), הִתַּנִּין אֲשֶׁר בַָּ\+יּם [Is 27:1](https://ref.ly/Isa27:1); also of killing vines, by hail [Psalm 78:47](https://ref.ly/Ps78:47). **5\.** of killing by beasts: lions [2 K 17:25](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:25), viper [Jb 20:16](https://ref.ly/Job20:16). **6\.** quite general is עֵת לַהֲרוֺג וְעֵת לִרְפּוֺא [Ec 3:3](https://ref.ly/Eccl3:3). **7\.** *destroy, ruin* לֶאֱוִיל יַהֲרָג־כָּעַש וּפֹתֶה תָּמִית קִנְאָה [Jb 5:2](https://ref.ly/Job5:2); מְשׁוּבַת פְּתָיִם תַּהַרְגֵם וְשַׁלְוַת כְּ סִילִים תְּאַבְּדֵם [Pr 1:32](https://ref.ly/Prov1:32); also of those ruined by shameless woman [Pr 7:26](https://ref.ly/Prov7:26).—Regular construction is c. acc.; obj. sometimes om., as [La 2:21](https://ref.ly/Lam2:21); used abs. [Ho 9:13](https://ref.ly/Hos9:13), [Je 4:31](https://ref.ly/Jer4:31), [15:3](https://ref.ly/Jer15:3), [La 3:43](https://ref.ly/Lam3:43), Ez [21:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:16), [Ec 3:3](https://ref.ly/Eccl3:3); sq. בְּ *slay among,* i.e. some of, [2 K 17:25](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:25), [2 Ch 28:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr28:6), [28:9](https://ref.ly/2Chr28:9), [Psalm 78:31](https://ref.ly/Ps78:31); sq. מִן (part of), \+ acc., [1 Ch 19:18](https://ref.ly/1Chr19:18); sq. dir. obj. c. לְ † [2 S 3:30](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:30), [Jb 5:2](https://ref.ly/Job5:2), [Psalm 135:11](https://ref.ly/Ps135:11) \= [136:19](https://ref.ly/Ps136:19), [136:20](https://ref.ly/Ps136:20) (but in last 3 first obj. is acc.). **Niph.** *Impf.* **1\.** pass. of **Qal 1 a,** יֵהָרֵג [La 2:20](https://ref.ly/Lam2:20) *shall priest and prophet be slain in the sanctuary?* **2\.** pass. of **Qal 1 b,** 3 fpl. תֵּהָרַגְנָה Ez [26:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek26:6); also Ez [26:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek26:15) בֵּהָרֵג הֶרֶג ( \= בְּהֵהָרֵג), but LXX Co בַּהֲרֹג חֶרֶב. **Pu.** *Pf.* 3 ms. הֹרָג [Is 27:7](https://ref.ly/Isa27:7) *be slain* (pass. of **Qal 1 b**); cf. 1 pl. הֹרַגְנוּ [Psalm 44:23](https://ref.ly/Ps44:23) (‖נֶחְשַׁבְּנוּ כְּצאֹן טִבְחָה).

## I הָרָה (id: BDB02471)

\[Str2029] † I. הָרָה **vb.** conceive, become pregnant (As. ἐρὐ cf. Dl:Pr 21 Muss. ArnoltHbr. Oct. 1890, 67 JägerBAS i. 473\);—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 ms. וְהָרָה consec. [Psalm 7:15](https://ref.ly/Ps7:15); 3 fs. הָרָתָה [Gn 16:4](https://ref.ly/Gen16:4), [16:5](https://ref.ly/Gen16:5); 2 fs. וְהָרִית consec. Ju [13:3](https://ref.ly/Judg13:3); 1 s. הָרִיתִי [Nu 11:12](https://ref.ly/Num11:12); 1 pl. הָרִינוּ [Is 26:18](https://ref.ly/Isa26:18); *Impf.* וַתַּ֫הַר [Gn 4:1](https://ref.ly/Gen4:1) \+ 26 t. (וַתַָּהַר [Gn 16:4](https://ref.ly/Gen16:4)); 3 fpl. וַתַּהֲרֶיןָ [Gn 19:36](https://ref.ly/Gen19:36); 2 mpl. תַּהֲרוּ [Is 33:11](https://ref.ly/Isa33:11); *Inf. abs.* הָרֹה [Jb 15:35](https://ref.ly/Job15:35), הָרוֺ [Is 59:4](https://ref.ly/Isa59:4) cf. also v:[13](https://ref.ly/Isa59:13) sub **Po.** infr.; *Pt.* f. sf. הוֺרָתִי Ct [3:4](https://ref.ly/Song3:4); הוֺרָתָם [Ho 2:7](https://ref.ly/Hos2:7); —**1\.** lit. *conceive, become pregnant* [Gn 16:4](https://ref.ly/Gen16:4), [16:4](https://ref.ly/Gen16:4), [16:5](https://ref.ly/Gen16:5), [19](https://ref.ly/Gen16:19); [36](https://ref.ly/Gen16:36), [25:21](https://ref.ly/Gen25:21), [38:18](https://ref.ly/Gen38:18) (all J), [2 S 11:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam11:5) (agency of man expressed by מִן [Gn 19:36](https://ref.ly/Gen19:36), לְ [38:18](https://ref.ly/Gen38:18)); usu. in phr. וַתַּהַר וַתֵּלֶד [Gn 4:1](https://ref.ly/Gen4:1), [4:17](https://ref.ly/Gen4:17), [21:2](https://ref.ly/Gen21:2), [29:32](https://ref.ly/Gen29:32), [29:33](https://ref.ly/Gen29:33), [29:34](https://ref.ly/Gen29:34), [29:35](https://ref.ly/Gen29:35), [30:5](https://ref.ly/Gen30:5), [30:7](https://ref.ly/Gen30:7) (all J), v:[17](https://ref.ly/Gen30:17), v:[19](https://ref.ly/Gen30:19), v:[23](https://ref.ly/Gen30:23) (all E), [38:3](https://ref.ly/Gen38:3), [38:4](https://ref.ly/Gen38:4) (both J), [Ex 2:2](https://ref.ly/Exod2:2) (E), [1 S 1:20](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:20), [2:21](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:21), [2 K 4:17](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:17), [1 Ch 7:23](https://ref.ly/1Chr7:23), [Is 8:3](https://ref.ly/Isa8:3), [Ho 1:3](https://ref.ly/Hos1:3), [1:6](https://ref.ly/Hos1:6), [1:8](https://ref.ly/Hos1:8), prob. also [1 Ch 4:17](https://ref.ly/1Chr4:17) ותהר אֶת־מִרְיָם \[ותלד] cf. Be; further Ju [13:3](https://ref.ly/Judg13:3), and cf. Moses' question הֶאָנֹכִי הָרִיתִי אֵת כָּל־הָעָם הַזֶּה אִם־אָנֹכִי יְלדְתִּיהוּ [Nu 11:12](https://ref.ly/Num11:12) *have I conceived all this people, or have I brought it forth?* Pt. f. ‖אֵם [Ho 2:7](https://ref.ly/Hos2:7), Ct [3:4](https://ref.ly/Song3:4). **2\.** metaph. הָרִינוּ חַלְנוּ כְמוֺ יָלַדְנוּ רוּחַ [Is 26:18](https://ref.ly/Isa26:18) of anxious and disappointed waiting; תַּהֲרוּ חֲשַׁשׁ תֵּלְדוּ קַשׁ [Is 33:11](https://ref.ly/Isa33:11), of futile planning, cf. Che; elsewhere of evil, mischief וְהָרָה עָמָל וְיָלַד שָׁקֶר [Psalm 7:15](https://ref.ly/Ps7:15) (subj. wicked man), cf. [Jb 15:35](https://ref.ly/Job15:35), [Is 59:4](https://ref.ly/Isa59:4), so also v:[13](https://ref.ly/Isa59:13), read הָרוֺ (Inf. abs.) cf. Di. **Pu.** *Pf.* 3 ms. הֹרָה ָ\+נּבֶר [Jb 3:3](https://ref.ly/Job3:3) *a man hath been conceived* (Böii. p. 103 BaNB 77 regard as **Qal** pass.). **Po.** *Inf. abs., conceive, contrive, devise* הֹרוֺ וְהֹגוֺ מִלֵּב דִּבְרֵי שָׁקֶר [Is 59:13](https://ref.ly/Isa59:13), but rd. rather הָרוֺ וְהָגוֺ **Qal** *Inf. abs.* v. supr. (Bal.c. expl. MT as **Qal** *Inf. pass.*).—On הוֺרַי [Gn 49:26](https://ref.ly/Gen49:26) v. הוֺר and הַּר **1 c.**

> \[Str2030] † II. הָרָה **adj. f.** pregnant —; abs. ´ה [Gn 16:11](https://ref.ly/Gen16:11) \+ 10 t.; cstr. הֲרַת [Je 20:17](https://ref.ly/Jer20:17); pl. cstr. הָרוֺת [Am 1:13](https://ref.ly/Amos1:13); sf. הָרוֺתֶיהָ [2 K 15:16](https://ref.ly/2Kgs15:16); הָרֹתֵיהֶם [2 K 8:12](https://ref.ly/2Kgs8:12); —*pregnant,* as attrib., ´אִשָּׁה ה [Ex 21:22](https://ref.ly/Exod21:22); as pred. [Gn 38:24](https://ref.ly/Gen38:24) (לִזְנוּנִים), v:[25](https://ref.ly/Gen38:25) (לְאִישׁ), [1 S 4:19](https://ref.ly/1Sam4:19), [2 S 11:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam11:5), [Is 7:14](https://ref.ly/Isa7:14) (\+ וְיֹלֶדֶת); in phr. הִנָּח הָדָה וְיֹלַדְתְּ [Gn 16:11](https://ref.ly/Gen16:11) (on resemblance to [Is 7:14](https://ref.ly/Isa7:14) cf. PetersHbr. Apr. 1885, 243; Apr. 1886, 175\), Ju [13:5](https://ref.ly/Judg13:5), [13:7](https://ref.ly/Judg13:7); \= subst. *pregnant women, women with child* [Am 1:13](https://ref.ly/Amos1:13), [2 K 8:12](https://ref.ly/2Kgs8:12), [15:16](https://ref.ly/2Kgs15:16), [Je 31:8](https://ref.ly/Jer31:8), and, in sim., [Is 26:17](https://ref.ly/Isa26:17); once of womb וְרַחְמָהּ הֲרַת עוֺלָם [Je 20:17](https://ref.ly/Jer20:17).

Di;—*conception,* in combin. מִלֵּדָה וּמִבֶּטֶן וּמֵהֵרָ\+יוֺן [Ho 9:11](https://ref.ly/Hos9:11); וַתֵּלֶד ´לָהּ ה ´וַיִּתֵּן י [Ru 4:13](https://ref.ly/Ruth4:13); עִצְּבוֺנֵח וְהֵרֹנֵח [Gn 3:16](https://ref.ly/Gen3:16) (‖תֵּלְדִי).

> \[Str2036] † הֹרָם **n.pr.m.** a Canaanitish king ruling in Gezer [Jos 10:33](https://ref.ly/Josh10:33).

> \[Str1027] הָרָם v. בֵּית הָרָם supr.

> \[Str2038] † \[הַרְמוֺן] **n.m.** mng. dub.; text perhaps corrupt; only [Am 4:3](https://ref.ly/Amos4:3) וְהִשְׁלַכְתֶּנַָה הַהַרְמ֫וֺנָה and *ye shall cast* them (your אחרית, posterity, AE; Hi RV *cast* \[yourselves], but read rather with Vrss as pass. ´וְהָשְׁל *shall be cast*) *into Harmon;* if text be sound, some locality must be meant, though the nature of the allusion is lost (AV, *into the palace,* treats הרמון improb. as \= ארמון). Targum SyrVer have *mountain* (*s*) *of Armenia* (הַר מוֺנָה), Symm *Armenia,* cf. LagGes. Abh. 172, HoffmZAW iii. 1882, 102, of exile; Hi\-St for ´ה rd. הֲדַדְרִמּוֺנָה *to Hadadrimmon,* n.pr.loc. in plain of Jezreel, so Gunning; LXX εἰς τὸ ὄρος τὸ Ῥομμαν, whence Ew ingeniously ההר רמונה and ye shall cast *Rimmonah* (name of idol, supposed fem. of Rimmon [2 K 5:18](https://ref.ly/2Kgs5:18)) *to the mountains.*

> \[Str761] הָרַמִּים v. אֲרַמִּי p. 74 supr.

> \[Str2039] † הָרָן **n.pr.m.** (*mountaineer;* i.e. mountain\-people (or land)? cf. Di [Gn 11:27](https://ref.ly/Gen11:27));—**1\.** son of Teraḥ, brother of Abram and father of Lot [Gn 11:27](https://ref.ly/Gen11:27), [11:27](https://ref.ly/Gen11:27), [11:31](https://ref.ly/Gen11:31) (all P), v:[28](https://ref.ly/Gen11:28) (J); also father of Milcah (Nahor's wife) \& Iscah v:[29](https://ref.ly/Gen11:29) (J). **2\.** a Levite (Gershonite) [1 Ch 23:9](https://ref.ly/1Chr23:9).—On הָרָן in **n.pr.loc.** v. sub בית הרם, p. 111\.

## הָרַס (id: BDB02483)

\[Str2040] † הָרַס **vb.** throw down, break or tear down (MI הרם; ? Ar. هَرَسَ, *bruise, bray, pound, crush* (grain))—**Qal** *Pf.* ´הָ [La 2:2](https://ref.ly/Lam2:2), [2:17](https://ref.ly/Lam2:17); הָרָס [Is 14:17](https://ref.ly/Isa14:17); וְהָרַסְתָּ֫ Ju [6:25](https://ref.ly/Judg6:25) וְהָרַסְתִּ֫י Ez [13:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:14), [Mi 5:10](https://ref.ly/Mic5:10); וְהָֽרְסוּ Ez [16:39](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:39), [26:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek26:4), [26:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek26:12) הָרָסוּ [1 K 19:10](https://ref.ly/1Kgs19:10), [19:14](https://ref.ly/1Kgs19:14); *Impf.* יַהֲרוֺס [Jb 12:14](https://ref.ly/Job12:14); sf. יֶהֶרְסֶָךָ [Is 22:19](https://ref.ly/Isa22:19); וַיֶּהֶרְסֶָהָ [1 Ch 20:1](https://ref.ly/1Chr20:1); יֶהֶרְסֶנָּה [Pr 29:4](https://ref.ly/Prov29:4) יֶהֶרְסֵם [Psalm 28:5](https://ref.ly/Ps28:5); 3 fs. sf. 3 ms. תֶּהֶרְסֶנּוּ [Pr 14:1](https://ref.ly/Prov14:1); 2 ms. תַּהֲרֹס [Ex 15:7](https://ref.ly/Exod15:7); אֶהֱרֹס [Je 24:6](https://ref.ly/Jer24:6), [42:10](https://ref.ly/Jer42:10) אֶהֱרוֺס [Mal 1:4](https://ref.ly/Mal1:4); 3 mpl. יֶהֶרְסוּ [Ex 19:21](https://ref.ly/Exod19:21), [19:24](https://ref.ly/Exod19:24); יַהֲרָֹסוּ [2 K 3:25](https://ref.ly/2Kgs3:25); *Imv.* הֲרָ־ס [Psalm 58:7](https://ref.ly/Ps58:7); sf. הָרְסָהּ [2 S 11:25](https://ref.ly/2Sam11:25); *Inf.* הֲרֹס [Je 31:28](https://ref.ly/Jer31:28) הֲרוֺס [Je 1:10](https://ref.ly/Jer1:10); *Pt. act.* הֹרֵס [Je 45:4](https://ref.ly/Jer45:4); *pass.* הָרוּס [1 K 18:30](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:30); —**1\.** *throw down, tear down,* c. acc.: altar(s) Ju [6:25](https://ref.ly/Judg6:25), [1 K 18:30](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:30), [19:10](https://ref.ly/1Kgs19:10), [19:14](https://ref.ly/1Kgs19:14); height (גַּב, where idol\-altar stood ? cf. גֶּב sub גבב) Ez [16:39](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:39); city (cities) [Is 14:17](https://ref.ly/Isa14:17), [2 S 11:25](https://ref.ly/2Sam11:25), [2 K 3:25](https://ref.ly/2Kgs3:25), [1 Ch 20:1](https://ref.ly/1Chr20:1) (cf. Di); pt. *destroyers,* of Zion [Is 49:17](https://ref.ly/Isa49:17) (‖מַחֲרִיבַיִח).

> \[Str2041] † הֶ֫רֶס **n.\[m.]** overthrow, destruction, only [Is 19:18](https://ref.ly/Isa19:18) one shall be called עִיר הַהֶרֶס *city of destruction,* i.e. (as usually explained) with punning allusion to On\-Heliopolis: it shall be called no longer עַיר הַחֶרֶסָ *city of the sun,* but עיר חהרסָ *city of destruction,* city whose temples, etc., of the sun have been destroyed; but perh. \< LXX πόλις ασεδεκ \= עִיר הֵצֶּדֶק *city of righteousness,* so GeiUrschrift 79 Brd Di (change intentional ? cf. id.); on other views v. id. \& De Che.

> \[Str2034] † \[הֲרִיסָה] **n.f.** ruin (concr.);—וַהֲרִסֹתָיו אָקִיס [Am 9:11](https://ref.ly/Amos9:11) *and its ruins* (i.e. of סֻכַּת דָּוִיד) *will I raise up* (‖ פִּרְחֵיהֶן).

> \[Str2035] † \[הֲרִיסוּת] **n.f.** overthrow, destruction, ruin;—אֶרֶץ הֲרִסֻתֵךְ [Is 49:19](https://ref.ly/Isa49:19) *the land of thine overthrow,* i.e. thy ruined land (‖חָרְבֹתַיִח וְשֹׁמֲמֹתַיִח).

Note partic. † הַר־מוֺעֵד [Is 14:13](https://ref.ly/Isa14:13) *mountain of meeting* or *of assembly,* i.e. the dwelling\-place of the gods, acc. to Babylonian conception, here represented as in the far north, v. esp. Che De Di, DlPa 117 ff. COTad loc. Jr57 ff. JenKosmologie 23\. *Upon the mt.* is עָל־הָהָר cf. וַתֵּבְךְּ עַל־בְּתוּלָיהָ עַל־הֶהָרִיס Ju [11:38](https://ref.ly/Judg11:38), \& the peculiar phr. וְאֵלְכָה וְיָרַרַתִּי עַ־ל הֶהָרִים v:[37](https://ref.ly/Judg11:37); *go up upon* עָלָה אָ־ל [Ex 19:23](https://ref.ly/Exod19:23), [24:13](https://ref.ly/Exod24:13), [34:4](https://ref.ly/Exod34:4) (all JE); cf. acc. in הַעַל אֹתָס הֹר הָהָר [Nu 20:25](https://ref.ly/Num20:25); *speak* with one *upon* is דבר בהר [Ex 31:18](https://ref.ly/Exod31:18), [34:32](https://ref.ly/Exod34:32), [Nu 3:1](https://ref.ly/Num3:1) (all P), [Lv 25:1](https://ref.ly/Lev25:1) (H), cf. *command, give commands, law*, etc., בהר [Lv 7:38](https://ref.ly/Lev7:38), [26:46](https://ref.ly/Lev26:46), [27:34](https://ref.ly/Lev27:34) (all P or H); other phrases with בְּ *upon* (lit. *in,* i.e. in the midst of a group of mts.) [Ex 34:3](https://ref.ly/Exod34:3) בְּכָל (JE) [25:40](https://ref.ly/Exod25:40), [26:30](https://ref.ly/Exod26:30), [27:8](https://ref.ly/Exod27:8), [Nu 28:6](https://ref.ly/Num28:6) (all P), [Is 13:4](https://ref.ly/Isa13:4); but בְּחֹרֵב [Psalm 106:19](https://ref.ly/Ps106:19) \= *at Horeb,* בְּהֹר הָהָר [Nu 33:37](https://ref.ly/Num33:37) *at Mt. Hor; go down from the mt.* is יָרַד מִן־הָהָר [Ex 32:1](https://ref.ly/Exod32:1), [32:15](https://ref.ly/Exod32:15) (both JE) [34:29](https://ref.ly/Exod34:29), [34:29](https://ref.ly/Exod34:29) (both P), cf. Ju [9:36](https://ref.ly/Judg9:36) etc. **b.** *mountain\-range* הַר־בָּשָׁן [Psalm 68:16](https://ref.ly/Ps68:16), [68:16](https://ref.ly/Ps68:16) \= הַר־אֱלֽהִים v:[16](https://ref.ly/Ps68:16); \= הַר־גַּבְנֻנִּים v:[16](https://ref.ly/Ps68:16), הָרִים גַּבְנֻנִּים v:[17](https://ref.ly/Ps68:17) (of the Jebel Hauran: v. בשׁן). **c.** *mountain,* indef., [Jb 14:18](https://ref.ly/Job14:18) (‖צוּר); usu. pl. *mountains*, in general, or *the mountains,* esp. in poet. \& the higher style; oft. fig.; הָרִים, הֶהָרִים, covered by flood [Gn 7:20](https://ref.ly/Gen7:20) cf. v:[19](https://ref.ly/Gen7:19), [8:5](https://ref.ly/Gen8:5); covered by waters \& freed therefrom by word of God (at creation) [Psalm 104:6](https://ref.ly/Ps104:6); a chief work of God (in creation) [Am 4:13](https://ref.ly/Amos4:13), [Psalm 65:7](https://ref.ly/Ps65:7), [90:2](https://ref.ly/Ps90:2), [Pr 8:25](https://ref.ly/Prov8:25) (‖גְּבָעוֺת); weighed by God [Is 40:12](https://ref.ly/Isa40:12); removed and overturned in anger of God [Jb 9:5](https://ref.ly/Job9:5) cf. [Psalm 46:3](https://ref.ly/Ps46:3), [46:4](https://ref.ly/Ps46:4); devastated by God [Is 42:15](https://ref.ly/Isa42:15) (‖גְּבָעוֺת) cf. [Je 9:9](https://ref.ly/Jer9:9); smoking at God's touch [Psalm 104:32](https://ref.ly/Ps104:32), [144:5](https://ref.ly/Ps144:5); melting at presence of ´י Ju [5:5](https://ref.ly/Judg5:5), [Mi 1:4](https://ref.ly/Mic1:4), [Psalm 97:5](https://ref.ly/Ps97:5), [Is 63:19](https://ref.ly/Isa63:19), [64:2](https://ref.ly/Isa64:2); trembling [Is 5:25](https://ref.ly/Isa5:25) cf. [Je 4:24](https://ref.ly/Jer4:24) (‖גְּבָעוֺת), [Na 1:5](https://ref.ly/Nah1:5) (‖ *id.*), Hb [3:10](https://ref.ly/Hab3:10); called to witness Yahweh's dealings with his people [Mi 6:2](https://ref.ly/Mic6:2) (‖הָאֵתָנִים מֹסְדֵי אָרֶץ) cf. v:[1](https://ref.ly/Mic6:1) (‖הַגְּבָעוֺת), entreated to cover the guilty [Ho 10:8](https://ref.ly/Hos10:8) (‖גְּבָעוֺת); addressed by ´י also Ez [6:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek6:3) (‖גְּבָעוֺת, opp. אֲפִיקִים, גֵּאָוֺת); specif., mts. of Israel summoned to hear ´י and addressed by him Ez [36:1](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:1), [36:1](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:1), [36:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:4), [36:8](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:8), [37:22](https://ref.ly/Ezek37:22), [38:8](https://ref.ly/Ezek38:8), [39:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek39:2), [39:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek39:4); summoned to praise ´י [Psalm 148:9](https://ref.ly/Ps148:9) (‖גְּבָעוֺת) cf. [Is 44:23](https://ref.ly/Isa44:23), [49:13](https://ref.ly/Isa49:13), [55:12](https://ref.ly/Isa55:12) (‖גְּבָעוֺת); leaping in praise of ´י [Psalm 114:4](https://ref.ly/Ps114:4), [114:6](https://ref.ly/Ps114:6) (both ‖גְּבָעוֺת); הַרֲרֵי־קֶדֶם [Dt 33:15](https://ref.ly/Deut33:15) (‖גִּבְעוֺת עוֺלָם); הַרֲרֵי־עַד Hb [3:6](https://ref.ly/Hab3:6) (‖ *id.*); הַרֲרֵי־עַד also [Psalm 76:5](https://ref.ly/Ps76:5) (acc. to LXX BI Che; MT has טֶרֶף for עַד), and prob. [Gn 49:26](https://ref.ly/Gen49:26) for MT הוֺרַי עַד (‖גִּבְעֹת עוֺלָם) cf. Di. **d.** *high mt.* הַר גָּבֹהַּ [Is 30:25](https://ref.ly/Isa30:25) (‖גִּבְעָה נִשָּאָה) cf. [Dt 1:7](https://ref.ly/Deut1:7), also [Je 17:26](https://ref.ly/Jer17:26) (opp. שְׁפֵלָה); [Gn 19:17](https://ref.ly/Gen19:17) (J; opp. כִּכָּר); oft. opp. גַּיְא [2 K 2:16](https://ref.ly/2Kgs2:16), Ez [31:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek31:12) (fig.) [32:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek32:5) (*id.*) [35:8](https://ref.ly/Ezek35:8) (‖גִּבְעָה), [36:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:4) (‖ *id.*), [Is 40:4](https://ref.ly/Isa40:4) (‖ *id.*), opp. בִּקְעָה [Dt 8:7](https://ref.ly/Deut8:7), [11:11](https://ref.ly/Deut11:11), [Psalm 104:8](https://ref.ly/Ps104:8); opp. מִדְבָּר [La 4:19](https://ref.ly/Lam4:19) (cf. [Jos 12:8](https://ref.ly/Josh12:8) supr.); בֵּין הָרִים [Psalm 104:10](https://ref.ly/Ps104:10) is ‖בַּנְּחָלִים; see also וְלֽא־אֱלֽהֵי עֲמָקִים ´אֱלֽהֵי הָרִים י [1 K 20:28](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:28), cf. v:[23](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:23) (opp. מִישׁוֺר); note also הַר הָעֵמֶק [Jos 13:19](https://ref.ly/Josh13:19) (P), \& גֵּיא־הָרַי, גֵּי־הָרִים [Zc 14:5](https://ref.ly/Zech14:5), [14:5](https://ref.ly/Zech14:5); further מִדְבַּר הָרִים [Psalm 75:7](https://ref.ly/Ps75:7) *mountainous desert* Vrss. \& most mod. (Baer מִדְבָּר but cf. De); *mountain\-ward* is הֶ֫רָה [Gn 14:10](https://ref.ly/Gen14:10). **f.** mts. as hiding\-places:—הַמִּנְהָרוֺת אֲוֶר בֶּהָרִים Ju [6:2](https://ref.ly/Judg6:2) *the burrows* (Stanley, VB) *which are in the mts.;* cf. [Psalm 11:1](https://ref.ly/Ps11:1) [Je 16:16](https://ref.ly/Jer16:16) (‖גִּבְעָה); הָפַךְ מִשֹּׁרֶשׁ הָרִים [Jb 28:9](https://ref.ly/Job28:9) (of mining), v. also [Dt 8:9](https://ref.ly/Deut8:9). **g.** mts. as running\-place of gazelles [1 Ch 12:8](https://ref.ly/1Chr12:8); of leopards הַרֲרֵי נְמֵרִים Ct [4:8](https://ref.ly/Song4:8); hunting\-ground for partridges [1 S 26:20](https://ref.ly/1Sam26:20); עוֺף הָרִים [Psalm 50:11](https://ref.ly/Ps50:11) cf. [11:1](https://ref.ly/Ps11:1) (fig.); wandering\-place of lost sheep (fig.) [Na 3:18](https://ref.ly/Nah3:18), [1 K 22:17](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:17), [Je 50:6](https://ref.ly/Jer50:6) cf. v:[6](https://ref.ly/Jer50:6) (‖גִּבְעָה), Ez [34:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:6) (‖כָּל־גִּבְעָה רָמָה) [2 Ch 18:16](https://ref.ly/2Chr18:16). **h.** grazing\-places for cattle בְּהֵמוֺת בְּהַרֲרֵי־אָלֶף[Psalm 50:10](https://ref.ly/Ps50:10) (rd. אֵל for אלף Ol BI Che), cf. יְתוּר הָרִים [Jb 39:8](https://ref.ly/Job39:8) of pasture of wild ass; also (si vera l.) בּוּל הָרִים [Jb 40:20](https://ref.ly/Job40:20), i.e. mts. as furnishing food for hippopot.; v. further Ct [4:6](https://ref.ly/Song4:6), [8:14](https://ref.ly/Song8:14), [Pr 27:25](https://ref.ly/Prov27:25). **i.** as place of field and vineyards [2 Ch 26:10](https://ref.ly/2Chr26:10) (opp. שְׁפֵלָה, מִישׁוֺר) [Is 7:25](https://ref.ly/Isa7:25); v. also הַמַּצְמִיחַ הָרִים חָצִיר [Psalm 147:8](https://ref.ly/Ps147:8) cf. [Am 9:13](https://ref.ly/Amos9:13); **j.** as kindled into flame (i.e. their forests; in sim.) [Psalm 83:15](https://ref.ly/Ps83:15). **k.** as scene of massacre, (fig.) [Is 34:3](https://ref.ly/Isa34:3) *melting with blood;* as place of battle array, *height* [1 S 17:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:3), [17:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:3). **1\.** as places of illicit worship [Is 65:7](https://ref.ly/Isa65:7) (‖גְּבָעוֺת) cf. [Je 3:6](https://ref.ly/Jer3:6) \& appar. v:[25](https://ref.ly/Jer3:25) (‖ *id.*) so הַר־גָּבֹהַּ וְנִשָּא [Is 57:7](https://ref.ly/Isa57:7), הֶהָרִים הָרָמִים [Dt 12:2](https://ref.ly/Deut12:2) (‖הַגְּבָעוֺת); but Ez [18:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek18:6), [18:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek18:15) rd. perh. הַדָּם for הרים cf. RSK 310 \& Ez [33:25](https://ref.ly/Ezek33:25). **m.** in various combinations צַד הָהָר [1 S 23:26](https://ref.ly/1Sam23:26), [23:26](https://ref.ly/1Sam23:26), [2 S 13:34](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:34) *side of the mt.,* צֵלַע הָהָר [16:13](https://ref.ly/2Sam16:13) *id.;* מֹץ הָרִים [Is 17:13](https://ref.ly/Isa17:13) *chaff of mts.;* זֶרֶם הָרִים [Jb 24:8](https://ref.ly/Job24:8) *mountain\-shower;* צֵל הֶהָרִים Ju [9:36](https://ref.ly/Judg9:36) *shadow of the mts.;* מִבֵּין שְׁנֵי הָרִים וְהֶהָרִים הָרֵי נְחשֶׁת [Zc 6:1](https://ref.ly/Zech6:1); *top of mt.* usu. ראֹשׁ הָהָר [Nu 14:40](https://ref.ly/Num14:40), [14:44](https://ref.ly/Num14:44), [1 S 26:13](https://ref.ly/1Sam26:13), [2 K 1:9](https://ref.ly/2Kgs1:9); as place for beacon [Is 30:17](https://ref.ly/Isa30:17) (‖גִּבְעָה), (cf. הַר־נִשְׁפֶּה [13:2](https://ref.ly/Isa13:2) \& נְשאֹ־נֵס הָרִים [Is 18:3](https://ref.ly/Isa18:3)), ראֹשׁ הֶהָרִים [Is 2:2](https://ref.ly/Isa2:2) \= [Mi 4:1](https://ref.ly/Mic4:1), ראֹשׁ הָרִים [Is 42:11](https://ref.ly/Isa42:11) (‖סֶלַע as dwelling\-place) v. also [Psalm 72:16](https://ref.ly/Ps72:16); רָאשֵׁי הֶהָרִים [Gn 8:5](https://ref.ly/Gen8:5); as lurking\-places for ambuscade Ju [9:25](https://ref.ly/Judg9:25), [9:36](https://ref.ly/Judg9:36), places for altars Ez [6:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek6:13) (‖גִּבְעָה רָמָה), for sacrifice [Ho 4:13](https://ref.ly/Hos4:13) (‖הַגְּבָעוֺת); ראֹשׁ הָהָר [Jos 15:8](https://ref.ly/Josh15:8) \= *mt.\-ridge*, cf. v:[9](https://ref.ly/Josh15:9), Ju [16:3](https://ref.ly/Judg16:3); תּוֺעֲפוֺת הָרִים [Psalm 95:4](https://ref.ly/Ps95:4); *foundations of mts.* מוֺסְדֵי הָרִים [Dt 32:22](https://ref.ly/Deut32:22), [Psalm 18:8](https://ref.ly/Ps18:8) (‖מוֺסְדוֺת הַשָּׁמַיִם [2 S 22:8](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:8)); cf. לְקִצְבֵי הָרִים יָרַדְתִּי [Jon 2:7](https://ref.ly/Jonah2:7). **n.** in fig. uses: תָּדוּשׁ הָרִים וְתָדֹק וּגְבָעוֺת כַּמֹּץ תָּשִים [Is 41:15](https://ref.ly/Isa41:15) fig. of Isr.'s overcoming its foes; יִתְנַגְּפוּ רַגְלֵיכֶם עַל־הָרֵי ָ\+נשֶׁף [Je 13:16](https://ref.ly/Jer13:16) of encountering hopeless calamities; הֶהָרִים [Is 54:10](https://ref.ly/Isa54:10) as less permanent \& changeless than Yahweh's kindness (‖גְּבָעוֺת); הַר הַמַּשְׁחִית [Je 51:25](https://ref.ly/Jer51:25) \& הַר שְרֵפָה v:[25](https://ref.ly/Jer51:25) fig. of Babylon. **2\.** *hill\-country, mountain\-region,* **a.** opp. plain, etc.:—dwelling\-place of Canaanites, הָהָר [Nu 13:17](https://ref.ly/Num13:17), [13:29](https://ref.ly/Num13:29) (of promised land; opp. אֶרֶץ הַנֶּגֶב, הַיָּם \& יַד־הַיַּרְדֵּן); בָּֽעֲרָבָה בָּהָר וּבַשְּׁפֵלָה וּבַנֶּגֶב וּבְחוֺף הַיָּם [Dt 1:7](https://ref.ly/Deut1:7); בָּהָר וּבָֽעֲרָבָה נֶגֶב כִּנְַּרוֺת וּבַשְּׁפֵלָה וּבְנָפוֺת דּוֺר מִיָּם [Jos 11:2](https://ref.ly/Josh11:2); בָּהָר וּבַשְּׁפֵלָה וּבָֽעֲרָבָה וּבָֽאֲשֵׁדוֺת וּבַמִּדְבָּר וּבַנֶּגֶב [12:8](https://ref.ly/Josh12:8); cf. [9:1](https://ref.ly/Josh9:1), [10:40](https://ref.ly/Josh10:40), Ju [1:9](https://ref.ly/Judg1:9); הָהָר indef. (*the hill\-country, the mountains*, in gen.) [2 Ch 2:1](https://ref.ly/2Chr2:1) (place for hewing wood) so v:[17](https://ref.ly/2Chr2:17); [Ne 8:15](https://ref.ly/Neh8:15) (place for cutting boughs), etc. **b.** of a particular mountain\-region, hill\-country: הַר הָאֱמֹרִי [Dt 1:7](https://ref.ly/Deut1:7) (cf. v:[7](https://ref.ly/Deut1:7)) v:[19](https://ref.ly/Deut1:19) (cf. v:[24](https://ref.ly/Deut1:24)), also [Jos 10:6](https://ref.ly/Josh10:6); of Amalekites Ju [12:15](https://ref.ly/Judg12:15); of Ephraim [Jos 17:15](https://ref.ly/Josh17:15), [19:50](https://ref.ly/Josh19:50), [20:7](https://ref.ly/Josh20:7), [21:21](https://ref.ly/Josh21:21), [24:30](https://ref.ly/Josh24:30), [24:33](https://ref.ly/Josh24:33), Ju [3:27](https://ref.ly/Judg3:27), [4:5](https://ref.ly/Judg4:5), [7:24](https://ref.ly/Judg7:24), [9:48](https://ref.ly/Judg9:48), [17:1](https://ref.ly/Judg17:1) \+ 6 t. Ju 17, [1 S 1:1](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:1), [9:4](https://ref.ly/1Sam9:4), [14:22](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:22), [2 S 20:21](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:21), [1 K 4:8](https://ref.ly/1Kgs4:8), [12:25](https://ref.ly/1Kgs12:25), [2 K 5:22](https://ref.ly/2Kgs5:22), [1 Ch 6:52](https://ref.ly/1Chr6:52), [2 Ch 13:4](https://ref.ly/2Chr13:4), [15:8](https://ref.ly/2Chr15:8), [19:4](https://ref.ly/2Chr19:4), [Je 4:15](https://ref.ly/Jer4:15), [31:6](https://ref.ly/Jer31:6); Ephraim and Gilead [50:19](https://ref.ly/Jer50:19) (cf. also הר(ה) [Jos 17:16](https://ref.ly/Josh17:16), [17:18](https://ref.ly/Josh17:18), Ju [3:27](https://ref.ly/Judg3:27)); of Israel [Jos 11:16](https://ref.ly/Josh11:16), [11:21](https://ref.ly/Josh11:21), cf. הָרֵי יִשְרָאֵל Ez [6:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek6:2), [19:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek19:9), [33:28](https://ref.ly/Ezek33:28), [34:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:13), [35:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek35:12); הָרֵי מְרוֺם יִשְרָאֵל Ez [17:23](https://ref.ly/Ezek17:23), [20:40](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:40) (‖הַר־קָדְשִׁי) [34:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:14); עָרֵי הָהָר i.e. in mountain\-country [Dt 2:37](https://ref.ly/Deut2:37) (of Ammonites), [Je 32:44](https://ref.ly/Jer32:44), [33:13](https://ref.ly/Jer33:13); hill\-country of Judah [Jos 11:21](https://ref.ly/Josh11:21), [15:48](https://ref.ly/Josh15:48), [18:12](https://ref.ly/Josh18:12), [20:7](https://ref.ly/Josh20:7), [21:11](https://ref.ly/Josh21:11), [2 Ch 21:11](https://ref.ly/2Chr21:11) (הָרֵי יהודה) [27:4](https://ref.ly/2Chr27:4); abode of Jebusites [Jos 11:3](https://ref.ly/Josh11:3).

> \[Str2022] הַרֲרִי, הֲרָרִי, הַרֲרָם, הַרֲרִי, הֲרָרֶיהָ v. הר. p. 249

> \[Str2043] † הֲרָרִי, הָרָרִי **adj. gent.** (\= *mountain\-dweller* (Thes) ? or deriv. fr. some unknown **n.pr.loc.**);—used only in defining certain of David's heroes: **1\. a.** שַׁמָּה הַהֲרָרִי [2 S 23:33](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:33) a (cf. Dr) \= [1 Ch 11:34](https://ref.ly/1Chr11:34) b (where rd. prob. שַׁמָּה for שָׁגֵה, LXXL Σαμαια). **b.** שַׁמָּה בֶּן־אָגֵא הָרָרִי [2 S 23:11](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:11) (rd. perh. הַהֲרָרִי cf. Dr; but LXX ὁ Ἁρουχαῖος, LXXL ὁ Αραχι, i.e. הָאַרְכִּי ? Klo). On שַׁמּוֺת הַהֲרוֺרִי [1 Ch 11:27](https://ref.ly/1Chr11:27) (LXX ὁ Ἁδι, LXXL ὁ Αρωρι) v. הֲרוֺרִי supr. p. 248\. **2\.** אֲחִיאָם בֶּן־שָׁרָר הָארָרִי [2 S 23:33](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:33) b (rd. prob. הַהֲרָרִי) \= אֲחִיאָם בֶּן־שָכָר הַהֲרָרִי [1 Ch 11:35](https://ref.ly/1Chr11:35).

> \[Str2044] † הָשֵׁם **n.pr.m.** father of one (or more ?) of David's heroes [1 Ch 11:34](https://ref.ly/1Chr11:34), but rd. prob. יָשֵׁן [2 S 23:32](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:32) and del. בני; cf. DrSm and v. further sub יָשֵׁן.

> \[Str2045] הַשְׁמָעוּת v. sub שׁמע. p. 1036

> \[Str827,8199] הָשְׁפוֺת v. אַשְׁפֹּת sub שׁפת. p. 1047

> \[Str2046] הִתּוּךְ v. sub נתך. p. 678

> \[Str2266] הִתְחַבְּרוּת v. sub חבר. p. 288

> \[Str2047] † הֲתָךְ **n.pr.m.** a Persian official at court of Ahasuerus [Est 4:5](https://ref.ly/Esth4:5), [4:6](https://ref.ly/Esth4:6), [4:9](https://ref.ly/Esth4:9), [4:10](https://ref.ly/Esth4:10).

## הָתַל (id: BDB02497)

\[Str2048] † \[הָתַל] **vb.**—only **Pi.** deceive, mock, *Impf.* וַיְהַתֵּל בָּהֶם [1 K 18:27](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:27) (secondary formation fr. תלל Hiph., q.v.; cf. Thes Ol:§ 257 f. ad fin. Sta§ 145 e; otherwise Köi. 352; v. further sub תלל).

> \[Str2049] † הֲתֻלִַים **n.\[m.]pl.** mockery;—´אִם־לאֹ ה עִמָּדִי [Jb 17:2](https://ref.ly/Job17:2) *truly mockery surroundeth me* (De Di Da cf. VB).

* **Associated Passages:** Job 17:2; 1 Kings 18:27


## התת (id: BDB02499)

\[Str2050] התת √ of תְּהוֺתֲתוּ [Psalm 62:4](https://ref.ly/Ps62:4) acc. to Thes al., but v. הות

251:b. Bef. התת ins. ‘† מַהֲתַלּוֺת **n.f.pl.** deceptions;—[Is 30:10](https://ref.ly/Isa30:10).’

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 62:4
* **Associated Articles:** הוּת (ID: `BDB02375@BDBHebrewLexicon`)
